Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 微调后的GLM检索器在域外泛化方面表现最佳，在两个多跳基准上达到SOTA；(2) 在域内多跳QA数据集上与现有工作性能相当，且随着参数量和子图覆盖率的增加展现出良好的扩展性；(3) GNN-based检索器在高效训练设置下实现了更高的图覆盖率；(4) 向量检索基线在单跳数据集上表现最优。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同检索器范式在RAG任务中的互补优势：GLM检索器在跨域泛化和多跳推理上具有显著优势，是构建可迁移知识图谱RAG系统的有前景方向；GNN检索器适合高效训练的图覆盖场景；而向量检索仍是单跳任务的有效选择。未来的工作可进一步探索如何结合这些方法的优势。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs requires retrievers that can effectively capture both graph structure and semantic information. Recent approaches have explored graph neural network (GNN)-based retrievers to model graph topology in multi-hop reasoning tasks. In parallel, graph language models (GLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm that integrates graph reasoning and the semantic capabilities of language models. In this work, we introduce a GLM-based retriever and investigate the comparative strengths of GLM-based, GNN-based, and traditional vector-search-based retrievers in single- and multi-hop RAG settings, and with a particular focus on transferability to unseen domains. Our findings suggest that finetuned GLM retrievers generalize better out of domain, achieving SOTA on two multi-hop benchmarks. On in-domain multi-hop QA datasets they remain comparable to prior work, with promising scaling as parameters and subgraph coverage increase. GNN-based retrievers achieve higher graph coverage with an efficient training setup, whereas the vector-search baseline excels at single-hop datasets.

</details>


### [104] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou,Jinfan Lv,Ruixuan Li,Longtai Zhang,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang,Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuantWAMs是一个针对世界动作模型（WAMs）的后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过共享基座异常值校准、协同训练目标显著性分析和固定干预滚动评估三种策略，将量化决策与模型结构、部署分布和任务目标对齐，在W4A4设置下将模拟性能差异控制在FP16的0.2-0.7个百分点内，并将峰值权重和激活内存降至FP16的约29%，同时实现1.4-1.6倍的块级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化方法不适用于世界动作模型（WAMs），主要因为三个方面：其一，现有PTQ依赖开环目标，无法反映WAMs的闭环迭代去噪执行方式；其二，现有方法采用同质模型假设，未能考虑WAMs中视频与动作模块的异构结构；其三，校准分布与真实部署场景不匹配，导致量化决策偏离实际需求。

Method: QuantWAMs提出三种核心策略：(1) 共享基座异常值校准（shared-basis outlier calibration），仅在坐标兼容的模块间池化激活证据以进行异常值校准；(2) 协同训练目标显著性分析（co-training-objective saliency），基于联合视频-动作梯度计算经验Fisher分数，在校准稳定的层粒度上分配权重精度；(3) 固定干预滚动评估（fixed-intervention rollout auditing），利用可达闭环状态修订去噪步数保护计划，且不改变精度预算。

Result: 在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上，跨RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及AgiBot G2真实机器人操作任务进行评估。在W4A4主导设置下，模拟结果平均值与FP16相差仅0.2-0.7个百分点；真实机器人实验在三个操作任务上验证了部署可行性；针对视频和动作块，峰值权重与激活内存降至FP16的约29%，并提供1.4-1.6倍的块级加速。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs通过将量化决策与部署校准上下文（模型结构、滚动分布和任务目标）对齐，实现了WAMs的高效低比特量化部署，在保持接近FP16精度的同时显著降低内存开销并提升推理速度，验证了在模拟和真实机器人场景中的可行性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>


### [105] [WIDE: Boosting Adaptive LLM Inference via Token-level Dynamic Width Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28418)
*Haozhe Hu,Hao Wu,Peiran Yin,Chao Han,Yunpu Ma,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WIDE，首个端到端可微的token级动态宽度剪枝框架，支持从层级到神经元块级别的细粒度动态剪枝，并配合剪枝-内核协同设计实现实际推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态结构化剪枝虽然硬件友好，但在高稀疏度下精度损失严重；而现有动态剪枝方法多局限于粗粒度的结构化决策，实际推理场景中的加速效果有限。需要一种兼顾细粒度动态计算分配与实际推理加速的方法。

Method: WIDE允许每个token动态选择注意力头组和FFN通道组，实现神经元块级别的动态剪枝粒度；采用两阶段训练管线学习token-wise稀疏执行模式；提出剪枝-内核协同设计框架，将动态稀疏加速分解为掩码重排序、硬件无关的块级跳过和硬件相关的块内跳过三层，以支持不同粒度的高效执行。

Result: 在50%稀疏度下，WIDE相比最先进的动态深度剪枝方法在仅校准设置下获得55.1%的性能提升；在prefill和decoding推理负载下，分别实现最高1.98x和4.95x的接近理论极限的kernel级加速，以及1.68x和1.55x的端到端加速。

Conclusion: WIDE验证了token级细粒度动态宽度剪枝在保证精度的同时可以实现实际可部署的推理加速，为动态剪枝的实用化提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Pruning is a promising approach for improving the efficiency of LLMs. Existing static structured pruning methods are hardware-friendly and can deliver practical throughput gains, but their input-agnostic computation allocation often causes substantial accuracy degradation under aggressive sparsity. Recent dynamic sparsity methods improve quality retention by adapting computation to individual inputs, yet they remain largely limited to coarse-grained structural decisions and their practical acceleration under real-world inference scenarios remains challenging. To address these challenges, we present WIDE, the first end-to-end differentiable token-level dynamic width pruning framework designed for both prefill and decode scenarios. WIDE enables fine-grained computation allocation by allowing each token to dynamically select attention-head groups and FFN-channel groups, extending dynamic pruning beyond layer-level decisions to neuron-block-level granularity. Through a two-stage training pipeline, WIDE learns effective token-wise sparse execution patterns and achieves substantially better quality retention than existing approaches. To make such fine-grained dynamic pruning practical, we further propose a pruning--kernel co-design framework that decomposes dynamic sparsity acceleration into mask reordering, hardware-agnostic block-level skipping, and hardware-dependent intra-block skipping, enabling efficient execution across different granularities. At 50% sparsity, WIDE provides 55.1% performance boost when compared to the state-of-the-art dynamic depth pruning under calibration-only settings. Under prefill and decoding inference workloads, WIDE achieves close-to-theoretical kernel-level speedups of up to 1.98x for prefill and 4.95x for decoding, as well as 1.68x and 1.55x end-to-end acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning/tree/main/WIDE.

</details>


### [106] [When Derived Measurements Mislead: Quantifying and Mitigating LLM Over-Trust with Privileged-Modality Reliability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28421)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Tianli Li,Mingzhe Cui,Yang Jiao,Lei Xie,Yi Pan,Xiao Hu,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了一个新的评估框架——派生特征过度信任（DFOT），用于量化大语言模型（LLM）在流水线中错误地将派生测量数据当作直接事实使用的失败模式。以生理传感为案例，通过PPG派生心律与离线ECG对照的实验设计，提出了五个量化估计量（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR），并在50,000条配对PPG-ECG记录上验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型流水线中越来越多地将派生测量值（如从PPG信号推导的心律）当作直接事实使用，但这些测量的有效性取决于具体实例（instance-dependent），导致下游LLM可能过度信任这些派生特征，在其有效范围之外使用它们，造成错误。现有评估方法缺乏对这种"派生特征过度信任"失败模式的统一量化框架。

Method: 1) 提出DFOT（派生特征过度信任）的概念和五个量化估计量：冲突过度信任率（COTR）、上下文诱导错误率（CIR）、正确修复率（CRR）、证据特异性修复边际（ESRM）和效用危害率（UHR）。2) 设计两个测试：D1测试衍生特征与离线金标准冲突情况下的拒绝能力，D2测试在误导性病史下拒绝可靠派生特征的能力。3) 使用ECG提供训练监督和离线参考但不对LLM可见，以ECG到PPG的特权蒸馏作为基线模型，以及PPG-only推理。4) 在50,000条配对PPG-ECG记录上进行验证。

Result: 在协议锁定的187名患者测试集上，基线方法（ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏）在四个修复和特异性端点上的性能提升了1.82至6.69个百分点，所有配对置信区间均排除零；效用危害率（UHR）增加了0.67个百分点（95% CI: -0.4至+1.7）。该框架不依赖于特定的可靠性生成器，提供了统一的评估目标。

Conclusion: DFOT提供了一个通用的评估框架，可量化LLM流水线中派生特征过度信任的失败模式，并为更强大的缓解方法提供统一的评估基准。未来可通过更强的缓解技术来降低DFOT相关的错误。

Abstract: Derived measurements increasingly enter large language model (LLM) pipelines as direct facts despite their instance-dependent validity. We define derived-feature over-trust (DFOT) as the failure in which a downstream LLM assigns such a measurement the epistemic status of a direct fact or uses it outside its valid scope. Using physiological sensing as a case study, D1 tests acceptance of a PPG-derived rhythm contradicted by offline ECG, whereas D2 tests rejection of an offline-confirmed reliable PPG rhythm under misleading severe history. ECG supplies training supervision and offline reference construction but is never shown to the LLM. Five estimands quantify this chain: conflict over-trust rate (COTR) and context-induced error rate (CIR) characterize D1/D2; correct repair rate (CRR) measures frozen-error repair; evidence-specific repair margin (ESRM) contrasts matched and patient-disjoint shuffled evidence; and utility harm rate (UHR) measures unnecessary verification among HIGH-reliability cases used without verification at baseline. The framework does not depend on a particular reliability generator. We demonstrate it on 50,000 paired PPG-ECG records using ECG-to-PPG privileged distillation as an illustrative baseline and PPG-only inference. On a protocol-locked 187-patient test, the baseline improves four repair and specificity endpoints by 1.82-6.69 percentage points, with all paired confidence intervals excluding zero; UHR increases by 0.67 percentage points (95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7). DFOT provides a common evaluation target for stronger mitigation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/Zongheng-Guo/When-Derived-Measurements-Mislead.

</details>


### [107] [A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28432)
*Chenghan Wu,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNICON（UNified In-Context Operator Networks），一个跨学科的数值智能基础模型。它通过从图结构示例中推断系统间的预测关系，并将其应用于同一系统的查询，无需微调即可在多个科学和社会系统中接近专家水平，甚至在某些训练中未见过的学科中超越最先进的专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能通常被理解为获取和应用知识、适应陌生情境及解决新问题的能力。大语言模型通过在文本上下文中推断任务相关知识来展现这种能力，但智能不应局限于语言领域。对于科学和社会系统，我们需要能够从数值上下文中获取和应用知识的模型，即所谓的"数值智能"。现有模型在此方面存在空白，因此需要一种能在数值领域展现类比学习能力的基础模型。

Method: UNICON采用统一上下文算子网络架构，以系统中的图结构示例作为上下文，推断这些示例之间共享的预测关系，并将该关系应用于同一系统的查询。该模型通过跨学科训练集进行训练，并可与语言模型代理结合以进一步提升性能。

Result: UNICON在多个科学和社会系统中接近专家级性能，且无需重新训练即可推广到训练中未见过的学科。与语言模型代理结合后，UNICON在训练中未见过的学科上超越了最先进的专家模型。训练语料库的多样性能够提升对未见学科的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UNICON被确立为数值智能的基础模型，展示了从纯数值上下文中进行类比推理和知识迁移的能力，可作为更广泛人工智能生态系统的基础构建模块。

Abstract: Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [108] [Metaphor Tracer: A Theory-Informed Analysis of Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28434)
*Marc Heimann,Roxana Assadi Moghaddam,Olga Brovkina,Mark Pettifor,Lutz Goetzmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种隐状态指标——"聚合器"(aggregator)和"分化器"(differentiator)，无需训练即可从单次前向传播中对文本中每个token位置进行评分。聚合器衡量该位置是否将整个文本整合为稳定配置；分化器则衡量其他token是否被瞬时携入其子空间。实验跨三个不相关模型验证：聚合器不受重复、surprisal和注意力衰减影响，标记的是token在文本中的"位置"而非显著性。以工程师设计的语域(6/6)和精神分析师标注的临床转录(34/36)作为独立真值验证，并通过基础/指令微调对及迁移测试揭示token结构与阅读保真度的关系，论证隐状态的结构价值是关系性的而非本质主义的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型隐状态是否能揭示单一文本的组织结构。作者希望从单次前向传播中，无需训练即可识别token位置在文本中的两种功能角色：作为全局整合的"聚合器"和作为信息运输的"分化器"，从而将隐状态解读从本质主义转向关系主义。

Method: 定义两种隐状态评分指标：聚合器(aggregator)衡量某位置将整个文本固化为稳定配置的程度；分化器(differentiator)衡量其他token是否被瞬时携带进入其子空间。所有常数在单篇发现文本上固定，其余文本均为确认性测试。跨三个不相关模型验证，并以工程师设计的语域结构(6/6)和精神分析师预先标注的临床转录(34/36)作为独立真值进行验证，同时设计匹配的base/instruct模型对进行迁移测试。

Result: 聚合器并非经典信息度量，也非显著性度量。跨三个模型，当符号重复时其surprisal和注意力下降，但聚合器分数保持稳定。聚合器在独立真值上表现优异：6/6语域边界单元和34/36临床转录标记单元，且高于词汇控制并呈梯度递增，表现出类型级(type-level)度量无法复现的分离模式。迁移测试显示：token结构随词类型漂移的模型对单篇话语阅读最差；匹配的base/instruct对中，指令微调提升保真度但不改变类型迁移。

Conclusion: 结构价值是token在"这一篇"文本中的位置属性，而非其向量本身的属性。作者论证了对隐状态的关系性(relational)而非本质主义(essentialist)解读，并将此工具操作化为一种早于该仪器存在的理论的实证架构。

Abstract: What do a language model's hidden states say about the organization of a single text? From one forward pass, without training, we score every token position on two properties. The *aggregator* measures whether the position consolidates the whole text into a stable configuration. The *differentiator*, whether other tokens are transiently carried into its subspace as the model reads: metaphor in its root sense, transport. Constants were frozen on one discovery text; every other is confirmatory.
  The aggregator is not, in the classic sense, an information measure, nor a measure of salience. Across three unrelated models, as a signifier repeats, its surprisal and its attention drain while its aggregator score holds: the channel marks a token's place in the text. That this tracks a reading rests on independent ground truth: an engineered register the aggregator follows across its boundaries (6/6 cells), and a psychoanalyst's marking of clinical transcripts, fixed before the instrument existed, in 34/36 cells, with a graded increment above lexical controls and dissociations no type-level measure reproduces. A transfer test gives the result its shape: the model whose token structure travels with lexical type reads the singular discourse worst, and in a matched base/instruct pair tuning raises fidelity without moving type-transfer. Structural value is a property of a token's place in *this* text, not of its vector alone: a relational rather than essentialist reading of hidden states, operationalizing theory that predated the instrument.

</details>


### [109] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR)，一种无外部反馈的多轮强化学习框架，将自验证作为计算控制策略，实现自适应测试时计算分配。在七个数学推理基准上，SVR以平均2.99轮推理达到0.563的宏平均准确率，超越标准GRPO和多轮基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀分配测试时计算预算会浪费算力于简单输入，而依赖验证器的引导式细化方法需要外部反馈。现有方法缺乏利用模型自身验证信号来控制计算分配的无外部反馈机制。

Method: SVR是一种无oracle的多轮强化学习框架，每轮模型同时输出解决方案、离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为Correct且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则利用自验证信号继续细化。真实标签仅用于构建训练奖励，不外泄到提示或推理阶段。使用GRPO在固定长度轨迹上训练，奖励涵盖解正确性、校准感知的自验证以及停止就绪正确状态，自适应停止仅在推理时激活。

Result: 在七个数学推理基准上使用Qwen3.5-2B，SVR达到0.563的宏平均准确率，仅需平均2.99轮推理。在完整系统比较中超越标准GRPO、强多轮基线及固定的oracle引导分数反馈参考方法，同时推理轮数远少于固定十轮推理。

Conclusion: 学习到的自验证信号可以作为有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留和自适应测试时计算分配，无需外部验证器反馈。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [110] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Lean定理证明器中引入了一个框架，同时解决约束编程的两个验证层级：一是参数化证明问题族层面的重构等价性、等价可满足性及对称性破缺约束的正确性；二是通过翻译后端（MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB）核验外部求解器生成的证书，实现无需信任外部求解器的端到端可满足性/不可满足性验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束编程是解决调度、规划、配置和验证等复杂组合问题的核心技术，但其结果的可信度面临双重挑战：前置重构（reformulation）是否语义保持，以及求解器是否正确。现有工作缺乏同时覆盖这两个验证层级的统一框架。

Method: 在Lean定理证明器中构建一个双层验证框架：(1) 公式级验证——参数化地为整个问题族证明等价性、等价可满足性以及对称性破缺约束的正确性；(2) 实例级验证——通过翻译后端将约束问题转换为MiniZinc、SMT-LIB和OPB等外部格式，核验求解器产生的证书。两层结合形成端到端工作流。

Result: 实验表明，框架的验证对称性破缺在实践中效果显著：每个问题族只需一次参数化证明，可跨所有实例规模复用，将求解器搜索开销最多降低2x10^7倍；在Lean内的认证过程开销可控，最大实例也仅需几分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架首次在定理证明器内统一了约束编程的两个验证层级，实现了不信任外部求解器的端到端可满足性/不可满足性证明，同时验证对称性破缺在实践中有巨大的性能收益。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [111] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoCLIP，一个结肠镜检查视觉-语言基础模型，利用从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中恢复的125,756个病灶级图像-文本对进行训练，在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中均优于通用和生物医学视觉-语言编码器，其中线性探针在良恶性分类上接近12名内镜医生的诊断水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管常规结肠镜报告中包含丰富的专家描述（如病灶外观、大小和位置），但这些报告总结的是整个检查过程而非逐帧标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间仅存在弱关联，视觉-语言模型在结肠镜领域一直未被充分利用。

Method: 从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中逐步恢复出125,756个病灶级图像-文本对，基于此训练EndoCLIP这一结肠镜专用视觉-语言基础模型，并分别在零样本和线性探针设置下评估其性能。

Result: EndoCLIP在病灶级图像-文本检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中均优于通用和生物医学视觉-语言编码器；在良恶性分类任务上，其线性探针性能接近一项涉及12名内镜医师的盲法研究中的专家水平。

Conclusion: 研究发现，恢复发现与图像之间的对应关系能将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标可以通过语言描述直接指定，而无需为每个任务单独标注，展示了语言驱动范式在临床视觉-语言建模中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [112] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种模块化的模糊规则神经符号框架，用于自动评估下水道管道严重程度。框架将Swin Transformer神经感知模块（预测14个多标签检测CODE等级）与决策树（J48）派生的19条IF-THEN模糊逻辑规则解耦，从而实现可追溯的推理过程。在3,244张图像、五个高度不平衡严重度类别的数据集上，该方法在准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC上分别提升了17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%，超越纯图像分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动下水道管道严重程度评估依赖直接的图像分类，形成"黑箱"模式，无法明确联系视觉缺陷与最终严重度评分。该研究旨在弥合神经网络感知与符号推理之间的鸿沟，使决策过程透明化、可追溯。

Method: 框架由两个解耦模块组成：(1) 感知模块：采用Swin Transformer直接从图像预测14个多标签检测CODE等级；(2) 推理模块：用J48决策树对真实CODE和严重度标签训练，将树路径转换为19条固定IF-THEN规则。推理时采用模糊逻辑，通过t-norm激活CODE条件、按规则置信度加权并结合s-norm生成可解释的类别证据。评估了Product、Łukasiewicz和Hamacher三种算子对。真实标签通过五个独立大型语言模型对原始检查员笔记达成共识生成。

Result: 在包含3,244张图像、五个严重不平衡严重度类别的数据集上，相比纯图像分类方法，准确率提升17.9%，平衡准确率提升12.2%，Macro F1提升23.0%，MCC提升17.3%。框架在获得具有竞争力的类别平衡性能的同时，实现了从预测CODE等级到规则支持、再到严重度证据的可追溯推理。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架有效结合了深度学习的感知能力与符号规则的推理优势，在保持竞争性能的同时提供了可解释的决策路径。其模块化设计允许感知和推理模块独立优化，为管道严重度评估领域提供了一个透明、可审计的解决方案框架。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [113] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过统一的复合策略模型将供应链中相互耦合的补货计划决策（品类选择、供应商分配、补货频率、配送路线）端到端联合优化，在两家大型生鲜零售供应链的真实数据上验证了其优于分阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链补货计划的各类决策（品类、供应商、补货频率、路由）在运营上高度耦合，但现实中往往由不同部门使用独立系统分别优化，导致缺货、库存积压和不必要的运输成本。现有方法缺乏对这些跨部门运营耦合的统一建模和端到端协调。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination）复合策略模型：将供应链实体表示为token，通过共享的运营表征进行上下文化，并将每种token类型映射到对应的决策接口。每个决策基于前序决策形成的部分计划，最终通过共享的系统级效用函数评估完整计划。

Result: 在叮咚买菜和京东两个不同补货层级的大规模供应链真实运营数据上验证，SCOPE在两类场景中均一致优于分阶段优化各决策环节的方法以及运营实践中常用的基线方法。

Conclusion: 学习并协调跨部门的运营耦合能够带来更有效的端到端供应链决策，验证了用统一策略模型替代孤立决策模块的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [114] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了InfoOpsBench，一个持续更新的AI基准测试，用于评估前沿语言模型是否会被国家支持的信息操作所利用。基于2,100多个来自俄罗斯、中国和伊朗的国家支持信息资产，测试了8家提供商的17个模型，发现大多数模型可以被协助进行信息操作，完整性得分从8.8%到94.5%不等，且模型选择会显著影响操作的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型可能被国家支持的信息操作所利用（即被"征用"），而现有静态基准无法适应快速演变的信息操作策略。需要一个动态、持续更新的基准来评估模型抵抗信息操作的完整性，帮助理解模型滥用风险。

Method: 从实时监控管道中提取2,100多个来自俄罗斯、中国和伊朗的国家支持信息资产，构建动态基准。在四种提示框架下测试8家供应商的17个模型，通过完整性得分（拒绝请求的百分比）评估模型抵抗信息操作的能力，并分析模型在事实核查率和生成内容特征上的差异。

Result: 大多数模型可被征用于信息操作，完整性得分从8.8%到94.5%，差距达85.7个百分点且与模型大小无关。模型选择改变操作性质：部分模型编造细节产生比原始材料更有害的输出，部分模型在合规的同时化解主张，事实核查率在2.9%到72.9%之间。除Z.ai的GLM 5.2外，中国开发的模型在事实依据充分但批评中国的主张上合规度骤降48-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 模型对信息操作的完整性与对良性主张的拒绝倾向部分相关，反映了在模型可用性与安全性之间平衡的挑战。动态基准设计使基准具有抗饱和性。中国开发模型在涉华批评主张上的合规度骤降揭示了一个值得关注的规律。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [115] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度受限信念更新（selective credibility-limited belief update），将认知输入相对于每个源世界转换为更弱的代理后再进行可信度受限转移，并给出语义与公理化刻画，识别出两个良好子类，证明其统一并拓展了Katsuno-Mendelzon更新与可信度受限信念更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可信度受限信念更新方法将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表示复合认知输入中仅有部分可被实现的情形。这限制了信念更新框架的表达能力。

Method: 引入选择性可信度受限信念更新：在每个源世界下，先将认知输入转换为较弱的代理（proxy），再执行可信度受限的状态转移。提供语义（可能世界模型）与公理化刻画；进一步定义两个子类——一致性保持更新算子（要求转换后的输入在原始输入一致时总是可信的）和极大一致性保持更新算子（额外要求代理在可信后果中信息量最大）。

Result: 给出了该算子类的语义与公理化特征；识别了两个良好子类；证明了可信度受限信念更新是该框架的特例，而Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新在移除可信度限制且变换函数为恒等映射时同样作为特例被恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架作为信念更新的统一且更具表达力的模型，涵盖既有方法并支持源依赖的选择性接受，为认知输入的部分实现提供了形式化基础。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [116] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA框架，使多智能体系统的通信拓扑在推理时能够自我演化。MANTA在执行前利用先验结构经验初始化任务条件化拓扑，部署时监测协作轨迹并在当前组织不足时进行有界结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链路、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径。在五个基准上与基线相比取得了最高平均分74.0，超过最强基线5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统虽能通过任务分解、智能体专业化、信息交换和中间验证改善复杂问题求解，但通信拓扑通常被视为固定设计选择或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理时自适应的能力，限制了协作架构的灵活性。

Method: MANTA框架包含两个阶段：(1)执行前，基于先验结构经验初始化任务条件化拓扑；(2)部署时，监测协作轨迹，在检测到当前组织效率不足时施加有界结构更新，可调整智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在五个基准（信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行、数学推理）上与单智能体和多智能体基线对比，MANTA取得最高平均得分74.0，比最强基线高5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时自我改进可扩展至协作架构本身，通信拓扑的在线自适应演化是提升多智能体系统性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [117] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了本地计算机使用代理（CUA）在严格硬件约束下的推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）效果，发现额外的计算往往带来收益递减并改变失败模式，最终提出高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、故障感知控制机制以及围绕本地模型能力设计的代理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署计算机使用代理对隐私、成本效率和实用性日益重要，但严格硬件约束下提升其性能仍具挑战。虽然推理时扩展已被证明能改进前沿CUA，但其对资源受限的本地模型的有效性尚不清楚。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B三个本地模型进行了系统的实证研究，从上下文（contextual）、时间（temporal）、结构（structural）和并行（parallel）四个维度考察推理时扩展的效果。

Result: （1）上下文扩展提供了历史基础，改善轨迹稳定性和任务准确率，但收益随token成本增加而饱和，失败模式从重复/停滞轨迹转向过早的虚假成功；（2）时间扩展减少最大步数停滞，但未实质性提升任务成功率，更长的视野往往延长错误轨迹而非纠正它们；（3）结构分解在本地两阶段代理中引入规划和格式化开销；（4）并行扩展只能部分缓解这些失败且计算成本高昂。

Conclusion: 推理时扩展对本地CUA的收益有限且存在维度差异，高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、故障感知控制机制，以及围绕本地模型能力与局限设计的代理框架。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [118] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualG-MRAG，一种双层级框架，通过解耦的宏推理图（全局结构路由）和微匹配图（局部证据验证）来解决多模态检索增强生成中多跳推理任务面临的图膨胀与检索噪声困境，并利用GNN检索器实现查询驱动的消息传递以及动态规划解码机制提取显式推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG在多跳推理任务上表现不佳。现有方法主要依赖独立实例级匹配，无法捕获跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；虽然图增强方法引入结构化建模，但在多模态场景下，细粒度视觉特征会导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，而粗粒度表示会丢失关键局部证据，存在粒度困境。

Method: DualG-MRAG采用双层级解耦架构：(1)构建宏图（Macro Graph）用于全局拓扑路由，微图（Micro Graph）用于精确的局部证据验证，隔离全局结构推理与细粒度匹配以抑制噪声；(2)将检索形式化为查询驱动的消息传递过程，通过GNN检索器实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播；(3)引入动态规划解码机制，直接从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代标准输入中孤立文档块作为生成模型的结构化引导。

Result: 大量实验表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂QA准确率上均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将全局结构推理与局部证据匹配解耦到两个层级图中，并配合GNN驱动的动态检索与推理路径解码，DualG-MRAG有效解决了多模态多跳RAG中的图膨胀和检索噪声问题，为复杂多模态推理提供了更可靠的检索与生成框架。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


### [119] [AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28617)
*Xiangning Lin,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Haoqian Zhang,Yipeng Zhao,Chengxuan Qian,Tianwei Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Zhenlong Yuan,Dingcheng Wang,Juncheng Wu,Yuan Si,Jiaxin Liu,Baolong Bi,Robert Mahari,Tobin South,Dazza Greenwood,Zexue He,Rishi Bommasani,Sophia Kazinnik,Andreas Haupt,Samuele Marro,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AISPA框架，用于系统审计商业AI产品中的系统提示（system prompts），并对88个商业AI产品中的3,249条指令进行审计，发现系统提示存在设计差异大、用户保护浅层化、提示词持续增长但问题指令仍普遍存在（约40%产品含对用户不利的指令）等问题，呼吁提高透明度、标准化和独立监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示由开发者配置以规范基础模型行为，广泛应用于商业AI产品中，但很少向公众或监管机构披露，造成AI系统大规模部署中严重的信任与问责缺口。作者旨在弥合这一缺口，提出用户视角的系统审计框架。

Method: 提出了AISPA（Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance）框架，从用户相关的八个维度系统审计系统提示的具体部分。对88个商业AI产品中的3,249条系统提示指令进行分类，将其区分为保护用户型（protective）或问题型（problematic）指令，并进行多维量化分析。

Result: 审计发现四大核心结论：(1) 不同产品与开发者之间的系统提示设计差异巨大；(2) 保护性指令虽广泛采用（98.9%产品至少含一条）但覆盖面浅（仅24%产品覆盖全部八个维度）；(3) 系统提示随时间不断变长且更注重用户保护；(4) 问题性指令仍普遍存在，约40%的产品包含至少一条违背用户利益的指令，且保护性指令与问题性指令常共存于同一提示中。

Conclusion: 研究表明商业AI产品的系统提示在用户保护方面虽有进步但仍存在显著不足，凸显了对系统提示加强透明度、标准化和独立监督的迫切需要。

Abstract: System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Theatre Chapbooks At Scale: A Statistical Comparative Analysis of Typography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27266)
*Diego Belzarena,Seginus Mowlavi,Paula Casariego Castiñeira,Alejandra Ulla Lorenzo,Gregory Randall,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种统计方法，通过自动提取印刷古籍中的字符图像并聚类对齐生成字符原型，从而量化两本书之间的字体距离，并采用反事实统计框架（a contrario framework）来解读字体距离的显著性。该方法应用于17世纪西班牙印刷戏剧小册子的语文学研究，规模远超人工目检能力，经专家验证后发现了新的印刷商归属并修正了旧有归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印刷古籍中的字体（typeface）差异是语文学研究（尤其是印刷商归属鉴定）的重要线索，但传统依赖人类专家逐本目检的方法在文献规模较大时效率极低、难以系统性覆盖。现有方法缺乏自动化和可量化的字体相似度度量，亟需一种能够大规模、自动化、有统计学依据的字体比较工具。

Method: 方法包含以下关键步骤：(1) 从书籍中自动提取字符图像；(2) 通过聚类和对齐从提取的字符图像中生成字符原型（character prototypes）；(3) 基于字符原型定义任意两本书之间的字体距离（typeface distance）；(4) 构建反事实统计框架（a contrario statistical framework），用于判定计算出的字体距离是否具有统计学意义上的显著性；(5) 分别对书籍中的Roman体和Italic体进行自动比较。

Result: 该方法被应用于17世纪西班牙印刷戏剧小册子的语文学研究，处理规模超出人类专家系统目检的能力。经人类专家验证，该方法成功发现了新的印刷商归属，并修正了此前错误的印刷商归属，表明其在数字书目学（digital bibliography）领域具有大规模应用的潜力。

Conclusion: 该统计方法能够在大规模历史文献语料上自动、可靠地比较字体相似度，为语文学研究提供了可扩展的工具。实际应用结果（新归属发现和旧归属修正）证明了其有效性，预示着该方法可在更大范围推动数字书目学的发展。

Abstract: We propose a statistical methodology that quantifies the similarity of typefaces between printed historical books. This provides a tool that accelerates philological analysis. Using character prototypes derived from clustering and aligning automatically extracted character images, the method defines a typeface distance between any two books. To produce actionable outputs, we develop an a contrario statistical framework to interpret the significance of the computed typeface distances. We apply the method to the philological study of 17 th -century Spanish printed theatre chapbooks in a quantity that exceeds the capabilities of systematic visual inspection by human experts. Our method enables the automatic comparison of Roman and Italic types extracted from different books. After validation by human experts, our method has led to new printer attributions being discovered, and former printer attributions being revised. This success strongly suggests that our method has the potential to enable digital bibliography on a larger scale than was previously possible.

</details>


### [121] [OVEarth-Bench: Evaluating Category Breadth and Query Diversity for Open-Vocabulary Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27278)
*Kaiyu Li,Zepeng Xin,Zixuan Jiang,Jing Fu,Lanxuan Xue,Lingyu Zhang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OVEarth-Bench基准，从类别广度和查询多样性两个方向扩展开放词汇地球观测（EO）评估，支持统一的零样本掩码和框定位协议。评估显示现有方法性能有限，MLLM方法表现最强，EO专用方法普遍逊于通用模型，为未来方法设计提供指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇地球观测基准通常覆盖狭窄的类别词汇或有限的查询形式，无法真实反映模型在多样化地理空间概念上的定位能力，亟需更全面、多样化和大规模的评估基准。

Method: OVEarth-Bench从两个方向扩展评估：(1) 类别广度——通过广泛的分层类别覆盖，包含正负表达能力；(2) 查询多样性——涵盖词汇、指代和推理三类查询。基准在统一的零样本协议下支持掩码和框两种定位任务，并系统评估了通用方法与EO专用方法。

Result: (1) 现有方法性能整体有限，但更广泛的类别覆盖带来更稳定的模型排名；(2) 基于MLLM的方法在所有方法中表现最佳；(3) EO专用方法普遍不如通用模型，很少能匹敌最强方法。

Conclusion: 研究结论为未来开放词汇EO方法设计提供指导，强调开发更真实、多样、高质量和大规模基准对可靠评估的重要性，并公开了数据与评估包。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary Earth observation (EO) aims to localize geospatial concepts specified in natural language rather than a fixed label set. Existing benchmarks, however, usually cover narrow category vocabularies or limited query forms. To fill this gap, we introduce OVEarth-Bench, which extends existing evaluation in two directions: category breadth, through broad hierarchical category coverage with positive and negative expressions, and query diversity, through vocabulary, referring, and reasoning queries. The benchmark supports mask and box localization under a unified zero-shot protocol. We evaluate a broad set of general and EO-specific methods. The evaluation reveals that: (1) the performance of current methods remains limited, while broader category coverage yields more stable model rankings; (2) MLLM-based methods achieve the strongest overall performance; and (3) EO-specific methods generally underperform general models and rarely match the strongest methods. These findings provide guidance for future open-vocabulary EO method design and highlight the importance of developing more realistic, diverse, high-quality, and large-scale benchmarks for reliable evaluation. Our data and evaluation package are released at https://earth-insights.github.io/OVEarth-bench.

</details>


### [122] [VETO: Towards Protecting Images From Frontier AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27292)
*Jonas Grebe,Hossein Shakibania,Tobias Braun,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VETO，一种针对现代图像编辑模型（如FLUX.2）的微妙反编辑"防护衣"，通过干扰模型中joint-attention机制来阻断模型读取源图像信息；同时引入VetoBench基准，用于评估包括传统局部编辑和广泛重语境化编辑在内的防御效果。实验表明VETO在两种现代编辑模型和三个基准上均优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代图像编辑模型（如FLUX.2）能力不断增强，不仅能进行局部修改，还能将对象和身份提取并重置于全新场景中，导致潜在有害编辑的空间大幅扩大。现有的反编辑防御方法针对的是旧版扩散模型中参考图像编码的语义瓶颈，但新一代编辑器通过joint-attention机制提取参考信息，常常能绕过这些防护。因此需要一种针对新机制的有效防御方法。

Method: 提出VETO（一种微妙的anti-edit cloak），通过干扰现代编辑模型中joint-attention模块的工作机制，阻断模型从源图像读取信息的关键通道。同时构建VetoBench基准，不仅评估传统局部编辑场景，还评估更广泛的语境迁移（recontextualization）场景下的防御效果。

Result: 在两种当代编辑模型和三个基准上的实验表明，VETO在所有测试场景中均持续优于现有防御方法，并在防护强度与图像保真度之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: 针对现代图像编辑模型的内部机制（joint-attention）设计防御是必要的。VETO提供了一种有效的方案，且VetoBench填补了现有编辑基准在重语境化评估上的空白，为未来反编辑防御研究提供了更全面的评估框架。

Abstract: The rise of powerful, accessible image-editing models such as FLUX.2 has brought high-fidelity editing within broad reach. Their capabilities now extend beyond localized modifications to extracting and recontextualizing objects and identities in entirely new scenes. By allowing prompt and generation tokens to attend directly to reference-image tokens, modern models blur the boundary between conventional editing and text-to-image synthesis. This expanded generative freedom also broadens the space of potential misuse, as harmful transformations are no longer confined to a predictable set of localized edits. Existing anti-edit defenses are designed to disrupt the semantic bottleneck of the reference-image encoding in legacy diffusion pipelines. However, newer editors distill reference information through joint-attention blocks, thereby often circumventing these protections. We therefore introduce VETO, a subtle anti-edit cloak that disrupts this inner mechanism through which modern models read the source image. Additionally, as existing editing benchmarks leave comprehensive recontextualizations largely untested, we introduce VetoBench, which evaluates defenses not only on conventional localized edits but also on broader contextual shifts. Across two contemporary editing models and three benchmarks, VETO consistently outperforms existing defenses while providing a stronger protection-fidelity trade-off.

</details>


### [123] [Bunraku: Turning a Single Illustration into an Editable Live2D Character](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27348)
*Junhao Chen,Jingjia Mao,Dayong Li,Chenghai Li,Saining Zhang,Zhihao Li,Hao Zhao,Yufei Wang,Ruqi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个从单张插画端到端生成完整Live2D结构化资产的系统，包括有序RGBA图层、逐图层形变网格和参数驱动的关键姿态顶点偏移，并贡献了标准化基准Live2D-Bench和8,884个模型的语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Live2D是2D动漫角色动画的主流格式，广泛应用于虚拟直播、手游和交互角色，但手工制作一个Live2D模型需要数周时间进行图层分离、遮挡补全、网格放置和关键帧制作，且此前没有任何生成方法能端到端产出这种结构化资产。

Method: 系统分两个阶段：Stage 1将图层分解建模为Live2D感知器官级分类法下的分层扩散过程，生成带隐藏区域补全的有序RGBA堆栈；Stage 2仅从各图层的alpha通道构建内容一致的三角网格，再联合预测所有图层的关键姿态位移场——每个顶点作为一个token，自注意力跨越图层边界，每个位移被分解为有界方向和log幅度。

Result: 联合而非独立预测是最大的增益来源，将网络规模放大112倍反而无提升。在50个保留角色上，无教师强迫的真实生成条件下，Stage 2每顶点方向余弦达到0.768（中位数0.828）。此外，衣物图层可凭自然语言指令重新贴图，而网格和预测动画可逐字节复用。

Conclusion: 这是首个端到端生成Live2D运行时所需全部结构化信息的系统，并提供了首个标准化基准与大规模监督语料，为该任务建立了评估标准。

Abstract: Live2D is the dominant 2D character-animation format for anime characters and virtual avatars, representing each character as a stack of RGBA layers driven by per-layer mesh deformation. Despite its wide use in virtual streaming, mobile games, and interactive characters, authoring a Live2D model still demands weeks of manual layer separation, occlusion completion, mesh placement, and keyframing, and no prior generative method produces such a structured asset end-to-end. We present the first system that, from a single illustration, generates all the structured information a Live2D runtime consumes: ordered RGBA layers, a deformation mesh per layer, and the parameter-driven keypose vertex offsets that make the character move. Stage 1 casts layered decomposition as a layered diffusion process under a Live2D-aware organ-level taxonomy, producing an ordered RGBA stack with hidden-region completion. Stage 2 builds a content-conforming triangle mesh for each layer from its alpha channel alone, then predicts the keypose displacement field of all layers jointly: every vertex of every layer is one token, self-attention spans layer boundaries, and each displacement is factorised into a bounded direction and a log-magnitude. Joint rather than independent prediction is what makes the result a coherent character instead of separately plausible parts, and is our largest gain; scaling the network 112x yields none. On 50 held-out characters, under true generation with no teacher forcing, Stage 2 attains a per-vertex direction cosine of 0.768 (median 0.828). Because a layer's mesh derives from its alpha channel, a clothing layer can be re-textured from a natural-language instruction while the mesh and predicted animation are reused byte-for-byte. We further contribute Live2D-Bench, the first standardized benchmark for the task, and an 8,884-model Live2D corpus with layer and animation supervision.

</details>


### [124] [Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation for Unpaired Cross-Modal Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27357)
*Dillan Imans,Phuoc-Nguyen Bui,Duc-Tai Le,Hyunseung Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出共享语义码本蒸馏（SSCD），通过一个共享的离散码本将教师和学生模态的表示映射到共同语义词汇空间，以分布对齐方式实现跨模态知识蒸馏，解决了医学图像中跨模态数据不配对、特征空间不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态知识蒸馏可将诊断知识从强但昂贵的教师模态转移到便宜易部署的学生模态。然而医学图像中两种模态通常不配对（来自不同患者队列），且特征空间几何不兼容，导致实例级蒸馏无效、直接特征匹配不可靠。

Method: SSCD通过共享离散码本比较教师和学生表示。每张图像被表示为共享、模态无关词汇上的分布，通过全局和类别条件两种方式对齐跨模态分布，无需配对样本或可直接比较的原始特征。码本通过指数移动平均在线更新，并通过熵正则化和死码重启保持多样性。推理时丢弃所有教师侧和码本模块，仅保留学生编码器和分类器。

Result: 在两个异构不配对设置（OCT到眼底视网膜疾病分类、CT到胸X光肺炎分类）上，SSCD分别将学生的macro-F1从64.5提升到70.2、从73.8提升到76.3，在两个设置上均优于所有评估的蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: SSCD通过共享语义码本实现了无需配对样本的跨模态知识蒸馏，在医学图像分析中取得了显著性能提升，且推理开销与纯学生模型相同，具有良好的实用性和部署价值。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can transfer diagnostic knowledge from a strong but costly teacher modality to a cheaper and more deployable student modality. In medical image analysis, however, the two modalities are often unpaired: they are collected from different patient cohorts and occupy geometrically incompatible feature spaces. This makes instance-level distillation invalid and direct feature matching unreliable. To address these challenges, we propose Shared Semantic Codebook Distillation (SSCD), which compares teacher and student representations through a shared discrete codebook. Each image is represented as a distribution over a common, modality-agnostic vocabulary, and knowledge is transferred by aligning these distributions across modalities, both globally and class-conditionally, without requiring paired samples or directly comparable raw features. The codebook is evolved online by exponential moving average and kept diverse through entropy regularization and dead-code restart. At inference, all teacher-side and codebook modules are discarded, leaving only the student encoder and classifier. On two heterogeneous unpaired settings, OCT-to-fundus retinal disease classification and CT-to-chest-X-ray pneumonia classification, SSCD improves the student from 64.5 to 70.2 macro-F1 and from 73.8 to 76.3 macro-F1, respectively, outperforming all evaluated distillation baselines on both settings. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/DillanImans/SSCD-unpaired-distillation

</details>


### [125] [PanDent: Toward Comprehensive Tooth-Level Structure-Language Consistency in Dental Radiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27378)
*Xiaohan Li,Xinyu Liu,Chang Liu,Sum Wing Au Yeung,Jun Liu,Yixuan Yuan,Hui Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PanDent，一个大规模、临床基础的口腔全景X光片（OPG）基准数据集，包含9,524张高质量影像及专家验证的牙级标注，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在牙科诊断中的临床推理能力。实验表明当前MLLMs虽能生成流畅报告但缺乏临床一致性，而在PanDent上微调可显著提升结构-语言一致性和诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在牙科全景X光片评估中缺乏细粒度、临床可靠的基准，无法反映专家对牙级诊断的解读标准，导致临床评估不准确。

Method: 构建PanDent基准数据集：包含9,524张高质量OPG影像，由经验丰富的牙医生成结构化牙级标注，并经口腔颌面放射科医生验证；采用临床定义的报告逻辑从已验证发现中构建临床一致的放射学报告，建立结构化临床证据与自由文本描述之间的显式对应关系；在多种MLLMs（包括专有SOTA模型、通用开源模型和医学专用模型）上进行评估，并探索在PanDent上的微调效果。

Result: 实验发现当前MLLMs能生成流畅的报告，但缺乏临床一致性，在细粒度定位和牙级诊断中产生大量错误；在PanDent上微调可显著提升结构-语言一致性，增强视觉定位准确性和诊断正确性，使模型输出更接近专家牙科解读。

Conclusion: PanDent为评估MLLMs的牙级临床推理建立了严格基准，是临床基础牙科AI的有价值资源，为进一步提升MLLMs在医学影像诊断中的临床可靠性提供了方向。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in dental panoramic radiography (orthopantomogram, OPG) is limited by the lack of fine-grained, clinically reliable benchmarks that reflect expert interpretation. This work introduces PanDent, a large-scale, clinically grounded OPG benchmark built upon fine-grained, expert-validated tooth-level annotations. The dataset comprises 9,524 high-quality OPGs, each associated with comprehensive structured annotations produced by experienced dentists and further validated by an oral and maxillofacial radiologist, providing clinically reliable supervision for tooth-level diagnosis and reasoning. Clinically consistent radiology reports are constructed from expert-validated findings using clinician-defined reporting logic, establishing explicit correspondence between structured clinical evidence and free-text descriptions. This design enables evaluation of whether MLLMs generate reports that are not only linguistically coherent but also clinically consistent with expert-validated tooth-level findings. Experiments are conducted on diverse MLLMs, including state-of-the-art (SOTA) proprietary models, general-domain open-source models, and medical-specific models. Results show that current MLLMs can generate fluent reports, yet fail to produce clinically consistent descriptions, exhibiting substantial errors in fine-grained localization and tooth-level diagnosis. Fine-tuning on PanDent significantly improves structure-language consistency, substantially enhancing visual localization accuracy and diagnostic correctness, and bringing model outputs closer to expert dental interpretation. These results establish PanDent as a rigorous benchmark for evaluating tooth-level clinical reasoning in MLLMs and a valuable resource for clinically grounded dental AI.

</details>


### [126] [VideoCoCo: Code-as-CoT for Physically-Consistent Video Generation via an Agentic Dual-Engine System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27380)
*Haodong Li,Tianfei Ren,Xiaoxiao Ma,Chunmei Qing,Zhen Fang,Sipeng He,Ziyu Guo,Haoyu Wu,Juanxi Tian,Yihang Zou,Ruichuan An,Dongzhi Jiang,Boxue Yang,Ji Xie,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Jialv Zou,Zhengrong Yue,Yaxin Luo,Xiaotong Li,Yuzhu Wang,Junyan Ye,Jinjing Zhao,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Renye Yan,Feng Zhao,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoCoCo是一个双引擎智能体框架，通过可执行的Blender代码作为过程级思维链，将物理一致性视频生成分解为确定性时空草稿生成和基于草稿的光照真实视频编辑两个阶段，在PhyGenBench和VBench-2.0上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型虽然视觉质量出色，但难以生成物理一致的动态，因为场景的时间演化需要从高度压缩的文本提示中隐式推断。已有的链式思维方法引入中间计划或视觉状态，但这些表示通常不可执行或时间稀疏，无法实例化和控制完整的时空过程。

Method: VideoCoCo采用双引擎架构：(1) 编码智能体根据文本提示合成可执行的Blender程序，明确指定场景及其时间演化；(2) 可执行仿真引擎运行程序生成确定性的时空草稿；(3) 生成式视频引擎通过草稿条件编辑将草稿转化为照片级真实视频。这种分解将过程级推理与高保真视觉实现分离。此外构建了VideoCoCo-3K数据集（草稿-指令-目标三元组）来适配视频编辑器。

Result: VideoCoCo在PhyGenBench上将OmniWeaving基线从0.475提升到0.558，在VBench-2.0上从52.18提升到77.88，在两个基准上均取得最佳平均分数。

Conclusion: 可执行代码为物理一致的视频生成提供了一种有效、可控且可检查的中间表示，揭示了将过程级推理（通过仿真）与高保真视觉实现分离的新范式。

Abstract: Text-to-video models have achieved remarkable visual quality, yet they still struggle to generate physically consistent dynamics because the temporal evolution of a scene must be inferred implicitly from a highly compressed text prompt. Existing chain-of-thought approaches introduce intermediate plans or visual states, but these representations are typically non-executable or temporally sparse, limiting their ability to instantiate and control the complete spatiotemporal process. To address this limitation, we introduce VideoCoCo, an agentic dual-engine framework in which executable Blender code serves as a process-level chain of thought. Given a text prompt, a coding agent synthesizes a Blender program that explicitly specifies the scene and its temporal evolution. The executable simulation engine runs the program to produce a deterministic spatiotemporal draft, which is subsequently transformed into a photorealistic video by a generative video engine through draft-conditioned editing. This decomposition separates process-level reasoning from high-fidelity visual realization. To adapt the video editor to simulated drafts, we construct VideoCoCo-3K, a curated dataset of draft-instruction-target triplets. VideoCoCo improves the OmniWeaving baseline from 0.475 to 0.558 on PhyGenBench and from 52.18 to 77.88 on VBench-2.0, achieving the best average score on both benchmarks. These results demonstrate that executable code provides an effective, controllable, and inspectable intermediate representation for physically consistent video generation.

</details>


### [127] [IGME: Efficient Chained Method Ensemble for Transferable Semantic Segmentation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27465)
*Mengqi He,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IGME方法，通过将可迁移攻击组合建模为可微分攻击组件的链式计算，并采用积分梯度式路径平均方向作为稳定化启发式，实现仅需单一源模型的语义分割高效可迁移对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割模型容易受到可迁移对抗扰动的影响，但评估密集预测模型上的迁移攻击计算成本极高。现有集成攻击通常依赖多个替代模型，进一步增加了计算开销，在分割任务上尤为困难。因此需要探索高效的单源模型替代方案。

Method: 将可迁移攻击组合形式化为在可微分攻击组件上的链式计算，使昂贵的源模型梯度计算得以共享；同时采用积分梯度风格的路径平均方向作为经验性稳定化启发式，以缓解链式组合带来的更新不稳定性。

Result: 在Pascal VOC和Cityscapes上的实验表明，IGME在CNN和Transformer分割模型上取得了与单源基线相当的迁移性，相比模型集成攻击具有更优的运行时间，且仅需访问一个源模型。

Conclusion: IGME在保持竞争力迁移性的同时显著提升了计算效率，证明了单一源模型条件下的高效可迁移攻击在语义分割任务上是可行的。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation models are vulnerable to transferable adversarial perturbations, yet evaluating transfer attacks on dense prediction models can be computationally expensive. Existing ensemble attacks often rely on multiple surrogate models, increasing the computation cost, even harder for segmentation. This paper studies an efficient single-source alternative for transferable attacks on semantic segmentation. We formulate transferable attack composition as a chained computation over differentiable attack components, allowing the expensive source-model gradient computation to be shared. To reduce the update instability introduced by chained composition, we further use an integrated-gradient-style path-averaged direction as an empirical stabilization heuristic. Experiments on Pascal VOC and Cityscapes evaluate the resulting transferability efficiency trade-off across CNN- and transformer-based segmentation models. IGME achieves competitive transferability compared with single-source baselines and favorable runtime compared with model-ensemble attacks, while requiring access to only one source model.

</details>


### [128] [ProgFormer: Hierarchical Voxel Diffusion Transformer for Longitudinal Brain MRI Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27537)
*Dexuan Ding,Yuankai Qi,Luping Zhou,Jian Yang,Quan Z. Sheng,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProgFormer，一种层级式体素空间扩散Transformer模型，用于纵向脑MRI预测。该模型通过粗路径建模整体脑结构和纵向上下文，细路径在体素级别进行精细细化，并通过条件流匹配直接在体素空间估计速度场实现端到端预测。在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS三个基准数据集上表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两方面问题：基于潜空间的方法在压缩-重建过程中丢失信息；直接体素空间方法使用统一预测通路，微小的局部变化会被占主导的稳定脑结构所遮蔽。因此需要一个既能保持全局结构一致性又能敏感捕捉细粒度疾病进展的模型。

Method: ProgFormer采用双通路层级架构：(1) 粗通路从3D patch token进行主要体积极预测，建模整体脑结构和纵向上下文；(2) 细通路以粗表示为时空基准，在单个patch内进行体素级细化。两条通路通过条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）在体素空间中联合估计速度场，无需单独学习的图像自编码器，通过Euler步积分从高斯噪声生成预测的未来扫描图像。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS三个广泛使用的基准数据集上，分别在成对（pairwise）和轨迹（trajectory）两种设置下进行了实验，结果表明ProgFormer优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: ProgFormer通过层级式粗-细双通路设计有效解决了全局结构保持与局部变化捕捉之间的矛盾，为纵向脑MRI预测提供了一种无需潜空间重建的端到端体素空间解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting future structural MRI of a brain is challenging because longitudinal changes are often subtle and confined to specific anatomical regions, while most subject-specific brain structure remains stable over time. An effective model should therefore preserve global brain structural consistency while remaining sensitive to fine-grained disease progression. Existing latent-space-based methods improve computational efficiency, but suffer from information loss during their compression-reconstruction procedure. In contrast, direct voxel-space methods avoid latent reconstruction but commonly use a unified prediction pathway to model brain structure and progression-related changes. Subtle local changes may therefore be overshadowed by the dominant stable brain structure. To address these challenges, we propose ProgFormer, a hierarchical voxel-space Diffusion Transformer for longitudinal brain MRI prediction. ProgFormer uses a coarse pathway to perform the primary volumetric prediction from 3D patch tokens. This pathway models overall brain structure and longitudinal context. The fine pathway then uses the coarse representations as spatio-temporal grounding for voxel-level refinement within individual patches. The two pathways jointly estimate a velocity field directly in voxel space through conditional flow matching, enabling end-to-end prediction without a separately learned image autoencoder. The predicted future scan is then generated from Gaussian noise by integrating the estimated velocity field over a sequence of Euler steps. Extensive experimental results on three widely used benchmarks, ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS, under both pairwise and trajectory settings demonstrate favourable performance compared against several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [129] [Drawing-Recode: Annotation Grounding for Parametric CAD Code Generation from Raster 2D CAD Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27558)
*Mingi Kim,Yongjun Kim,Hyungki Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Drawing-Recode框架，从光栅格式的2D CAD图纸中恢复参数化CAD序列，通过图像编码器提取几何特征、文本识别模块识别标注，并利用交叉注意力与Annotation Grounding Loss将标注与几何信息显式关联，最终由LLM生成SPCC格式的CAD代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字转型前积累的大量光栅格式2D CAD图纸需要被数字化恢复为参数化CAD序列以支持零件复制和制造流程自动化。然而现有研究要么仅处理矢量图纸，要么局限于特定领域，且未能将尺寸标注与几何信息显式关联，限制了尺寸信息在参数化CAD序列恢复中的应用。

Method: Drawing-Recode包含三部分：(1)图像编码器提取几何特征；(2)独立文本识别模块识别尺寸标注；(3)通过交叉注意力机制和提出的Annotation Grounding Loss (AGL)将标注显式接地到几何信息上。融合后的特征输入LLM生成SPCC格式的CAD代码。

Result: 实验表明Drawing-Recode优于现有基线方法，且在模拟工业条件的扫描图纸上表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Drawing-Recode有望促进工业场景中光栅2D CAD图纸的数字化，助力零件复制和制造自动化。

Abstract: Recovering Parametric CAD sequences from raster-format 2D Computer-Aided Design (CAD) drawings accumulated prior to digital transformation is important for part reproduction and manufacturing process automation. However, existing studies either process only vector drawings or are limited to specific domains, and fail to explicitly connect dimensional annotations to geometric information, limiting their use of dimensional information for 3D Parametric CAD sequences recovery. We propose Drawing-Recode, a framework that generates Parametric CAD sequences as CAD code from raster 2D CAD drawings. Drawing-Recode extracts geometric features via an image encoder and recognizes annotations through a separate text recognition module, then explicitly grounds annotations to geometric information using cross-attention and our proposed Annotation Grounding Loss (AGL). The resulting features are fed into a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate CAD code in the Structured Parametric CAD Code (SPCC) format. Experiments show that Drawing-Recode outperforms existing baselines and remains robust on scanned drawings resembling industrial conditions. We expect Drawing-Recode contributes to digitizing raster 2D CAD drawings in industrial settings and to part reproduction and manufacturing automation.

</details>


### [130] [Inference-Time Agentic Decision Rules Beat Longer Evolving Search for Multi-Image Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27564)
*Site Li,Jianyi Hao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 多图像医学VQA的核心挑战在于代理式决策而非prompt长度。通过对比五种推理时代理策略，最简的order-vote聚合策略在最终测试集上达到57.89%准确率，显著优于固定基线和更复杂的order-rerank变体；将进化搜索预算从50代扩展到100代并未带来泛化收益，反而导致测试准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多图像医学视觉问答（VQA）不仅仅是一个提示长度（prompt-length）问题，而是一个根本性的代理式决策挑战。医学视觉语言代理需要跨有序图像聚合证据，对答案顺序扰动保持鲁棒性，并避免对嘈杂的搜索时反馈过拟合。现有方法缺乏对推理时代理策略的系统性对比。

Method: 在MedFrameQA框架下，使用相同的ShinkaEvolve高预算配置，对五种推理时代理策略进行受控对比实验。实验在可复现的内部冻结划分上进行（1,331进化样本、665留出样本、855最终测试样本），每个策略进行五次独立重复运行，并使用配对bootstrap分析验证性能差异的显著性。

Result: order-vote策略在最终测试集上达到57.89±0.65%准确率，显著优于固定基线（52.73±0.42%）和更复杂的order-rerank变体（55.79±0.43%）。将进化搜索预算从50代扩展到100代导致最终测试准确率从57.89%降至56.02%，即使留出性能略有提升。

Conclusion: 对于多图像医学推理而言，定义正确的代理式决策规则比扩大优化搜索预算更具影响力。最鲁棒的解决方案往往是最简单的聚合策略，复杂方法可能更脆弱，且扩展搜索预算不一定带来泛化收益。

Abstract: Multi-image medical VQA is not merely a prompt-length problem; it is a fundamental challenge of agentic decision-making. Medical vision-language agents must aggregate evidence across ordered images, remain robust to answer-order perturbations, and avoid overfitting to noisy search-time feedback. We study MedFrameQA through a controlled comparison of five inference-time agentic strategies, optimized using the same high-budget ShinkaEvolve configuration and evaluated on a reproducible internal frozen split (1,331 evolution, 665 holdout, 855 final test). Across five independent repeated runs, the strongest method emerges as the simplest robust aggregator: the \textbf{order-vote} policy achieves $57.89 \pm 0.65\%$ final-test accuracy, significantly outperforming the fixed baseline ($52.73 \pm 0.42\%$) and the more complex, albeit brittle, order-rerank variant ($55.79 \pm 0.43\%$). Paired bootstrap analysis confirms these significant gains. Extending the evolutionary search budget from 50 to 100 generations yields no generalization benefit: while holdout performance marginally increases, final-test accuracy drops from $57.89\%$ to $56.02\%$. Our findings suggest that for multi-image medical reasoning, defining the correct agentic decision rule is substantially more impactful than expanding the optimization search budget.

</details>


### [131] [Objective-Aligned Direct Answer SFT for Robust Multi-Frame Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27566)
*Site Li,Jianyi Hao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在MedFrameQA医学多帧视觉问答基准上比较了多种自适应方法，发现直接解码器式答案SFT（在MedGemma-1.5-4B上）是最强的鲁棒自适应家族，而非复杂的控制器式或分阶段方法；事后校准可修复置信度估计且不影响准确率，方法可迁移到Qwen2.5-VL-3B等骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多帧医学VQA领域的研究倾向于追求日益复杂的方法（控制器式推理、定位感知重排、静态难负例混合、分阶段连续训练），本文质疑这种复杂性偏好，提出一个更简单的竞争性假设：与基准最终答案目标紧密对齐的方法应是最强的鲁棒自适应家族，并希望通过严格的受控评估来检验这一假设。

Method: 在MedFrameQA上对四类方法进行系统性受控比较：控制器式方法、脚手架演化（scaffold evolution）、静态混合监督、延续式变体，以及直接仅答案监督微调（answer-only SFT）。评估控制条件包括固定数据划分、匹配计算预算、重复随机种子和校准。基于MedGemma-1.5-4B骨干进行实验，并辅以Qwen2.5-VL-3B作为次骨干验证迁移性，采用事后校准修复置信度估计。

Result: 直接解码器式答案SFT在MedFrameQA上显著优于其他复杂自适应家族，在保留报告集上大幅超越冻结基线，且跨重复种子和匹配对照保持高度稳定，而非孤立超参数峰值。事后校准在不降低准确率的前提下有效修复置信度估计，且核心方法在Qwen2.5-VL-3B上一致迁移。

Conclusion: 主要结论不是复杂辅助机制获胜，而是目标对齐的直接答案SFT是MedFrameQA上最强的鲁棒自适应家族。通过建立这一强而极简的基线，作者希望引导社区关注根本性的鲁棒优化而非架构复杂性。

Abstract: Multi-frame medical VQA appears to reward increasingly complex adaptation: controller-style inference, localization-aware reranking, static hard-negative mixing, and staged continuation all appear plausible from first principles. We test a simpler competing hypothesis on MedFrameQA: methods that remain tightly aligned with the benchmark's final answer objective should be the strongest \emph{robust} adaptation family once evaluation is controlled across fixed splits, matched budgets, repeated seeds, and calibration. We compare controller-based methods, scaffold evolution, static mixed supervision, continuation-heavy variants, and direct answer-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT). The strongest robust family is direct decoder-only answer SFT on MedGemma-1.5-4B. Empirically, this family yields substantial improvements in held-out report accuracy over frozen baselines while remaining remarkably stable across repeated seeds and matched controls, ensuring our claims reflect true family-level robustness rather than an isolated hyperparameter peak. Furthermore, post-hoc calibration effectively repairs confidence estimation without compromising accuracy, and the core approach transfers consistently to secondary backbones like Qwen2.5-VL-3B. The main result is therefore not that a complex auxiliary mechanism wins, but that objective-aligned direct answer SFT is the strongest robust adaptation family we found for MedFrameQA. By establishing this strong, minimalist baseline, we hope to redirect community focus toward fundamentally robust optimization rather than architectural complexity.

</details>


### [132] [ZMIS-SAM: Segment Anything Model Enhanced with Wavelet Transform for Zooplankton Microscopy Image Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27585)
*Dekun Yuan,Zhongwei Li,Zheng Qiao,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于SAM和小波变换的浮游动物实例分割模型ZMIS-SAM，通过三个核心创新模块（ZM-ViT、NFAM、WM2FE）解决了SAM在浮游动物微观图像分割中分类不准确、细长附肢分割不连续和边界分割不完整的问题，在多个数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮游动物作为海洋食物链中的初级消费者，对维持海洋生态平衡至关重要。然而SAM因缺乏浮游动物领域知识，在微观图像实例分割任务中表现有限，存在分类不准确、细长附肢分割不连续和边界分割不完整等问题。

Method: 提出ZMIS-SAM框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) ZM-ViT通过两个轻量级适配器增强SAM对浮游动物形态和图像强度分布的建模能力；(2) 邻域特征聚合模块（NFAM）通过整合通用特征与领域特定特征，改善半透明细长附肢的连续分割；(3) 基于小波的多尺度多方向特征增强模块（WM2FE）通过恢复高频细节来细化边界分割完整性。

Result: 大量实验表明，ZMIS-SAM在浮游动物数据集上取得了最先进的实例分割性能，并在多个公共跨域数据集上展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ZMIS-SAM通过将SAM与领域知识和小波变换相结合，有效解决了浮游动物实例分割中的关键挑战，为该领域提供了更精准、更可靠的分割方案。

Abstract: As primary consumers in the marine food chain, zooplankton play a crucial role in maintaining marine ecological balance. However, the Segment Anything Model (SAM) exhibits limited performance in microscopic image instance segmentation due to its lack of zooplankton-specific domain knowledge. To address these challenges, we propose a novel instance segmentation model based on SAM and wavelet transform (ZMIS-SAM), effectively tackling issues such as inaccurate classification, discontinuous segmentation of slender appendages, and incomplete boundary segmentation. Our framework incorporates three core innovations: ZM-ViT enhances SAM's capability to model zooplankton morphology and image intensity distributions through two lightweight adapters, the Neighboring Feature Aggregation Module (NFAM) improves continuous segmentation of semi-transparent slender appendages by integrating general-purpose and domain-specific features, and the Wavelet-based Multi-scale Multi-directional Feature Enhancement (WM2FE) module effectively recovers high-frequency details to refine boundary segmentation completeness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ZMIS-SAM achieves state-of-the-art instance segmentation performance on the zooplankton dataset and exhibits strong generalization capability across multiple public cross-domain datasets.

</details>


### [133] [MeshFM: 2D Features Are All You Need for 3D Shape Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27592)
*Jinfan Zhou,Richard Liu,Itai Lang,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshFM是一种高效的3D特征提取前馈框架，通过从2D视觉基础模型蒸馏特征到3D，采用两阶段训练策略（先优化特征场，再用网络回归），无需3D标注即可在部件分割、密集对应和网格变形等下游任务上与3D监督方法表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D特征学习方法通常依赖3D标注或推理时的优化过程，成本高昂且效率低下。作者旨在利用2D视觉基础模型中蕴含的丰富语义信息，构建一个无需3D标注、无需推理优化的前馈式3D特征提取方法。

Method: 提出两阶段训练策略：(1) 仅使用2D特征监督在3D空间中优化一个特征场；(2) 训练一个前馈网络回归该特征场。整个训练过程无需3D标注，仅依赖2D基础模型中的信息，且训练中增强了对输入对象极端旋转的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量实验表明，MeshFM仅用2D监督训练就能在部件分割、密集对应和网格变形等任务上与显式使用3D监督的方法表现相当，甚至无需任务特定的微调。

Conclusion: MeshFM证明了2D视觉基础模型的特征可以直接、高效地蒸馏到3D领域，为3D特征学习提供了一种无需3D标注的有效替代方案，并具备良好的泛化能力和多任务适用性。

Abstract: We present MeshFM, an efficient feedforward framework for extracting rich features from 3D inputs. Our method distills 2D features from visual foundation models into 3D. We train a feedforward network to directly predict 3D features without requiring optimization during inference. The approach utilizes a two-stage training strategy. First, we optimize a feature field in 3D using only 2D feature supervision. Second, we train a network to regress this feature field. The entire procedure requires no 3D annotation, instead relying on the powerful information in 2D foundation models. We demonstrate that our learned features can be immediately applied to downstream tasks, including part segmentation, dense correspondence, and mesh deformation. Extensive experiments show that MeshFM, trained solely with 2D supervision, performs on par with methods trained explicitly with 3D supervision, even without task-specific fine-tuning. Moreover, our model is trained to be robust to extreme rotations of the input objects. Project page: https://threedle.github.io/MeshFM/

</details>


### [134] [MPIE-Bench: Benchmarking Anatomically Plausible Multi-Person Interaction Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27616)
*Jiajia Lin,Mingxuan Du,Tuowen Zhou,Benfeng Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MPIE-Bench多人物交互编辑基准（2500个样本、405个场景、14类交互、4个接触密度）以及MPIE-Eval评测方法，通过基于网格的多人物重建对接触时刻的解剖学与交互几何进行量化评分。实验显示现有文本到图像编辑器在多人物接触场景中表现不佳（mesh Anatomy最高0.65、mesh Interaction最高0.72），而VLM检查清单评测存在明显饱和问题（>0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像及个性化编辑模型虽能高质量生成单体图像，但在生成多人共享接触动作（如拥抱、携带、摔跤）时仍存在肢体融合、多余肢体、身体互相穿透等严重缺陷。此外，现有评测基准忽视这些解剖与几何问题，VLM作为评审的检查清单评测在交互维度上易饱和（分数虚高），无法真实反映人类可见的错误，因此需要更可靠的基准与评测方法。

Method: 构建MPIE-Bench基准：从视频中挖掘2500个编辑三元组，覆盖405个场景、14种交互类别和4个接触密度等级（C0-C3）。提出MPIE-Eval评测框架，包含两个新评测轴：1）Anatomy——通过冻结的公开多人体网格重建模型来判断每个人形质量是否可被完整重建身体集合所解释；2）Interaction——评估重建身体之间的穿透程度与表面距离是否与指令所要求的接触行为一致。该评测基于网格几何而非VLM定性判断。

Result: 在十个编辑模型上，mesh Anatomy得分最高为0.65，mesh Interaction最高为0.72，且没有单一编辑器在两个维度均表现优异；而VLM检查清单对相同图像评分超过0.95，暴露了评测饱和问题。五名评分者的人工研究确认两个评测轴比零样本VLM评审更贴近人类判断，且在对所有权重和阈值进行消融后排名依然保持稳定。

Conclusion: MPIE-Bench和MPIE-Eval为多人交互编辑提供了首个系统性评测工具，弥补了现有评测在解剖正确性与交互几何上的空白。该工作证明VLM评审在多人物接触场景中严重高估模型性能，而基于网格几何的评测能更准确反映人类感知，为未来多人交互生成与编辑模型的改进提供了可量化、可复现的评估标准。

Abstract: Text-to-image and personalized editing models now synthesize high-fidelity single-subject images with ease. Yet placing multiple named people into shared contact actions such as embrace, carry, or grapple still exposes major failures: fused limbs, invented extremities, and interpenetrating bodies. Existing evaluations largely overlook these anatomical and geometric issues, and VLM-as-a-judge checklists often saturate on Interaction while the errors remain obvious to humans. We introduce MPIE-Bench, a 2,500-sample benchmark of video-mined editing triplets spanning 405 scenes, 14 interaction categories, and four contact densities (C0-C3). We also propose MPIE-Eval, whose two new axes score contact-time geometry from a frozen public multi-person mesh reconstruction. Anatomy asks whether every human-like mass is explained by a complete set of reconstructed bodies, and Interaction asks whether the penetration and surface distance between those bodies match the contact the instruction asked for. Across ten editors, mesh Anatomy tops out at 0.65 and mesh Interaction at 0.72 on two different models, so no single editor is strong on both, while VLM checklists rate the same images above 0.95. A five-rater study confirms that both axes track human judgement more closely than a zero-shot VLM judge, and the rankings hold under ablation of every weight and threshold.

</details>


### [135] [MedXplore: Towards Reliable and Unbiased Generalized Category Discovery in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27620)
*Jianwei He,Kailin Lyu,Junhao Dong,Long Xiao,Wenjie Hou,Jingze Lu,Di Wu,Lin Shu,Jie Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedXplore是一个针对医学影像广义类别发现（GCD）的统一框架，从感知层和决策层两个层面进行优化，在多个基准上平均All准确率提升8.5%，并显著降低了旧-新类别混淆错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学图像分析方法依赖大规模标注和封闭世界假设，在临床实践中难以成立。虽然广义类别发现（GCD）在自然图像领域进展迅速，但在医学影像中仍未被充分探索，需要针对医学图像特性（如病变区域局部性、旧类偏置等）设计专门的GCD方法。

Method: 提出MedXplore框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 感知层：Frequency-SNR Adaptive Attention and Consistency (FAAC)，从频域视角进行可学习的全频谱滤波和全局-局部能量对比激活，既突出全局背景下的局部异常信号，又为补丁一致性学习提供可靠的语义锚点；(2) 决策层：Adaptive Cosine-Angular Margin (ACAM)，利用语义难度和特征置信度动态调整角度边距，平衡类内紧凑性和类间可分性。

Result: 在多个基准上，MedXplore相比最强对比方法平均All准确率提升8.5%。在Kvasir数据集上，旧-新类别误分类错误从14.50%降至0.80%，展现出在严重旧新模糊场景下的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedXplore通过感知层和决策层的协同优化，有效提升了病变敏感表示学习能力并缓解了旧类偏置问题，为医学影像中的广义类别发现提供了可靠且无偏的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown strong potential in medical image analysis, but most existing methods rely on large-scale annotations and a closed-world assumption that rarely holds in clinical practice. Although Generalized Category Discovery (GCD) has advanced rapidly on natural images, it remains underexplored in medical imaging. To address this issue, we propose MedXplore, a unified framework for reliable and unbiased medical GCD, optimizing from both perceptual and decision levels.
