### [202] [ShadowDancer: Teaching Video World Models Any Action by Learning Unified Dynamics Representations from a Video and Its Shadow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28362)
*Jin Cao,Zian Meng,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShadowDancer 提出一种基于"影子对"（shadow pairs）与跨影子预测（cross-shadow prediction）的新型框架，实现对交互式视频世界模型的任意动作、帧级精确控制。通过同一动力学在不同外观下的成对视频进行自监督学习，使任意示范视频成为可复用的动作资产，无需动作标签、运动估计器或微调即可在新场景中复现动力学，平均盲测胜率达86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型的动作接口存在表示层面的根本矛盾：宽松编码（文本/语义指令）让模型自由发挥、控制不精确；精确编码（结构化信号）只适用于单一动力学家族且难以获取。示范视频天然能逐帧指定任意动力学，但其动力学仅通过某一特定外观（"影子"）显现，导致从示范中学到的动作难以迁移到新场景。作者希望利用成对"同动力学、不同外观"的视频，精确可控地定义并迁移任意动作。

Method: 两大核心创新：(1) 影子对（shadow pairs）——由 Shadow Library 在规模上自动构建，将同一条动力学在不同随机重采样外观下重放为成对视频，使某个动力学族可精确控制，当且仅当其影子对可被构建；(2) 跨影子预测（cross-shadow prediction）——通过用一条影子预测另一条影子来学习动作，配对时被重采样的内容在构建时被自动丢弃，被保留下来的内容即构成动作，从而得到统一的动力学表征，驱动块因果世界模型（block-causal world model）。

Result: 实验覆盖多种动力学家族，相较强潜在动作（latent-action）基线与交互式世界模型基线，ShadowDancer 在动作迁移与长程动作推演（long action rollout）上均有显著提升，滚动比较的平均盲测胜率（blinded win rate）达到86%。

Conclusion: ShadowDancer 证明：通过在可控构建的影子对上进行自监督跨影子预测，可以优雅地解决动作接口的表示难题，使任意示范剪辑变为可复用的动作资产，在新环境中无需额外标注或微调即可实现帧级精确的动力学控制，为交互式视频世界模型提供了一种通用、可扩展的动作控制范式。

Abstract: We present ShadowDancer, a novel approach to any-action, frame-level control of interactive video world models. The obstacle is representational: existing interfaces either encode an action loosely, leaving how it unfolds for the model to improvise, or encode it exactly through structured signals that serve one family and are hard to acquire, so precise control across diverse dynamics remains impractical. Demonstration videos are the natural remedy, specifying any dynamics frame by frame; yet a video shows its dynamics only through one particular appearance, a single shadow of the underlying dynamics, so actions learned from demonstrations transfer poorly to new scenes. ShadowDancer addresses this with two key innovations: (1) shadow pairs, video pairs that replay the same dynamics under independently resampled appearance, constructed at scale by our Shadow Library, so that a dynamics family becomes controllable exactly when such pairs can be constructed for it; and (2) cross-shadow prediction, which learns actions by predicting one shadow from the other, so that whatever the pairing resamples is discarded by construction and whatever it preserves becomes the action, yielding a unified dynamics representation that drives a block-causal world model. Any demonstrated clip thus becomes a reusable action asset, replayed in new environments without action labels, motion estimators, or fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate improved action transfer and long action rollout over strong latent-action and interactive world model baselines across diverse dynamics families, with an average blinded win rate of 86% in rollout comparisons. We show video results at https://ShadowDancer-1.github.io

</details>


### [203] [Explaining Image Similarity with Automatically Extracted Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28386)
*Isaac Roberts,Petra Bevandic,Alexander Schulz,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型无关、度量无关的图像相似性可解释性框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）自动提取概念激活向量（CAVs），通过扰动嵌入空间中的概念方向来衡量其对相似度函数的影响，从而解释图像对之间相似性的驱动因素，并扩展到群组级别和示例检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像相似性可解释性方法大多依赖基于梯度的归因图，只能提供局部的解释，无法在嵌入空间区域中提供关于纹理、形状、颜色等全局性驱动的洞察。因此需要一种能够揭示图像相似性背后具体概念的框架。

Method: 提出一个模型和度量无关的框架：1）使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）自动从嵌入空间中提取概念激活向量（CAVs）；2）对图像对的嵌入沿发现的概念方向进行扰动，测量所选相似性函数的变化以获得概念重要性；3）提供概念归因图进行局部定位；4）扩展到群组级别，解释图像簇中相似性的驱动因素；5）引入示例检索（Exemplar Retrieval），检索具有相似相似性原因的图像样本。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的潜在空间扰动相比像素空间基线更忠实于底层数据分布，概念重要性能够线性恢复真实的相似性得分。定性结果进一步证实了该方法在理解模型个体和群体相似性判断方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了图像相似性可解释性中缺乏全局概念性洞察的问题，能够从个体和群组层面解释模型做出相似性判断的原因，且不依赖于特定模型或相似性度量。

Abstract: Image similarity underlies many computer vision applications, yet it is often unclear why two images receive a high or low similarity score. Existing explainability methods often rely on gradient-based attribution maps to provide local justifications for similarity. These approaches struggle to provide global insights into what specifically drives similarity in regions of an embedding space, such as texture, shape, or color. We introduce a model- and metric-agnostic framework that explains image similarity using Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) extracted automatically via Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). Given a pair of images, we perturb their embeddings along discovered concept directions and measure the resulting change in a chosen similarity function, yielding concept importances. For image pairs, we provide localization with concept attribution maps. We extend this procedure to group-level settings, explaining what drives similarity across a cluster of images rather than a single pair, and further, we introduce Exemplar Retrieval, aiming to recover samples with similar reasons contributing to similarity. Our experiments show that our latent perturbations are more faithful to the underlying data distribution than pixel-space baselines, and that concept importances linearly recover the true similarity score. Qualitative results further confirm the usefulness of our methods in understanding a model's individual and group similarity judgments.

</details>


### [204] [Hand-Object Interaction in the Age of Large Foundation Models:Reconstruction, Generation, and Embodied Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28394)
*Weiquan Lin,Yu Deng,Shiyang Liu,Luping Xiao,Xu Tang,Junzhi Yu,Jiaolong Yang,Lei Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇关于基础模型先验用于手-物交互（HOI）建模的系统性综述。文章将文献组织为六个HOI任务，提出包含几何、语义和视觉三大类共八种基础模型子先验的分类体系，系统分析了这些先验在不同HOI流水线和任务中的表示、注入与适配方式，并进一步探讨了HOI知识在机器人学习中的应用、数据集与评估协议及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手-物交互（HOI）建模需要在严重的视觉不确定性下联合推理手部姿态、物体几何、接触关系、语义和动力学，挑战巨大。基础模型提供了跨域大规模学习获得的迁移先验知识，为突破任务特定数据和模型的局限提供了新途径。然而相关文献增长迅速但碎片化严重，现有研究通常仅将其简单描述为"使用大模型"，缺乏对引入何种知识、知识在HOI流水线的何处注入、以及它帮助减少哪些HOI不确定性的系统性刻画，因此需要一篇系统的综述来建立统一认知框架。

Method: 本文通过系统性文献综述方法对HOI领域的基础模型先验进行全面梳理。首先将HOI文献组织为六个任务（覆盖重建和生成两大方向）；然后提出包含八种基础模型子先验的分类学框架，归入几何（形状检索、形状重建、空间重建）、语义（语义接地、语言推理）和视觉（视觉表征、图像生成、视频生成）三大类别；基于该分类系统分析不同先验在HOI流水线和任务中的表示、注入及适配方式；并额外考察HOI知识在机器人学习中的三种应用途径（人类数据预训练、人-机器人技能迁移、HOI到机器人数据生成）；最后汇总数据集、评估协议并讨论局限和未来方向，并维护一个持续更新的在线仓库。

Result: 本文建立了首个面向HOI的基础模型先验系统性综述框架，提出了六任务×八子先验的组织结构，明确了不同先验类型对应的知识来源、注入位置和所缓解的HOI不确定性类型；系统梳理了HOI知识向机器人学习迁移的三种路径；汇总了相关数据集和评估协议，并提供了持续更新的社区资源仓库。

Conclusion: 基础模型先验为HOI建模提供了不同于任务特定数据和模型的新范式，通过几何、语义和视觉三类先验的系统注入，可在不同层面缓解HOI中的视觉不确定性。HOI知识向机器人学习的迁移展示了更广泛的应用潜力，但当前仍存在可泛化性不足等挑战，未来需要更统一的框架和持续更新的社区资源来推动该领域的长远发展。

Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) modeling remains challenging because it requires joint reasoning about hand articulation, object geometry, contact, semantics, and dynamics under severe visual uncertainty. Foundation models introduce transferable prior knowledge learned from large-scale cross-domain data, offering new ways to address these challenges beyond task-specific data and models. However, the rapidly growing literature remains fragmented, and existing studies typically describe these methods simply as ``using large models'' without systematically characterizing what knowledge is introduced, where it enters the HOI pipeline, or which HOI uncertainty it helps reduce. This survey presents the first systematic review of foundation-model priors for HOI. We organize the literature into six HOI tasks spanning reconstruction and generation. More importantly, we establish a taxonomy of eight foundation-model sub-priors grouped into geometric, semantic, and visual families. Geometric priors encompass shape retrieval, shape reconstruction, and spatial reconstruction; semantic priors include semantic grounding and language reasoning; and visual priors cover visual representation, image generation, and video generation. Based on this taxonomy, we systematically analyze how different priors are represented, injected, and adapted across HOI pipelines and tasks. Beyond how foundation models empower HOI, we further examine how HOI-derived knowledge is used in robot learning, including human-data pretraining, human-to-robot skill transfer, and HOI-to-robot data generation. Finally, we summarize datasets and evaluation protocols, and discuss limitations and future directions toward more generalizable HOI systems. To support long-term progress, we curate a live repository that continuously aggregates emerging methods and benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [Large scale cross-regional remote sensing flood monitoring framework for operative mapping and impact analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28401)
*Ilya Novikov,Svetlana Illarionova,Ruslan Dzharkinov,Maria Smirnova,Ayrat Abdullin,Anna Korotkova,Mariia Ulianova,Dmitrii Shadrin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于俄罗斯联邦领土可持续洪水监测与灾害评估的端到端多模态框架，基于SAR数据、多光谱影像和DEM及其衍生物组成的21通道输入。在自收集的覆盖七个俄罗斯区域的多模态数据集上，比较了监督U-Net++与自监督AnySat两种水表检测策略，并利用最佳洪水预测按照俄罗斯紧急情况部官方方法评估城市区域洪水影响，应用于2019年图伦洪水时结果与官方评估高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的遥感洪水监测在环境多样的大范围区域仍具挑战，因为大多数分割算法缺乏大规模应用所需的泛化能力，且标注洪水数据稀缺且分布不均。需要开发能在数据和标注有限条件下仍有效工作的洪水监测框架。

Method: 构建端到端多模态框架，融合SAR数据、多光谱影像和DEM及其衍生物形成21通道输入。自收集覆盖七个俄罗斯区域的多模态数据集，比较两种水表检测策略：监督U-Net++与自监督AnySat架构（预训练+微调）。最佳预测结果按俄罗斯紧急情况部官方方法评估城市洪水影响（面积、物质损失、伤亡、生态和农业影响）。

Result: 在本文数据条件下，监督学习（U-Net++）比AnySat更有效，但AnySat在稳定性上更优，且在存在大量未标注数据或推理时缺少模态的场景中具有优势。应用于2019年图伦洪水时，结果与官方评估高度一致，唯物质损失因使用开源数据库而略有差异。

Conclusion: 深度学习和多模态卫星数据集成在跨多样环境和数据受限条件下具有可扩展、可靠洪水监测的潜力。监督方法在有限标注数据下效果更佳，而自监督方法为未来更大规模未标注数据和模态缺失场景提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Effective flood monitoring is critical for minimizing the impacts of flood disasters on populations and infrastructure. Yet reliable remote sensing across extensive and environmentally diverse regions remains challenging, as most segmentation algorithms lack the generalisation capacity required for large-scale application, while annotated flood data are scarce and unevenly distributed.
  This study presents an end-to-end multimodal framework for Russian Federation territories sustainable flood monitoring and damage assessment based on synthetic aperture radar data, multispectral imagery, and digital elevation models with their derivatives, forming a 21-channel input. Using a self-collected multimodal dataset covering seven Russian regions, two strategies for water surface detection under limited data conditions were compared: a supervised U-Net++ model and the self-supervised AnySat architecture pre-trained and fine-tuned for the segmentation task. Under the data conditions of this study, supervised learning proved more effective, while the AnySat-based approach offered greater stability and retains advantages for settings where larger unlabelled data or missing modalities at inference are expected.
  The best flood area predictions were used to estimate flood impact in urban areas in terms of the area affected, material damage, casualties, and ecological and agricultural impact. The estimations were conducted following the official methodology of the Russian Ministry of Emergency Situations. Applied to the 2019 Tulun flood, the obtained results closely matched official assessments, except for material damage, due to the open-source databases usage. The results demonstrate the potential of deep learning and multimodal satellite data integration for scalable, reliable flood monitoring across diverse environmental and data-limited conditions.

</details>


### [206] [Negative controls reveal volume-driven confounding in radiomics and imaging foundation model features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28423)
*Katy L. Scott,Sejin Kim,Joshua Siraj,Caryn Geady,Matthew Boccalon,Mattea Welch,Mogtaba Alim,Andrew J. Hope,Benjamin Haibe-Kains*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出READII-2-ROQC，一个开源的影像组学与成像基础模型质量控制框架，通过体积保持的负对照（体素扰动图像）来评估影像特征是否捕捉独立的空间信号，并应用于三个公共癌症影像队列共3,552个肿瘤体积，发现多个模型在空间结构被破坏后仍保持性能，揭示了体积驱动或上下文混杂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影像组学和成像基础模型有望作为肿瘤生物学的非侵入性生物标志物，但预测性特征可能反映的是肿瘤体积或采集伪影，而非有意义的图像结构。需要一个系统性的质量控制框架来区分真实的影像空间信号与混杂因素。

Method: READII-2-ROQC框架生成跨肿瘤区域、背景区域和全图像的体素扰动图像，采用可配置的随机化策略（即体积保持的负对照），然后比较原始图像与对照图像之间的特征行为和模型性能。框架提取了PyRadiomics和基础模型特征，并对每个肿瘤体积生成九个匹配的对照。

Result: 在三个公共癌症影像队列中处理了3,552个肿瘤体积，复现了已发表的生存率和HPV状态预测特征。结果显示多个模型在空间结构被破坏后仍保持预测性能，表明存在体积驱动或上下文混杂；而另一些模型则显示出对扰动的敏感性（即真实的信号依赖）。

Conclusion: READII-2-ROQC提供了一种可扩展的质量控制策略，用于开发可解释的、具有生物学基础的影像生物标志物，并促进可复现的影像组学工作流程。

Abstract: Radiomics and imaging foundation models promise non-invasive biomarkers of tumour biology, yet predictive signatures may reflect tumour volume or acquisition artifacts rather than meaningful image structure. We introduce READII-2-ROQC, an open-source framework that uses volume-preserving negative controls to assess whether radiomic and deep imaging features capture independent spatial signals. READII-2-ROQC generates voxel-perturbed images across tumour, background and whole-image regions using configurable randomization strategies, then compares feature behaviour and model performance between original and control images. Applied to three public cancer imaging cohorts, the framework processed 3,552 tumour volumes and extracted PyRadiomics and foundation-model features from original images and nine matched controls. Reproducing published survival and HPV-status signatures, we show that multiple models retain performance after spatial structure is destroyed, revealing volume-driven or contextual confounding, whereas others show perturbation-sensitive signal. READII-2-ROQC provides a scalable quality-control strategy for developing interpretable, biologically grounded imaging biomarkers and reproducible radiomics workflows.

</details>


### [207] [ViewMind3D: Modular View-Aware Inference for Training-Free 3D-QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28442)
*Ping-Kun Chiang,Kun-Ru Wu,Po-han Li,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViewMind3D是一个完全无需训练的模块化框架，通过将3D问答任务分解为四个可解释组件（多视角选择、视觉定位、BEV空间编码和角色推理生成），利用通用LLMs和VLMs实现无需3D重建和无需微调的高效3D空间推理，在ScanQA和SQA3D基准上取得了与训练方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D问答方法大多依赖3D特定的训练或微调，需要昂贵的标注，限制了可扩展性和实际应用。作者希望利用通用LLMs和VLMs的能力，构建一个无需训练、无需完整3D重建的模块化框架，以提升3D空间推理的实用性和可解释性。

Method: ViewMind3D将3D-QA任务分解为四个可解释组件：(1)基于问题的多视角选择；(2)使用语言条件对象线索的引导式视觉定位；(3)通过鸟瞰图视角指示器进行空间上下文编码；(4)基于角色推理的结构化答案生成。整个框架无需任何模型微调，通过模块化编排通用LLMs和VLMs实现推理。

Result: 在ScanQA和SQA3D基准上，ViewMind3D取得了与之前的训练免方法以及微调3D-LLMs相当的性能。在SQA3D的空间接地问题类型（如"What"问题）上性能提升显著，总体准确率达50.8%，在ScanQA上CIDEr分数达73.4。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过模块化编排通用LLMs和VLMs可以有效实现3D空间推理，无需训练或微调即可在真实环境中的机器人感知任务中取得竞争性性能，为可扩展、可解释的3D问答提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) have enabled new possibilities for 3D question answering (3D-QA), a key capability for embodied AI and robotic perception. However, most existing methods rely on 3D-specific training or fine-tuning with costly annotations, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present \textbf{ViewMind3D}, a fully training-free and modular framework for 3D spatial reasoning over multi-view observations of a scene without requiring complete 3D reconstruction. The framework decomposes the 3D-QA task into four interpretable components: (1) question-driven multi-view selection, (2) guided visual grounding with language-conditioned object cues, (3) spatial context encoding via a bird's-eye-view (BEV) viewpoint indicator, and (4) structured answer generation through role-based reasoning. This design enables structured, robust, and interpretable reasoning without requiring model tuning. Experimental results on ScanQA and SQA3D show that ViewMind3D achieves competitive performance compared to prior training-free and fine-tuned 3D-LLMs. In particular, our method improves performance on spatially grounded question types, such as ``What'' questions in SQA3D, while maintaining strong overall accuracy (50.8\%) and achieving 73.4 CIDEr on ScanQA. These results demonstrate that effective 3D reasoning can be achieved through modular orchestration of general-purpose LLMs and VLMs for robotic perception in real-world environments.

</details>


### [208] [VisualRouter: Query-Grounded Visual Sampling for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28463)
*Haiyue Zhang,Yi Bin,Xun Jiang,Zeyu Ma,Duo Peng,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了VisualRouter，一个无需训练、即插即用的查询引导视觉采样框架，通过区分全局/局部查询并采用相应采样策略，在长视频理解中显著优于均匀采样和现有免训练采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在处理长视频时面临视觉token过多和上下文窗口有限的挑战。现有视觉采样方法要么依赖相关性感知采样导致帧选择冗余、时间覆盖不足，要么采用固定采样策略不考虑查询类型，缺乏针对不同查询类型自适应的采样方案。

Method: VisualRouter首先将查询分类为全局或局部两种类型，然后应用相应的采样策略。对于全局查询，采用相关性-覆盖率混合策略，在保留时间覆盖的同时保留查询相关的视觉证据；对于局部查询，采用事件感知的帧选择策略，包括事件分区、片段级帧分配和事件内帧选择三个步骤，在有限的输入帧数下联合平衡相关性、覆盖率和多样性。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU三个基准上，VisualRouter在Qwen2.5-VL-7B下相对于均匀采样分别获得5.2%、7.7%和11.6%的性能提升，并且在同一设置下超越了现有的免训练视觉采样方法。

Conclusion: VisualRouter通过查询引导的自适应采样策略，有效解决了长视频理解中视觉采样的冗余和覆盖不足问题，且无需训练即可即插即用地提升多种LVLMs的性能。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved significant progress in video understanding, yet understanding long videos remains challenging due to the large number of visual tokens and limited context windows. Visual sampling provides a practical solution by selecting an informative subset of frames. However, existing methods typically either rely on relevance-aware sampling, leading to redundant frame selection and insufficient temporal coverage, or adopt a fixed sampling strategy regardless of query type. In this paper, we propose VisualRouter, a training-free and plug-and-play framework for query-grounded visual sampling. VisualRouter first classifies each query as either global or local and then applies the corresponding sampling strategy. For global queries, it employs a relevance-coverage hybrid strategy that preserves temporal coverage while retaining query-relevant visual evidence. For local queries, it adopts an event-aware frame selection strategy that performs event partitioning, segment-level frame allocation, and intra-event frame selection, jointly balancing relevance, coverage, and diversity with a limited number of input frames. Experiments show that VisualRouter consistently improves multiple LVLMs over uniform sampling, achieving gains of 5.2%, 7.7%, and 11.6% on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU with Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and outperforming existing training-free visual sampling methods under the same setting.

</details>


### [209] [Can Vision-Language Models Reason about AI Edits in Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28464)
*Darsha Udayanga,Pin-Yu Chen,Payel Das,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GRPO强化学习框架训练视觉语言模型（VLM）以检测和定位AI篡改图像的方法。模型生成结构化推理轨迹预测图像是否被篡改，并由轻量级分割模型生成像素级定位掩码，无需显式推理监督即可取得与SOTA相当的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的图像篡改日益复杂，传统二元分类器缺乏可解释性和泛化能力。现有的VLM方法依赖监督微调和人工策展的解释，未能充分利用VLM固有的推理能力。本文旨在探索是否可以仅通过强化学习（而非显式推理监督）教会VLM推理AI生成图像编辑。

Method: 提出基于GRPO的训练框架：采用简单准确率和格式奖励，模型先生成结构化思考轨迹（reasoning trace），再预测图像是否被篡改；随后用轻量级分割模型基于推理输出生成像素级定位掩码。同时引入统一度量准有效交并比（eff-IoU）联合评估检测与定位性能。

Result: 在多个图像篡改数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检测和定位性能上与最先进的图像伪造检测器相当，尽管所需监督显著更弱。

Conclusion: 强化学习为教VLM推理AI生成内容提供了有效且可扩展的机制，展示了RL范式在图像取证领域的潜力。

Abstract: Detection and localization of AI-tampered images are critical for trustworthy AI, yet modern generative models have made such manipulations increasingly difficult to identify. While traditional binary classifiers can detect image tampering, they lack interpretability and generalization. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising alternative due to their strong visual understanding and reasoning capabilities; however, existing approaches typically rely on supervised finetuning with curated explanations rather than exploiting their inherent reasoning capabilities. In this work, we investigate whether VLMs can be trained to reason about AI-generated image edits using reinforcement learning (RL) rather than explicit reasoning supervision. Motivated by the success in Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), an RL technique that incentivizes the model to reason by generating thinking traces prior to giving the final answer, we propose a GRPO-based training framework that utilizes simple accuracy and format rewards. Given an input image, the model produces a structured reasoning trace and predicts whether the image has been tampered with. A lightweight segmentation model is then guided by the reasoning output to generate pixel-level localization masks. Experiments across multiple image manipulation datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves competitive detection and localization performance compared to state-of-the-art image forgery detectors, despite requiring substantially weaker supervision. We introduce effective intersection over union (eff-IoU), a unified metric to jointly evaluate detection and localization. These results suggest that reinforcement learning provides an effective and scalable mechanism for teaching VLMs to reason about AI-generated content.

</details>


### [210] [Towards Real-Time PixOOD: Efficient Anomaly Segmentation for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28483)
*Luca de Martino,Federico Aromolo,Federico Nesti,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效加速的实时异常分割pipeline，基于PixOOD方法进行了Neyman-Pearson评分阶段的重新设计，并通过TensorRT编译优化部署于桌面和嵌入式平台，在RTX 4060上达到182 FPS（20x加速），在Jetson AGX Orin上达到75 FPS（18x加速），适用于自动驾驶和铁路领域的车载实时处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时异常分割对自动驾驶系统的安全性至关重要，但现有高精度方法计算开销大，难以在嵌入式硬件上部署。需要一种兼顾精度与效率的加速方案，满足车载边缘计算的实时性需求。

Method: 重新设计了PixOOD（最先进的分布外检测方法）中的Neyman-Pearson评分阶段，并将整个处理流程通过硬件优化的TensorRT编译器进行部署，实现计算图优化和硬件加速。

Result: 在桌面级NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU上达到182 FPS，在嵌入式平台NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上达到75 FPS，分别比原始基线快20倍和18倍。

Conclusion: 研究证明先进的异常分割方法能够通过合理的算法重设计和硬件优化，实现自动驾驶与铁路应用中的高效车载实时处理。

Abstract: Real-time anomaly segmentation is essential for the safety of autonomous systems. Although recent approaches offer high accuracy, their computational cost limits their deployment on embedded hardware. This work presents an efficient and accelerated pipeline designed for both embedded and desktop platforms, targeting the autonomous driving and railway domains. The proposed approach reformulates the Neyman-Pearson scoring stage of PixOOD, a state-of-the-art out-of-distribution detection method, and deploys the full pipeline through hardware-optimized TensorRT compilation, reaching up to 182 FPS on a desktop NVIDIA RTX 4060 GPU and 75 FPS on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin embedded platform, respectively 20x and 18x faster than the original baseline. The achieved results demonstrate that advanced anomaly segmentation can be efficiently deployed for onboard processing in autonomous driving and railway applications.

</details>


### [211] [AuricularWorld: Hierarchical Action-Guided World Modeling for Fine-Grained Auricular Structure Segmentation from CT Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28487)
*Jingwen Yang,Senmao Wang,Luoyao Kang,Runmeng Cui,Keying Zhang,Yunjia Bao,Haifan Gong,Lin Lin,Haiyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于世界模型（world-model）的CT耳廓结构分割框架。该框架在编码器-解码器架构的中间潜空间中引入确定性循环状态空间模型，通过多尺度特征融合构建结构观测、迭代潜空间演进来逐步精化分割结果。实验表明，该方法在细小、不规则且重叠的耳廓结构上显著提升分割精度，HD95降低超过43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT中耳廓结构的精细分割极具挑战性：耳朵在图像中占据区域极小、软骨边界极不规则、软骨与周围软组织界面模糊，且临床标注存在嵌套重叠标签（复合结构及其对应软骨区域）。传统的前馈式分割模型依赖单次前向预测，缺乏迭代推理能力，难以在这些困难条件下进行逐步精化的解剖推理。

Method: 该方法构建了一个世界模型驱动的分割框架，具体包括：(1) 在编码器-解码器架构的中间潜空间中引入确定性循环状态空间模型（deterministic recurrent state-space model）；(2) 融合多尺度编码器特征与部分解码表示形成结构观测，用于初始化潜动态；(3) 推理时执行三步潜空间推进（latent rollout），通过层级解剖动作（hierarchical anatomical actions）逐步更新循环状态并精化潜表示；(4) 将潜轨迹投影回解码器并与高分辨率特征融合生成最终分割；(5) 提出平衡的层级动作目标函数，解决前景稀疏、解剖组缺失以及添加/删除操作不平衡等问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的框架在CT中细小、不规则且重叠的耳廓结构分割任务上持续提升分割精度，HD95降低超过43%，验证了潜空间世界模型推理在医学图像分割中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，通过在潜空间中引入世界模型式的迭代推理机制，可以有效克服传统前馈分割模型在极端困难解剖结构上的局限性，为挑战性医学图像分割提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of auricular structures in CT is challenging because the ear occupies a small image region, cartilage boundaries are highly irregular, and interfaces between cartilage and surrounding soft tissues are often ambiguous. Clinical annotations may also include both composite structures containing cartilage and adjacent skin and their corresponding cartilage-only regions, producing nested and overlapping labels. We propose a world-model-based segmentation framework that enables iterative anatomical reasoning beyond conventional feed-forward prediction. Built on an encoder-decoder architecture, the framework introduces a deterministic recurrent state-space model into the intermediate latent space. Multi-scale encoder features and partially decoded representations are fused to form a structural observation that initializes the latent dynamics. During inference, the model performs a three-step latent rollout without ground-truth guidance. Hierarchical anatomical actions update the recurrent state and progressively refine the latent representation. The resulting latent trajectory is projected back into the decoder and combined with high-resolution features to produce the final segmentation. To learn reliable latent transitions, we introduce a balanced hierarchical action objective that addresses foreground sparsity, missing anatomical groups, and imbalance between add and remove operations. Extensive experiments show that the proposed framework consistently improves segmentation accuracy and reduces HD95 by more than 43% for small, irregular, and overlapping auricular structures in CT. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of latent world-model reasoning for challenging medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [212] [RefCaptioner: Multi-Reference Image-Grounded Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28509)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Xiaohan Zhang,Yuqing Wen,Yuqi Tang,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Weihong Lin,Xinlei Yu,Yujie Wei,Xinwei Long,Fengxiang Wang,Xinlong Chen,Yue Ding,Jialu Chen,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个新任务——多参考图像接地视频描述（multi-reference image-grounded video captioning），并设计了两阶段后训练框架RefCaptioner，通过混合数据监督微调与层级覆盖折扣GRPO算法，同时提升参考图选择、短语级绑定、干扰项拒绝和跨参考一致性，实验表明其在开源模型中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述模型能生成自然语言描述，但无法将局部视觉元素显式地接地（ground）到多张参考图像上，这限制了描述的精确性和可追溯性。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: RefCaptioner采用两阶段后训练框架：(1) 混合数据监督微调（mixed-data SFT）保持通用视频描述能力；(2) 层级覆盖折扣GRPO（Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO）强化学习，联合优化参考图选择、短语级绑定、干扰项拒绝和跨参考一致性。训练语料包含2万视频和17.1万张参考图像。

Result: 在提出的MRVBench基准上，RefCaptioner在开源模型中取得最佳综合性能，同时在标准视频描述基准上保持竞争力；人工评估表明其描述更受标注者青睐，且能提升开源和闭源视频生成器的源一致性重建效果。

Conclusion: 多参考图像接地视频描述是可实现且有价值的新任务；RefCaptioner通过两阶段后训练有效提升了描述的准确性和可接地性，并具备跨模型的源忠实度增强能力。

Abstract: Existing video captioning models generate natural descriptions of video content but cannot explicitly ground local visual elements to multiple reference images. We introduce multi-reference image-grounded video captioning, a new task requiring factual video descriptions with phrase-level reference grounding, and propose RefCaptioner, a two-stage post-training framework for this task. RefCaptioner combines mixed-data SFT with Hierarchical Coverage-Discounted GRPO to jointly improve reference selection, phrase-level binding, distractor rejection, and cross-reference consistency while preserving general video-captioning ability. To support training, we construct a corpus containing $20,000$ videos and 171,354 reference images. We further introduce MRVBench, a benchmark for evaluating caption factuality and multi-reference grounding on both real-world and AI-generated videos. Experiments show that RefCaptioner achieves the best overall performance among the open-source models while remaining competitive on standard video captioning benchmarks. Human evaluation further confirms that its captions are preferred by annotators and enable more source-faithful video reconstruction with both open-source and proprietary video generators.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Frame Selection: Generative Latent Evidence Aggregation for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28516)
*Bowen Liu,Shuning Wang,Xinpeng Ding,Zhiheng Wu,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenEvA框架，在视频选择帧之后引入生成式潜在证据聚合阶段，通过查询条件的证据分布将跨帧互补线索整合为紧凑的潜在证据，并在不需要时自适应跳过该过程，显著提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法通常将视频压缩为少量帧或视觉token进行生成，但仅保留相关视觉内容作为显式证据并不足以确保跨时刻的互补线索被整合用于回答问题。即"证据可得"不等于"证据被整合"，需要一种机制在生成前将选定的帧组织为查询相关的跨帧证据。

Method: 提出GenEvA（生成式潜在证据聚合）框架，将由分布引导的潜在证据界面（latent evidence interface）置于后选择阶段（post-selection stage）。具体包括：1）使用查询条件证据分布（query-conditioned evidence distribution）聚焦聚合到相关帧；2）从帧特定信息形成紧凑的跨帧潜在证据；3）同一分布决定是否插入该潜在补充（自适应证据调用，Adaptive Evidence Invocation），因为并非所有情况下都需要跨帧整合。

Result: 在四个基准和两个Video-MLLM骨干上，GenEvA持续优于匹配帧数的基线。在8帧设置下，LLaVA-Video在四个基准上平均提升+5.2点，Qwen2.5-VL在LVBench上准确率提升+10.1点。这些增益仅需0.11%–0.40%的平均视频token开销。

Conclusion: GenEvA验证了"后选择阶段的潜在证据聚合"这一关键思想的有效性——在帧选择后、生成前显式组织跨帧证据能够以极小的token开销显著提升长视频理解性能；分析进一步表明其具备任务感知的分配能力，且自适应证据调用能带来额外收益。

Abstract: Long-video understanding commonly compresses videos into a small set of frames or visual tokens for answer generation. Existing compact pipelines focus on retaining relevant visual content as explicit evidence. Yet making evidence available does not ensure that complementary cues across moments are integrated for answering. Our key idea is to organize selected frames into query-relevant cross-frame evidence before generation. We formulate this post-selection stage as a latent evidence interface and instantiate it with GenEvA ($\textbf{Gen}erative$ $Latent$ $\textbf{Ev}idence$ $\textbf{A}ggregation$), a distribution-guided latent evidence aggregation framework. Specifically, GenEvA uses a query-conditioned evidence distribution to focus aggregation on relevant frames, forming compact cross-frame latent evidence from their frame-specific information. Since cross-frame integration is not always needed, the same distribution determines whether to insert this latent complement. Across four benchmarks and two Video-MLLM backbones, GenEvA consistently improves matched-frame baselines. At 8 frames, it raises the four-benchmark LLaVA-Video average by $+5.2$ points and Qwen2.5-VL accuracy on LVBench by $+10.1$ points. These gains require only $0.11\%$--$0.40\%$ average video-token overhead; analyses further show task-aware allocation and benefits from Adaptive Evidence Invocation.

</details>


### [214] [What to Remove, What to Preserve: Dual-Ambiguity Rectification for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28526)
*Cencen Liu,Wen Yin,Dongyang Zhang,Dongmin Li,Shan Zhao,Bing Su,Tao He,Jielei Wang,Guoming Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DAR-Net（双歧义修正网络），用于全合一图像复原任务。该网络通过退化原型表示（DAR）、语义歧义修正（SeAR）和空间歧义修正（SpAR）三个模块，解决了异构退化条件下共享潜空间中退化信息与场景内容纠缠导致的"语义歧义"和"空间歧义"问题，在多种退化基准上取得了最新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全合一图像复原方法将异构退化条件编码到共享潜空间中，导致退化相关线索与场景内容相互纠缠。作者将其特征化为"双重歧义"：通道调制层面的语义歧义（可能导致内容损坏）和复原响应层面的空间歧义（可能导致残余伪影），这两者严重制约了统一框架下的复原性能。

Method: DAR-Net包含三个核心模块：(1) 退化原型表示（DAR）模块，通过单纯形约束的原型混合建模构造结构化的退化状态；(2) 语义歧义修正（SeAR）模块，基于退化状态生成退化感知提示，改善解码器中的通道级条件化；(3) 空间歧义修正（SpAR）模块，将退化感知特征与互补特征正则化到正交响应子空间中，减少移除与保留线索之间的空间干扰。

Result: 在标准全合一图像复原基准上，DAR-Net在三种退化（three-degradation）和五种退化（five-degradation）设置下均取得最佳整体性能，相比最强竞争者平均PSNR分别提升0.14 dB和0.34 dB；在CDD-11和WeatherBench基准上也展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: DAR-Net通过显式地构造结构化退化表示并对双重歧义进行针对性修正，为全合一图像复原中退化信息与场景内容的解耦提供了一种有效方案，验证了退化原型建模与正交响应子空间正则化在统一复原框架中的价值。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to handle diverse degradations within a unified framework. Existing methods commonly encode heterogeneous degradation conditions in a shared latent space, where degradation-related cues and scene content can remain entangled. We characterize the resulting challenge as dual ambiguity: semantic ambiguity in channel-wise modulation and spatial ambiguity in restoration responses, which can lead to content corruption and residual artifacts. To mitigate this issue, we propose DAR-Net, a Dual-Ambiguity Rectification Network for all-in-one image restoration. DAR-Net first introduces a Degradation Archetype Representation (DAR) module to construct a structured degradation state through simplex-constrained archetype mixture modeling. Based on this state, a Semantic Ambiguity Rectification (SeAR) module generates degradation-aware prompts to improve channel-wise conditioning in the decoder. A Spatial Ambiguity Rectification (SpAR) module further regularizes degradation-aware and complementary features toward orthogonal response subspaces, reducing spatial interference between removal and preservation cues. Extensive experiments on standard all-in-one restoration benchmarks show that DAR-Net achieves the best overall performance under both three-degradation and five-degradation settings, improving the average PSNR over the strongest competitor by 0.14 dB and 0.34 dB, respectively; it additionally shows superior performance on CDD-11 and WeatherBench.

</details>


### [215] [MarkushGlyph and OCSRGlyph: Improved Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28532)
*Alex Andonian,Samuel G Rodriques,Andrew D White,Siddharth M Narayanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCSRGlyph和MarkushGlyph两个模型，将光学化学结构识别（OCSR）和Markush结构解析统一为图像到文本的翻译任务，并引入新的Markush结构翻译评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学结构在专利和科学文献中以图像形式出现，需要转换为线性符号以便程序化使用。虽然单个分子的OCSR（光学化学结构识别）已较为成熟，但Markush结构（表示一族分子的结构）解析仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 将OCSR和Markush结构解析统一视为图像到文本的翻译问题。提出OCSRGlyph（通过精细考虑立体化学提升性能的SOTA模型）和MarkushGlyph（将整个Markush结构作为图像输入的单阶段视觉-语言模型，而不同于先前多阶段分离处理视觉和文本输入的系统）。同时提出一个新的Markush结构翻译准确率评估指标。

Result: OCSRGlyph在单分子OCSR任务上达到最新技术水平（SOTA），优于先前方法。MarkushGlyph利用视觉-语言模型直接读取完整Markush结构图像。新指标能够处理先前指标中存在的失效模式。

Conclusion: 通过将两类化学结构识别任务统一为图像到文本翻译，并在立体化学处理、端到端视觉语言建模和评估指标三个方面进行创新，推动了Markush结构解析这一挑战性任务的发展。

Abstract: Chemical structures appear in patents and the scientific literature as images. For programmatic usage, such as indexing in databases or constructing machine learning model training sets, they must be transformed into line notations. The two common forms of this task are translating an image of a single molecule (optical chemical structure recognition - OCSR) and translating a Markush structure that represents a family of molecules. While prior work in the former case is quite mature, Markush structure parsing remains a challenging task. In this work, we treat both tasks as an image-to-text translation problem. We then propose OCSRGlyph, a state-of-the-art OCSR model, improving performance over prior methods by carefully considering stereochemistry. For the Markush task, we introduce MarkushGlyph, a vision-language model that reads the entire Markush structure as an image. This contrasts with prior systems, which often use multiple stages to separately process visual and text input content. Finally, we introduce a new metric for determining the accuracy of Markush structure translations, handling failure modes present in prior metrics.

</details>


### [216] [ScaFE: Data-Efficient Scar Classification with LLM-Generated Clinical Feature Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28538)
*Ruman Wang,Hangting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ScaFE框架，将大语言模型的临床知识转化为确定性、可执行的图像特征程序，用于病理性疤痕（瘢痕疙瘩与增生性疤痕）的本地化分类，在跨医院场景下以轻量级随机森林实现81.0%的站点宏平均平衡准确率，较最强基线BiomedCLIP高出10个百分点，同时满足数据治理与审计要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理性疤痕分类面临三重挑战：专家标注数据稀缺、医院间采集差异大、端到端图像模型过于依赖数据；而直接调用托管视觉语言模型（VLM）既可能与本地数据治理规范冲突，又难以复现和审计决策结果。因此需要一种既能利用大模型临床知识、又能本地化执行且可审计的分类方案。

Method: ScaFE的核心思路是将临床知识转移为程序而非直接图像推理：（1）通过联网LLM检索临床证据并合成度量视觉可评估疤痕属性的特征程序；（2）候选程序在受限的本地环境中执行，仅返回聚合验证统计量和特征级SHAP摘要用于迭代修复和精炼，原始图像与患者级输出保留在本地；（3）最终采用轻量级随机森林对结构化特征表示进行分类。

Result: 在来自三家医院的600张照片上，leave-one-site-out评估下ScaFE达到81.0%的站点宏平均平衡准确率，超过最强基线BiomedCLIP 10.0个百分点；仅用10%开发数据时仍保持72.0%平衡准确率并领先11.8个百分点。迭代精炼将可执行程序率从66.7%提升至95.0%，91.7%的最终特征有经核实的证据支持。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LLM知识可以通过本地化、可审计的特征程序实现数据高效、跨站点的医学图像分类，而非依赖直接VLM决策，为医学影像领域在数据治理约束下利用大模型知识提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Classifying pathological scars from clinical photographs requires distinguishing keloids from hypertrophic scars despite limited expert-labeled data and substantial acquisition variation across hospitals. End-to-end image models remain data-dependent, whereas sending photographs to a hosted vision-language model (VLM) may conflict with local data-governance requirements and yields decisions that are difficult to reproduce and audit. We introduce ScaFE (Scar Feature Engineering), which transfers clinical knowledge from a large language model (LLM) into deterministic, executable feature programs instead of asking the model to diagnose images. A web-enabled LLM retrieves clinical evidence and synthesizes programs that measure visually assessable scar attributes. Candidate programs execute in a restricted local environment, and only aggregate validation statistics and feature-level SHAP summaries are returned for iterative repair and refinement; raw images and patient-level outputs remain local. A lightweight Random Forest then operates on the resulting structured representation. On 600 photographs from three hospitals under leave-one-site-out evaluation, ScaFE achieves 81.0% site-macro balanced accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline, BiomedCLIP, by 10.0 percentage points. With only 10% of the development data, ScaFE retains 72.0% balanced accuracy and an 11.8-point lead. Iterative refinement also raises the executable-program rate from 66.7% to 95.0%, with verified evidence for 91.7% of the final features. These results show that LLM knowledge can support data-efficient, cross-site medical image classification through local and auditable feature programs rather than direct VLM decisions.

</details>


### [217] [MIND: Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers for Medical Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28565)
*Yunzhan Fu,Xiangyu Shen,Yifei Sun,Yuhan Chen,Jian Wu,Hongxia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIND，一种基于Diffusion Transformers的多模态意图驱动医学图像融合网络，利用BioMedGPT生成意图驱动文本引导融合，通过多尺度潜在适配器保留2D空间连续性，并通过医学语义一致性损失保证融合图像与诊断意图深度对齐，在多个数据集上取得优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像融合方法通常采用全局统一的融合规则，缺乏对诊断意图和病理结构的深层理解，导致融合结果与临床诊断需求脱节。

Method: (1) 利用BioMedGPT从源图像生成意图驱动的融合文本，以病理感知的诊断意图引导融合过程；(2) 设计多尺度潜在适配器（Multi-scale Latent Adapter），在序列化前显式提取源图像特征并通过严格维度对齐注入网络，弥补DiTs中1D序列展平导致的2D空间连续性损失；(3) 设计医学语义一致性损失（medical semantic consistency loss），实现融合图像与融合文本之间的深层语义锁定，同时保持底层物理流形重建的稳定性。

Result: 在Harvard、BraTS和GFP数据集上的综合实验表明，MIND在融合质量上优于现有方法，显著提升了下游脑肿瘤分割精度，并支持灵活的交互式融合。

Conclusion: MIND在意图驱动的智能临床决策支持系统中展现出重要潜力，为医学图像融合提供了融合诊断意图的新范式。

Abstract: Medical image fusion aims to integrate complementary information from diverse imaging modalities to support clinical diagnosis. Existing methods typically apply uniform fusion rules globally, lacking a deep understanding of diagnostic intents and pathological structures. To address these limitations, we propose MIND, a Multimodal Intent-Driven Network via Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) for medical image fusion. Specifically, we utilize BioMedGPT to generate intent-driven fusion texts from source images, guiding the fusion process with pathology-aware diagnostic intents. To combat the loss of 2D spatial continuity caused by 1D sequence flattening in DiTs, we design a Multi-scale Latent Adapter. This module explicitly extracts source image features before serialization, injecting them into the network via strict dimensional alignment to effectively supplement image features. To resolve the semantic shift caused by decoupling image outputs from diagnostic intents, we design a medical semantic consistency loss. This loss ensures deep semantic locking between fused images and fusion texts while maintaining the stability of the underlying physical manifold reconstruction. Comprehensive experiments on the Harvard, BraTS, and GFP datasets reveal that MIND delivers superior fusion quality, significantly improves downstream brain tumor segmentation accuracy, and enables flexible interactive fusion, holding significant promise for intent-driven intelligent clinical decision support systems.

</details>


### [218] [Finding Change in Satellite Archives from Text: How to Combine Before-and-After Images Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28571)
*Simon Roy,Mark Bong,Giovanni Beltrame*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了双时相卫星图像对检索中的融合模块设计，提出训练无关的两阶段搜索（差分校准+注意力重排）可降低10-15倍查询成本且不损失召回率；同时发现Mamba线性扫描在典型patch数量下受限于内存带宽而非注意力，且轻量压缩（TBF）虽减少参数和延迟但过度压缩会丢失变化相关细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行地球观测需要在大规模档案中检索符合自然语言变化描述的双时相卫星图像对，而融合模块在查询时需对大量候选项运行，其速度直接决定搜索成本。现有研究缺乏对融合模块设计的系统性、统计可靠的对比，需要严格评估不同方案的效率与效果。

Method: 使用固定CLIP图像编码器和统一训练方案，对比三大类共八种融合模块设计：注意力机制、状态空间模型（Mamba）以及学习压缩（Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF），在LEVIR-CC和Dubai-CC两个基准上使用十个随机种子进行统计检验。

Result: 三点主要发现：（1）训练无关的两阶段搜索（廉价差分模型短筛+注意力融合重排）在LEVIR-CC上匹配或超越全融合召回率，查询成本降低10-15倍，Dubai-CC上R@1/R@5相当；（2）Mamba线性扫描在视觉Transformer典型patch数（L=196）下无速度优势，受限于内存带宽，而注意力可良好映射到并行硬件；（3）TBF压缩融合表征减少2.3倍参数和1.6倍延迟，仅损失0.007的BLEU-1变化描述得分，但更激进的压缩会静默丢失变化相关细节。

Conclusion: 基于统计稳健的实验对比，推荐两阶段搜索作为实用的低成本方案；警示Mamba在视觉任务中的理论优势难以兑现；并指出压缩方法需关注超出聚合指标的细节损失。

Abstract: Operational Earth observation increasingly calls for answering queries such as ``find the image pairs where a new building appeared.'' This means searching an archive of before-and-after (bi-temporal) satellite image pairs and ranking each pair by how well it matches a natural-language description of the change. The component that performs this match, the fusion module that combines the ``before'' and ``after'' views, must be run at query time across many candidate pairs, so its speed largely sets the cost of every search. We present a controlled comparison of how to build that module. Using one fixed image encoder (a frozen CLIP model) and one training recipe for all variants, we evaluate eight designs drawn from three families: attention, state-space models (Mamba), and learned compression (our Temporal Bottleneck Fusion, TBF). Each design is tested on two benchmarks (LEVIR-CC and Dubai-CC) with ten random seeds, so the reported differences are statistically grounded. We outline three findings: first, a training-free two-stage search (a cheap difference model that shortlists candidates, followed by attention fusion that re-ranks them) matches or exceeds full-fusion recall on LEVIR-CC while cutting query cost $10$-$15\times$, with comparable R@1/R@5 on Dubai-CC; second, the linear-time scan of Mamba, attractive on paper, gives no speed benefit at the patch counts typical of vision transformers ($L{=}196$): the scan is limited by memory bandwidth, whereas attention maps cleanly onto parallel hardware; and third, compressing the fused representation (TBF) reduces parameters by $2.3\times$ and latency by $1.6\times$ for a change-only BLEU-1 cost of $0.007$, although more aggressive compression quietly discards change-relevant detail that aggregate metrics fail to reveal.

</details>


### [219] [ROAD: Reciprocal-Objective Alignment of Discriminative Semantics for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28581)
*Xiao Luo,Mingyang Du,Xin Zhou,Tianrui Feng,Xiwu Chen,Xiaofan Li,Jiangning Zhang,Dingkang Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ROAD框架，通过将判别式3D基础模型中的丰富先验知识迁移到扩散Transformer中，仅用工业基线Step1X-3D的1.5%训练数据即可实现具有竞争力的高保真3D生成性能，大幅度降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高保真3D生成严重依赖扩大模型容量和数据规模，计算成本极高，且通常需要从零学习几何结构，忽略了判别式3D基础模型中已蕴含的丰富语义和结构先验。作者认为利用这些判别模型对3D世界的深度理解可以显著降低生成成本。

Method: 提出ROAD框架，将判别式3D基础模型的先验知识迁移到扩散Transformer中。针对生成式与判别式潜在表示之间的语义-结构异质性，引入互惠目标对齐策略（reciprocal-objective alignment），包含两个核心组件：(1) 整体语义压缩（Holistic Semantic Condensing）用于确保全局语义一致性；(2) 结构最优对齐（Structural Optimal Alignment），将其建模为二部图匹配问题以严格对齐不同潜在空间之间的微观几何细节。3D基础模型仅在训练阶段用于对齐监督，推理时不参与，不增加推理成本。

Result: 与工业基线Step1X-3D相比，ROAD仅使用1.5%的训练数据即实现了高度竞争力的生成性能，显著降低了训练成本，有效减少了高保真3D生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过有效迁移判别式3D基础模型的先验知识到生成模型中，可以实现高保真3D生成的降本增效，为3D生成领域提供了一条不依赖大规模数据和算力堆叠的新路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D generation predominantly relies on scaling model capacity and data, which incurs prohibitive computational costs. This paradigm typically requires learning geometry from scratch and overlooks the rich semantic and structural priors already encapsulated in discriminative 3D foundation models. We contend that leveraging the profound understanding of the 3D world possessed by these discriminative models can significantly reduce generative cost. To this end, we propose ROAD, a framework that reduces the training cost of 3D generation by transferring these rich discriminative priors into diffusion transformers. To address the inherent semantic-structural heterogeneity between generative and discriminative latents, we introduce a reciprocal-objective alignment strategy. This method synergizes Holistic Semantic Condensing to enforce global semantic coherence and Structural Optimal Alignment, which is formulated as a bipartite matching problem to rigorously align microscopic geometric details between disparate latent spaces. The 3D foundation model is only used for training-time supervision of alignment and is not used at inference, incurring no additional inference cost. Compared with the industrial baseline Step1X-3D, the proposed ROAD achieves highly competitive generation performance with only 1.5% of the training data and significantly reduces training costs, effectively reducing the computational overhead of high-fidelity 3D generation. Code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/ROAD.

</details>


### [220] [MixFrag: Fragility-Guided Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28589)
*Md. Mehrab Hossain Opi,Robiul Islam Ryad,Md. Umar Faruk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixFrag是一个基于脆弱性引导的混合精度后训练量化（PTQ）框架，用于Vision Transformers。它通过KL散度估计组件级量化脆弱性，并将比特分配建模为多选背包问题（MCKP），在目标比特预算下实现自适应层间精度分配。在ImageNet-1K分类和COCO检测/分割任务上均取得优异性能，尤其在MP3/MP3设置下比之前最佳方法提升高达9.6 AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法对Transformer各组件统一使用相同比特宽度，忽略了不同组件对量化的敏感度差异，导致精度分配效率低下。因此需要一种能够根据组件脆弱性差异进行自适应混合精度分配的量化框架。

Method: 1) 通过计算全精度与隔离量化输出分布之间的KL散度，在小校准集上估计组件级量化脆弱性；2) 将比特分配问题形式化为多选背包问题（MCKP），在目标比特预算约束下实现自适应层间精度分配。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上多个ViT架构中取得有竞争力的分类性能；在COCO目标检测和实例分割任务上达到混合精度PTQ方法的最优性能，在MP3/MP3设置下较之前最佳方法提升高达9.6 AP；额外分析验证了脆弱性度量的有效性及其与学习到的比特分配之间的强相关性。

Conclusion: MixFrag被证明是一种有效的Vision Transformers混合精度后训练量化框架，其脆弱性引导的比特分配策略能显著提升PTQ的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has emerged as an effective solution for deploying Vision Transformers (ViTs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [221] [VAD: Attributing Visual Evidence for Target Reconstruction in Multimodal On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28590)
*Kangning Zhang,Yixing Li,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jianghao Lin,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉归因蒸馏（VAD），一种反事实目标重构算法，用于在多模态教师蒸馏中估计教师修正中由视觉证据支持的成分，并将其重构为学生锚定的训练目标，在六个细粒度视觉基准上优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过特权视图教师监督学生生成的轨迹来迁移细粒度视觉知识，但其下一个token修正混合了视觉信号、语言先验和教师特有影响。关键挑战在于估计哪些修正确实由视觉证据支持，而不仅仅是确定蒸馏的位置或强度。

Method: VAD的核心是反事实目标重构算法：在每个学生生成的前缀位置，用同一个固定教师在证据存在和移除两种条件下进行评估，计算居中log概率的变化作为视觉证据方向的带符号代理ut。然后将原始教师修正投影到该代理上，得到干预对齐分量和代理未解释的残差，仅用前者重构学生锚定的训练目标。训练时，重构目标提供主要监督信号，特权教师仅提供弱正则化。

Result: 在4B和9B两个规模的六个细粒度视觉基准上，VAD优于直接特权视图蒸馏和视觉优势加权方法。token级和受控目标分析表明，代理对齐分量富含任务相关的视觉修正，产生更强的目标偏移，尤其是在证据反驳错误答案时效果显著。

Conclusion: 反事实目标重构是源混合监督的有效替代方案，证明了通过反事实估计分离视觉证据贡献、重构训练目标这一技术路线的可行性。

Abstract: Multimodal on-policy distillation (OPD) transfers fine-grained visual knowledge by supervising student-generated trajectories with a privileged-view teacher. Yet its next-token corrections are source-mixed, combining visual signals with linguistic priors and teacher-specific effects. The key challenge is to estimate which corrections are supported by visual evidence, not merely where or how strongly to distill. We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [222] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视智能体视觉推理，从工具使用的两个关键维度（模式自适应性MA和工具效果TE）进行分析，发现现有模型在这两方面表现不佳：模式自适应性有限，且工具带来的收益被简单样本上的损失抵消。为此提出Beacon模型，通过必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导的能力扩展机制进行强化学习，在多个基准上实现了更强的整体性能、更高的模式自适应性和真实的工具增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体视觉推理的根本目标是提高多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在复杂任务上的成功率，而当前模型虽然配备了推理范式但效率低下且效果有限。现有模型的模式自适应性（能否识别何时真正需要工具）不足，同时工具使用在困难样本上带来的增益在很大程度上被在模型已能解决的简单样本上引入的误差所抵消，亟需重新思考工具使用的设计与优化。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，核心包括两个机制：Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward（必要性感知自适应奖励），根据任务必要性和实际情况鼓励工具的自适应调用，避免不必要的计算开销；Hint-Guided Capability Expansion（提示引导的能力扩展），在强化学习阶段强化模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力，从而真正拓展模型能力边界。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的广泛实验表明，Beacon实现了强于现有模型的整体性能，同时在模式自适应性（MA）和工具效果（TE）两个维度上均有显著提升，证明了自适应工具调用和工具能力扩展机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉推理的核心挑战不在于让模型更频繁地使用工具，而在于何时使用工具（模式自适应性）以及工具能否真正带来能力增益（工具效果）。Beacon通过奖励设计和能力扩展机制有效解决了这两个问题，为智能体视觉推理的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [223] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Chimera是一个混合视觉扩散骨干网络，通过结合KDA长上下文注意力、MLA全局交互和短卷积局部建模，配合HeteroP模块级缩放方案和Chinchilla-style计算最优定律，在11B参数（2B激活）规模下实现了优于全注意力基线的高效长上下文视觉生成，并支持零样本视频时长外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，而全注意力的二次方复杂度在计算上不可承受，亟需设计兼具线性复杂度、全局交互能力和良好可扩展性的高效架构。

Method: Chimera采用统一raster-ordered token流（无需位置嵌入），由三部分组成：Kimi Delta Attention（KDA，O(N)复杂度，负责长上下文状态追踪）、交错Multi-head Latent Attention（MLA，负责直接全局交互）以及模态感知短卷积（负责局部时空上下文）；稀疏MoE层扩展容量同时控制激活计算。同时提出HeteroP模块级超参数迁移方案，依据每个张量的功能扇入和模型深度在宽度与深度间传递超参数，由此拟合Chinchilla-style计算最优定律，指导模型规模、训练token数和图像-视频数据比例的分配。

Result: 三个主要实验结果：(1) 在预训练扩散损失上，稠密骨干相对全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线提升1.7倍计算效率，完整系统达7.3倍；(2) 无需长度微调，Chimera可从5秒训练片段零样本外推到30秒视频，最后5秒FID仅退化6.5%；(3) 拟合定律显示图像预训练的计算最优策略将近乎均匀分配计算给激活模型大小与训练token数，而视频预训练在高预算下略微偏向更大模型。

Conclusion: 工作为设计和扩展高效长上下文扩散架构奠定了根基，证明了混合架构配合系统化缩放定律可以在大幅降低计算复杂度的同时保持生成质量，并实现训练长度之外的零样本外推能力。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [224] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一种围绕"物理语言"（一种世界状态转换的紧凑离散表示）构建的物理世界模型。它采用"先推理后渲染"范式：先用物理语言序列推断未来世界演化，再将该序列渲染为视频，从而在视频生成与理解基准上展现物理一致的世界建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型直接在像素空间预测未来视频，将潜在的世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式的物理推理能力。人类能够从视觉经验中抽象出预测结构，并用自然语言进行显式推理，受此启发，作者希望构建一个能显式推理物理世界演化的世界模型。

Method: PhiZero包含两个核心组件：(1) 从野外视频中通过自监督方式学习"物理语言"——一种世界状态转换的紧凑离散表示；(2) 采用reason-then-render（先推理后渲染）范式，先用物理语言序列显式推断未来世界演化，再将推断出的状态转换渲染成视频。

Result: 在视频生成与理解基准上的大量实验验证了PhiZero建模物理一致世界演化的能力；同时展示了其在现实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟以及零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: PhiZero证明通过紧凑离散的物理语言进行显式推理，可以替代直接在像素空间预测的方式，构建出更具物理一致性、可解释且支持细粒度控制的物理世界模型，为世界建模开辟了类似自然语言结构化推理的新方向。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [225] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（Ambient Capture Engine），一个将真实家庭环境改造为空间校准、时间同步的"记录工作室"的数据引擎，用于解决具身智能面临的多模态数据瓶颈问题。基于ACE构建了ACE-Data-0数据集（150小时、1700万帧、5万参与者、200个任务类别、7.5万个交互片段），并引入层级化基准测试，揭示了现有SOTA方法在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度下的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈：模型需要捕捉第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉在人类追求目标过程中的协同演化，但现有数据集在视角、模态或空间尺度上割裂了这一经验，导致完整的感知-行动环路只能被部分观测。

Method: ACE以两种互补尺度运行：桌面尺度配置用于解析手-物操作，房间尺度配置用于捕捉全身运动、移动和家庭交互。系统记录第一人称和多视角外置视频、全身和关节手部运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频和触觉信号，形成统一的多感官流。数据采集采用目标级（而非逐步）指令以保留自然行为变异性。基准测试为层级化设计，从信号到场景组件再到交互。

Result: 构建了ACE-Data-0数据集：150小时、1700万帧视频、50名参与者在2个环境中完成200个任务类别共75000个交互片段，涵盖原子操作、长时家庭活动链和人-场景交互。基于此数据集对SOTA方法的评估显示，在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间时间跨度下存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供了对齐的感知、运动学和接触监督的同步人类演示数据，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-动作系统和具身AI提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [226] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken通过训练单个可学习的检索目标嵌入，从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择与查询相关的稀疏视觉token，从而解决长视觉上下文中视觉语言模型性能退化及GPU内存受限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉语言模型构成挑战：随着干扰物数量增加模型性能退化，且一次性处理所有token在GPU内存限制下计算不可行。需要一种高效的选择机制来提取查询相关的关键视觉信息，同时避免处理全部token带来的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出ReToken方法：训练一个可学习的嵌入作为显式检索目标，用于从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择稀疏的、与查询相关的视觉token子集。该方法仅需在小规模图像问答数据集上训练。

Result: 在图像和视频基准上均获得一致提升：在Visual Haystacks上，Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4分，InternVL3.5提升12.4分（相对提升超过20%）；在LVBench上零样本迁移至长视频场景，Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0分提升。轻量化设计使训练和长视频推理均能在单张H100上完成。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量级的可学习检索嵌入实现了高效的视觉token稀疏选择，在长视觉上下文任务中显著提升性能，且训练成本很低，具备良好的计算可行性和跨模态（图像到视频）迁移能力。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [FAVA: Formal Authorization for Verified Agents with Evidence-Backed Permission Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27267)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FAVA是一个面向LLM智能体的形式化授权框架，通过LLM引导的权限中间表示将自然语言任务转化为结构化约束，再经确定性降级为证据背书的权限图，最终由SMT求解器对安全策略进行数学验证，在效果动作执行前授权或拦截。在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane场景中达到90.5%的决策合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在动态环境中自主交替进行语义推理和复杂系统操作，静态的工具级权限不足以保障安全，因为安全授权高度依赖上下文和不断演化的运行时状态与数据流。

Method: FAVA采用三层架构：(1) LLM引导的权限中间表示(IR)将模糊的自然语言任务翻译为结构化约束；(2) 确定性降级(lowering pass)将IR转换为显式追踪数据流、依赖关系和上下文标签的证据背书权限图；(3) SMT授权器对当前图与安全策略进行数学验证，运行时网关强制执行求解结果，授权或拦截并给出精确反例。

Result: 在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane场景中，FAVA在聚合数据集上达到90.5%的决策合规率(DCR)，并成功在trace条件下拦截动态违规轨迹。

Conclusion: FAVA通过形式化方法（SMT求解）为LLM智能体提供了可验证的动态授权机制，证明上下文相关的安全授权可以通过结构化约束和数学验证来实现，弥补了静态权限控制的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents autonomously interleave semantic reasoning with complex system operations. In these dynamic environments, static tool-level permissions are fundamentally insufficient; safe authorization is highly context-dependent and heavily reliant on evolving runtime states and data flows. We present FAVA (Formal Authorization for Verified Agents), a permission-carrying authorization framework for agent execution. FAVA utilizes an LLM-guided Permission Intermediate Representation (IR) to translate ambiguous natural-language tasks into structured constraints. A deterministic lowering pass then converts this IR into an evidence-backed permission graph that explicitly tracks data flows, dependencies, and contextual labels. To provide strict security guarantees, a Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) authorizer mathematically verifies the current graph against security policies before any effectful action executes. A runtime gateway then enforces the solver's result, either authorizing the execution or intercepting it with a precise counterexample. We evaluate FAVA across OpenAgentSafety, OctoBench, and ActPlane scenarios. Our evaluation demonstrates that FAVA achieves a 90.5% Decision Compliance Rate (DCR) over the aggregate dataset, successfully intercepting dynamic violating traces in the evaluated trace-conditioned scenarios.

</details>


### [228] [Open Security Benchmark: Towards Autonomous Enterprise Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27288)
*Gal Engelberg,Michael Arenzon,Leon Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Open Security Benchmark (OSB)框架，用于在真实企业级私有环境中端到端评估自主网络安全防御中的智能体AI。OSB通过冻结的企业安全状态快照和封闭式ground truth，在文本转SQL和厂商原生API两种模式下评测安全态势调查能力，填补了企业环境数据缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业正转向自主网络防御（agentic AI构建态势感知并推理决策），但现有研究无法回答"智能体是否值得被信任"这一问题。真实企业环境是私有、跨厂商且高度关联的，没有任何一个公开共享、可查询的目标环境可供端到端评估这类智能体。这一"环境数据缺口"阻碍了该领域的可信度验证。

Method: OSB框架由五大组件构成：(1)数据层——合成组织环境数据集，涵盖多规模；(2)任务与评估集层；(3)多维评分层；(4)最小可审计的测试夹具(harness)；(5)自带路径(BYOP)——同一基座上同时支撑公共对比评测和私有租户评估。框架冻结企业环境为不可变快照，锚定答案至封闭式ground truth，并通过两种评测模态（关系快照上的文本转SQL、同一环境的厂商原生API）评估态势调查。

Result: 作者实例化了两个身份安全评测包和一个跨多规模的合成组织环境数据集家族，并规划了向更多态势子域、调查模态和防御阶段（从评估到修复）的扩展路径。

Conclusion: OSB为自主网络防御智能体提供首个可共享、可查询、基准化的企业级评估框架，以填补环境数据缺口，为可信AI驱动的安全态势管理建立评测基础。

Abstract: Enterprises are moving toward autonomous cyber defense: agentic AI that builds situational awareness of an organization's security state and reasons from it to assessments, decisions, and actions. This rests on a holistic view of the enterprise's security state, the continuous, cross-vendor picture of identities, cloud and infrastructure, data, applications, and their configurations that security posture management assembles. As agents take on this work, what matters is not whether an agent can produce an answer but whether it should be trusted to. The field cannot yet answer this question. Real enterprise environments are private, cross-vendor, and deeply correlated, and none is exposed publicly as a shared, queryable target for evaluating such agents end to end. We call this the environment data gap. We present Open Security Benchmark (OSB), a framework that benchmarks agentic AI on this work. OSB surfaces a curated enterprise environment - a frozen, holistic view of the security state - and evaluates posture investigation across two modalities: text-to-SQL over a relational snapshot and each vendor's native API over a served instance of the same environment. Freezing the environment pins the target state as an immutable snapshot and anchors answers to a closed-form ground truth. OSB is built from five components: a data layer, a task and evaluation-set layer, a multi-dimensional scoring layer, a minimal auditable harness, and a bring-your-own path that serves public comparison and private tenant evaluation from one substrate. We instantiate the framework with two identity-security packs and a family of synthetic-organization environment datasets spanning multiple scales, and chart its extension to further posture subdomains, investigation modalities, and defense stages from assessment toward remediation.

</details>


### [229] [RoguePrompt: Dual-Layer Encoding for Self-Reconstruction to Circumvent LLM Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27373)
*Benyamin Tafreshian,Prathamesh Dhake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoguePrompt，一种通过两级嵌套编码（Vigenere+ROT13）和自然语言重建指令来绕过LLM安全机制的越狱攻击流水线，在黑盒威胁模型下对313个真实被拒提示进行测试，实现了93.93%的过滤器绕过率、79.02%的重建率和70.18%的执行率，并提供了阶段级的失败证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的越狱攻击研究通常将"可见接受、隐藏请求成功恢复、后续执行"合并为一个聚合的攻击成功指标，缺乏对多阶段提示转换攻击在可观察的黑盒交互中具体在哪一阶段失败的细粒度证据。作者希望通过阶段级度量来揭示越狱攻击的失败位置。

Method: RoguePrompt流水线将被禁止的提示进行分割，并应用两层嵌套编码——先Vigenere后ROT13——再配合自然语言重建指令。该流水线在黑盒威胁模型下开发与评估，仅通过API或用户界面访问托管模型。评估使用313个真实且被硬拒绝的提示，成功度量分为三个独立阶段：审核绕过、指令重建和执行，各阶段超过自动化判定标准即视为成功。

Result: RoguePrompt在三个阶段分别实现了93.93%的过滤器绕过率、79.02%的指令重建率和70.18%的执行率，提供了阶段级证据表明多层提示编码有效，并揭示了越狱攻击在审核绕过、指令重建和执行各环节的具体失败情况。

Conclusion: 分层提示编码越狱方法具有显著有效性，但通过阶段级分解可以发现，攻击的瓶颈存在于从"绕过审核"到"重建指令"再到"实际执行"的逐步衰减过程中，这为理解多阶段越狱攻击的失败机制和防御策略提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly integrated into mainstream development platforms and daily technological workflows, typically behind moderation and safety controls. Despite these controls, preventing prompt-based policy evasion remains challenging, and adversaries continue to "jailbreak" LLMs by crafting prompts that circumvent implemented safety mechanisms. Prior work has established cipher-mediated interaction, code-embedded decryption, prompt decomposition and reconstruction, and layered custom encryption as viable attack primitives. However, reported evaluations generally collapse visible acceptance, successful recovery of the concealed request, and subsequent execution into an aggregate attack-success outcome. This leaves limited evidence about where multistage prompt-transformation attacks fail within an observable black-box interaction. This paper introduces RoguePrompt, a jailbreak pipeline that partitions a forbidden prompt and applies two nested encodings, Vigenere followed by ROT13, along with natural-language reconstruction instructions. RoguePrompt was developed and evaluated under a black-box threat model, with only API or user-interface access to the hosted models, and was tested on 313 real-world, hard-rejected prompts. Success was measured in terms of moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution when the relevant stage exceeded its automated criterion. RoguePrompt achieved average rates of 93.93% for filter bypass, 79.02% for reconstruction, and 70.18% for execution. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of layered prompt encoding while providing stage-level evidence of where multistage jailbreaks fail during moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution.

</details>


### [230] [Low-Latency Bootstrapping for CKKS using Roots of Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27401)
*Jean-Sebastien Coron,Robin Koestler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRU（Sparse Roots of Unity）自举算法，一种针对CKKS同态加密方案的新自举方法。与原始CKKS自举依赖多项式近似模约减不同，SPRU直接将模q的加法群嵌入复单位根中，在CKKS中原生求值，显著降低乘法深度，允许使用更小的环维数并提高效率。在OpenFHE C++库中，对于少量槽位的密文，SPRU自举可实现最高5倍的时延降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CKKS自举方法需要同态估计一个逼近mod q的模约减多项式，计算代价高且乘法深度大。作者希望找到一种更高效的自举方法，减少乘法深度并支持更小的环维数，从而提升整体性能。

Method: SPRU自举的核心思想是直接将模q的加法群嵌入复单位根（roots of unity）中，利用CKKS方案原生支持复数运算的特性来求值该嵌入。这种方式避免了显式逼近模约减多项式，从而降低了乘法深度。

Result: 在使用OpenFHE C++库的实现中，SPRU自举在处理少量槽位的密文时，相比原始CKKS自举可减少高达5倍的时延（latency）。同时，由于乘法深度的降低，可以使用更小的环维数，进一步提升效率。

Conclusion: SPRU自举提供了一种新的自举思路，通过单位根嵌入替代多项式逼近模约减，显著改善了CKKS自举的效率，特别适用于小型槽位密文场景。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse Roots of Unity (SPRU) bootstrapping, a new bootstrapping algorithm for the CKKS homomorphic encryption scheme for approximate arithmetic. The original CKKS bootstrapping method relies on homomorphically evaluating a polynomial that approximates modular reduction modulo q. In contrast, SPRU bootstrapping directly embeds the additive group modulo q into the complex roots of unity, which can be evaluated natively in the CKKS scheme. This approach significantly reduces the multiplicative depth required for bootstrapping, enabling the use of a smaller ring dimension and improving efficiency. In practice, using the OpenFHE C++ library, SPRU bootstrapping achieves up to a 5x reduction in latency when applied to ciphertexts with a small number of slots.

</details>


### [231] [Granite: A Modular Methodology for Foundational Verification of Hardware-Software Leakage Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27480)
*Stella Lau,Andres Erbsen,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Granite是一种用于RTL处理器模块化验证的方法论，同时验证功能正确性和非泄漏性，通过泄漏感知精化（leakage-aware refinement）将ISA级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行联系起来，最终通过可组合的证明和静态分析推导出硬件-软件密码实现逐周期保密性的单一定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器时序侧信道（如逐周期时序）可能泄漏秘密信息，而现有的验证方法往往只关注功能正确性，或在指令集级与微架构级之间缺乏模块化、可组合的连接。需要一种方法论，能够同时保证功能正确性和非泄漏性，并支持规范在不同层级间的可组合证明。

Method: Granite的核心技术是"泄漏感知精化"（leakage-aware refinement via determinism），将正确性和保密性统一建立为对一族逐周期、确定性规范机器的迹等价关系。秘密无关的不确定性通过将规范存在性地参数化为仅作用于公开数据的不可信确定性函数来处理。子模块各自证明其泄漏感知规范，这些证明组合成全设计的保证，从而覆盖一个安全实现空间。

Result: 作者证明了流水线RISC设计（含推测执行、精确中断和I/O）的逐周期时序仅由ISA泄漏契约中规定的可观察量决定。对于遵循密码学常数时间准则的程序，该结果排除了通过已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。该工作据称是首个实现指令集级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行（含线级观测）之间模块化、基础性连接的工作。

Conclusion: Granite建立了ISA级泄漏契约与微架构级执行之间的模块化基础连接，通过泄漏感知精化同时保证功能正确性和非泄漏性。其证明与认证的静态分析组合，可推导出关于硬件-软件密码实现逐周期保密性的单一Rocq定理，将所有中间规范（包括ISA契约本身）从可信计算基中消除。

Abstract: Granite is a methodology for modular verification of both functional correctness and nonleakage of RTL processors against ISA contracts. We prove that the cycle-by-cycle timing of a pipelined RISC design--with speculation, precise interrupts, and I/O--is determined solely by observables specified in an ISA leakage contract. For programs that keep observables independent of secrets (i.e., following the cryptographic-constant-time discipline), this result rules out information leakage through known and unknown timing side channels.
  Granite's specifications only constrain functional correctness and information-flow dependencies: not how many cycles an instruction takes, at which instruction an interrupt is handled, or the exact latencies of submodules such as multipliers and memory. Granite's central technique is leakage-aware refinement via determinism, which establishes correctness and confidentiality together as trace equivalence with respect to a family of cycle-level, deterministic spec machines. Secret-independent nondeterminism is handled by existentially parameterizing specs with untrusted, deterministic functions acting only on public data. Submodules are proved against their own leakage-aware specs, and these proofs compose into the whole-design guarantee--which therefore holds over a space of secure implementations.
  We believe this work is the first to achieve modular and foundational connection between instruction-set-level leakage contracts and microarchitecture-specific cycle-by-cycle execution with wire-level observations. Our proofs compose with a certified static analysis that recognizes cryptographic-constant-time code to derive a single Rocq theorem about the cycle-by-cycle confidentiality of a hardware-and-software cryptographic implementation--eliminating every intermediate specification, including the ISA contract itself, from the trusted computing base.

</details>


### [232] [Flock: Fast Proving for Batch Boolean Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27491)
*Benedikt Bünz,Ron D. Rothblum,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flock是一个基于哈希的SNARK系统，用于极速证明大批量标准密码学哈希函数（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）的布尔计算。它在M4 Max单核上每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销仅为原生执行的250倍以内；在十核上每秒超过660k次BLAKE3压缩，比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多SNARK应用的关键瓶颈在于证明大批量标准密码学哈希函数求值（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）。现有的SNARK方案（包括基于椭圆曲线的方案和Binius64等）在此场景下速度不够快，需要一种专门针对批量布尔计算优化的高效SNARK系统。

Method: Flock结合了lincheck和zerocheck协议的新优化，并采用与编码智能体共同设计的激进优化的概念验证实现。它针对同一R1CS电路（以及输入/输出关系）的批量证明进行优化，支持哈希链和Merkle路径开放证明，原则上可扩展为完整的基于哈希的签名验证。

Result: 在M4 Max处理器单核上，Flock每秒证明82k次BLAKE3压缩函数、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销低于原生执行的250倍；十核上吞吐量超过660k次BLAKE3压缩/秒。在SHA-256证明中，Flock比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。

Conclusion: Flock展示了通过针对批量哈希计算的专门优化（lincheck/zerocheck协议优化及激进实现）可大幅提升SNARK证明效率，为SNARK在哈希密集应用中的实际部署提供了极具竞争力的方案。

Abstract: For many applications of SNARKs, a key bottleneck is proving large batches of standard cryptographic hash evaluations, such as SHA-256, Keccak, or BLAKE3. We introduce Flock, a hash-based SNARK for extremely fast proving of such batched Boolean computations. Flock proves batches of the same R1CS circuit (plus input/output relations between them), can prove hash-chains and Merkle path openings, and in principle can be extended to full-fledged hash-based signature verification. At its core, Flock combines new optimizations for the lincheck and zerocheck protocols with an aggressively optimized proof-of-concept implementation co-designed by coding agents.
  On a single core of an M4 Max processor, Flock proves 82k evaluations of the BLAKE3 compression function, 42k SHA-256 compressions, and 30k Keccak permutations per second --- less than a $250\times$ overhead over native execution. On ten cores, throughput exceeds 660k BLAKE3 compressions per second; in proving SHA-256, Flock is more than $9\times$ faster than Binius64, the prior state of the art, and more than $500\times$ faster than the fastest elliptic curve-based SNARK we measured against.

</details>


### [233] [Send and Pretend: Exploiting Transcript Consistency Issues in End-to-End Encrypted Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27510)
*Gabriel K. Gegenhuber,Moritz Grefner,Maximilian Günther,Matthäus Wininger,David Schmidt,Aljosha Judmayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示主流端到端加密（E2EE）即时通讯应用（如Threema、WhatsApp、Signal和iMessage）在群聊中均无法保证"对话一致性"（Transcript Consistency, TC），恶意群成员可通过选择性省略、重排序或篡改内容向不同收件人投递不一致的对话视图，且不会触发任何用户界面警告。作者在恶意参与者威胁模型下系统研究了该问题的范围，识别出多种"歧义向量"（evalocation vectors），涵盖协议回退路径和群内成对投递信道，并展示了具体攻击场景（如社会工程、规避审核、操纵投票），还发现了与隐私相关的实现特定行为。最后提出了实用的低开销缓解措施和UI信号策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着E2EE即时通讯应用在群聊中引入投票功能（如Signal和iMessage近期新增），用户隐含期望所有参与者看到一致的对话结果和视图。然而这一"对话一致性"属性在主流E2EE协议中并未得到保障。作者旨在系统性揭露这一安全缺口，因为既有的E2EE安全叙事（强调端到端加密）可能给用户造成虚假的安全感，尤其是在敏感协调场景（如政治决策者的群聊）中，后果可能十分严重。

Method: 作者采用恶意参与者威胁模型（malicious-participant threat model），针对群聊中共享对话的完整性及跨链接设备的不一致投递进行系统性分析。方法包括：(1) 审查主流E2EE协议（Threema、WhatsApp、Signal、iMessage）的协议实现与回退路径；(2) 识别多个"歧义向量"，涵盖协议降级/回退机制以及群组内故意使用成对传递通道的情况；(3) 构建具体利用场景（社会工程、规避审核、操纵投票）进行实证；(4) 发现实现特定行为（如设备操作系统指纹识别）带来的隐私问题；(5) 基于先前对话一致性研究成果进行对比与归因，并提出低开销的缓解措施和UI信号策略。

Result: 研究结果表明目前所有主流E2EE群聊应用都不提供任何形式的对话一致性保证。具体发现包括：存在多个可被利用的歧义向量，从协议回退路径到群内成对投递通道；成功演示了包括操纵投票在内的具体攻击场景；发现了额外的隐私泄露行为（设备OS指纹识别）；这些问题跨越多个服务，属于系统性的设计缺陷而非单一实现的遗漏。

Conclusion: 作者认为当前主流E2EE群聊协议在对话一致性方面存在根本性缺陷，需要系统性修复。他们提出了实用且低开销的缓解措施和UI信号策略，可集成到现有的E2EE群组协议中，以缩小恶意参与者可操纵对话视图的空间，并最终为群聊用户提供与其安全预期相符的一致性保障。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted (E2EE) messaging apps are widely praised for their security and thus also used for sensitive coordination in group chats (e.g., by political decision makers). After Threema and WhatsApp, also Signal and iMessage have recently introduced polls to aid agreement processes in groups. This implicitly sets the expectation that all participants see the same outcome and thus have the same view of the conversation. This property is commonly referred to as transcript consistency (TC).
