In this work, we demonstrate that today's major E2EE messengers do not guarantee any form of TC for group chats, allowing a malicious group member to selectively omit, reorder, or present altered content to different recipients without triggering warnings in their user interface. We systematically investigate the extent of the problem under a malicious-participant threat model that targets the integrity of the shared transcript, or inconsistent delivery across a user's linked devices. We identify multiple equivocation vectors that range from protocol fallback paths to deliberate use of pairwise delivery channels within groups. We demonstrate concrete exploitation scenarios such as social engineering, evading moderation, and, in particular, rigging polls. Beyond these cross-service design issues, we also uncover implementation-specific behaviors with privacy implications (e.g., device OS fingerprinting). Finally, we contextualize our findings within prior transcript-consistency research and outline practical low-overhead mitigations and UI signaling strategies that can be integrated into state-of-the-art E2EE group protocols.

</details>


### [234] [ThreatForest: Multi-Agent Attack Tree Generation with Pluggable TTP Framework Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27528)
*Cristian Leo,Anton Dykyi,Danny Cortegaca,Daniel Begimher,Prakash Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatForest是一个多智能体系统，可从源代码仓库自动生成结构化攻击树，将攻击步骤映射到TTP框架（MITRE ATT&CK、CAPEC、云特定威胁矩阵），并综合可操作的缓解措施。实验表明嵌入阶段（而非多智能体管线本身）是主要精度瓶颈，系统在威胁陈述、攻击树和缓解措施上的质量评分为0.63-0.68，但TTP映射仅0.29。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁建模对安全软件开发至关重要，但云原生架构的人工分析速度慢且依赖稀缺的安全专家资源。现有方法缺乏自动化端到端的威胁建模系统，需要一种能够自动从代码库生成威胁模型并映射到标准对抗框架的方法。

Method: ThreatForest采用多阶段多智能体管线：仓库分析、上下文精炼、威胁生成、并行攻击树构建（含TTP映射和缓解措施综合）以及报告生成。整个流程以有向图编排，具有确定性验证门、有界重试和三个人类在环验证点。TTP映射使用领域特定的sentence-transformer通过余弦相似度匹配候选技术。

Result: 在七个应用领域上使用16维度量标准评估，由独立LLM评分者面板配合对抗性验证和专家审阅。威胁陈述、攻击树和缓解措施的质量评分为0.63-0.68，但仅嵌入TTP映射为0.29。受控的单次调用基线使映射可辩护性翻倍以上，证明瓶颈在嵌入编码器而非多智能体设计。

Conclusion: ThreatForest是首个端到端系统，可将代码库转化为跨对抗框架的TTP映射攻击树并附带基于证据的缓解措施。系统同时提供了一个可复用的基准评测框架，并精确隔离了当前TTP映射的瓶颈——嵌入模型本身，为后续改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Threat modeling is essential for secure software development, yet manual analysis of cloud-native architectures is slow and demands scarce security expertise. We present ThreatForest, a multi-agent system that generates structured attack trees from source code repositories, maps attack steps to adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from a pluggable set of frameworks (MITRE ATT&CK, CAPEC, and cloud-specific threat matrices), and synthesizes actionable mitigations. ThreatForest decomposes threat modeling into a multi-stage agent pipeline -- repository analysis, context refinement, threat generation, parallel attack-tree construction with TTP mapping and mitigation synthesis, and report generation -- orchestrated as a directed graph with deterministic verification gates, bounded retries, and three human-in-the-loop validation points. A domain-specific sentence-transformer maps each attack step to candidate techniques by cosine similarity; we show empirically that this embedding stage, not the surrounding pipeline, is the dominant accuracy bottleneck. We evaluate ThreatForest across seven application domains on a sixteen-dimension rubric, scored by a panel of independent LLM raters with an adversarial verification pass and expert review. Panel-measured quality reaches 0.63-0.68 (on a 0-1 scale) for threat statements, attack trees, and mitigations, but only 0.29 for embedding-only TTP mapping -- a gap stable across all seven domains that isolates the binding constraint. A controlled single-call baseline on the same model more than doubles mapping defensibility, pinning the limitation on the embedding encoder rather than the multi-agent design. To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [235] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AnchorMark是一种无需训练、鲁棒的基于反演（inversion-based）的水印方法。它利用发现的潜在空间特性"Rotation Synchrony"（图像域旋转与恢复的初始潜变量中的旋转角度一致），在初始潜变量的中心区域嵌入同步锚点，在提取时准确估计并校正旋转角度，从而显著提升旋转及复合攻击下的比特准确率，同时对图像质量影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于反演的水印方法在复合有损后处理下仍然脆弱，尤其是涉及旋转攻击时，旋转会破坏潜空间解码所需的空域对应关系，导致水印提取失败。因此需要一种能够抵抗旋转及复合攻击的鲁棒水印方案。

Method: 论文揭示了一个称为"Rotation Synchrony"的潜在空间性质：图像域的旋转角度与恢复的初始潜变量中对应的旋转角度相同。基于这一性质，AnchorMark在初始潜变量的中心区域嵌入一个同步锚点（synchronization anchor），在提取阶段利用该锚点准确估计旋转角度并进行校正，从而恢复了潜空间解码所需的空域对应关系。该方法无需训练，直接在生成过程中嵌入水印载荷。

Result: 实验表明，AnchorMark在旋转以及旋转与其他攻击的组合攻击下，显著提升了比特准确率，同时对合成图像质量的影响有限。

Conclusion: AnchorMark通过利用Rotation Synchrony性质在潜变量中心嵌入同步锚点，有效地解决了基于反演的水印方法在旋转及复合攻击下鲁棒性不足的问题，实现了无需训练的高鲁棒水印嵌入。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [236] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了图神经网络交通流量预测的对抗鲁棒性研究，指出现有攻击与防御大多基于不现实的威胁模型。作者提出了一种物理感知的定点攻击（可精确定位特定路段造成拥堵）以及一种物理感知的检测-缓解防御框架，实验表明该防御在15组模型-数据集设置中的13组上优于对抗训练，且几乎零清洁损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前针对交通预测的对抗攻击研究使用了不切实际的威胁模型和无目标的目标函数，无法反映真实场景。实际攻击者可仅操纵少量传感器，将攻击定位到特定路段或路线，造成局部交通中断而整体网络影响极小。这种定向攻击尚未被充分研究，且基于范数约束训练的传统对抗防御无法泛化到物理感知的结构性攻击。

Method: 将鲁棒性问题重构为检测问题：训练一个物理感知的检测器，其输出作为额外输入特征喂给加固后的预测器（forecaster），在预测器参数固定的前提下针对自适应攻击进行对抗训练。攻击方面提出了物理感知攻击，模拟真实拥塞模式而非范数扰动。

Result: 物理感知攻击可将目标链路误差放大数倍而全网误差几乎不变；传统的基于范数扰动的对抗训练对这类攻击几乎无效；所提出的检测-缓解防御在15个模型-数据集组合中的13个上优于甚至针对物理感知攻击本身强化过的对抗训练，在未见过的攻击上表现最好，且清洁数据代价接近零。

Conclusion: 交通预测的对抗安全研究必须在应用特定的约束条件（物理可行性、局部性、攻击者能力）下评估，抽象的攻击和防御框架可能高估或低估真实安全影响。检测-缓解范式比传统对抗训练更适合此类物理感知的定向攻击场景。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [237] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SubCASP方法，一种基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的变电站网络攻击策略阶段识别方法，通过融合IDS数据日志来推断当前攻击阶段、下一攻击阶段及回溯攻击路径，以弥补现有IDS仅能标记孤立症状而缺乏攻击阶段上下文的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符合IEC 61850的数字变电站提高了电力系统运行效率，但攻击者可以滥用IEC 61850通信操纵断路器操作，造成严重的系统影响。这些网络攻击基于多阶段策略构建，而现有入侵检测系统（IDS）往往只能标记孤立的症状，缺乏攻击阶段上下文，无法支持缓解措施的部署。

Method: SubCASP采用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）方法，融合多个IDS数据日志来推断当前攻击阶段、预测下一攻击阶段并重建回溯攻击路径。攻击阶段基于ATT&CK框架的威胁建模推导得出。模型在可复现的攻击图数据集上进行训练和评估。

Result: 测试结果展示了SubCASP在不同IDS可观测性水平和缺失IDS数据日志场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubCASP通过HMM融合IDS日志，能够为变电站网络攻击提供阶段感知的态势理解，提升多阶段攻击检测的上下文完整性，进而支持更有效的缓解措施部署。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [238] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了分布式点函数（DPF）的定义、构造与应用，并讨论了其推广形式——函数秘密共享（FSS），涵盖从私密信息检索到伪随机相关生成器等广泛场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF作为轻量级密码学原语，已支撑多种重要应用（如私密信息检索、匿名消息传递、预处理安全计算等），但缺乏系统性的综述性整理，有必要对相关定义、构造及应用进行梳理。

Method: 采用综述方法，系统整理DPF的定义形式、各类构造方案及其在多种应用场景中的部署方式，并进一步讨论DPF向FSS的推广及其对应用的泛化影响。

Result: 展示了DPF在多个密码学应用中的核心地位，并说明高效的FSS方案能够将DPF的大部分应用推广到更丰富的函数类。

Conclusion: DPF是兼具轻量级结构和广泛适用性的重要密码学原语，其向FSS的扩展进一步提升了表达能力和应用覆盖面。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [239] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARGE框架，利用CWE条目和大型语言模型（LLM），为未经验证的RTL模块自动生成安全属性（security properties）。该方法利用CWE的层级结构推理，识别安全关键资产，推断预期安全行为并生成属性，无需可信设计规格。在Hack@DAC18/19/21上检测出42个已知bug中的27个；在OpenPiton SoC上89%生成的SVA可运行，92.2%非空泛（non-vacuous），并通过生成属性发现了一个先前未识别的新bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未验证的RTL（寄存器传输级）模块缺乏安全性验证手段。传统方法依赖可信设计规格或大量手动工程来编写安全属性，成本高且易出错。本文希望利用CWE（通用弱点枚举）的层级结构和LLM来自动生成安全属性，减少手动工作并提高安全属性生成的准确性。

Method: CHARGE是一个自动化框架，核心是利用CWE条目的层级结构特性进行推理，以提高在未验证RTL模块中识别安全关键资产的准确性。从识别出的资产和CWE语义中推断预期安全行为，进而生成形式化安全属性（SystemVerilog Assertions，SVAs），全程无需可信设计规格。实验使用OpenAI的GPT-4.1作为LLM后端。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18、19和21的开源SoC设计上，CHARGE检测出42个已知bug中的27个。对于Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC，89%的生成SVA能在Cadence JasperGold FPV中运行，92.2%为非空泛断言。与手动编写的开源属性集相比，CHARGE正确编写了3个手动属性写错/不正确的bug的属性。此外，CHARGE生成的属性还发现了OpenPiton SoC中一个此前未被识别的新bug。

Conclusion: CHARGE展示了利用LLM和CWE层级结构自动生成RTL安全属性的可行性，在无需可信设计规格的前提下，能够以较高的准确率和非空泛率生成有效安全属性，不仅覆盖了大多数已知漏洞，还能发现人工未发现的新缺陷，显著减少手动安全验证工作量。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出NANOEDGEGUARD，一种面向医疗物联网边缘网关的内核平面闭环速率控制机制。通过内核流量控制钩子实施多级可审计策略，利用迟滞实现快速恢复，相比用户态防火墙基线将99分位告警RTT降低13.3%，超量突发流量减少46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网服务依赖边缘网关中继遥测数据和传递时间敏感的告警。即便是短暂的流量突发也可能放大最坏情况延迟并干扰紧急消息，而静态防火墙规则或用户态控制环无法提供快速的执行与显式恢复能力。

Method: 设计NANOEDGEGUARD内核平面闭环控制器：在边缘观察每源流量强度，通过内核流量控制钩子强制实施可审计的多级速率策略；采用迟滞机制实现快速执行与显式恢复；记录策略转换以供审计。实验使用Raspberry Pi网关托管MQTT代理，连接两个ESP32端点产生生命体征、告警及定时突发流量。

Result: 相比用户态防火墙基线，自适应内核平面速率控制将99分位告警RTT降低13.3%，且保持与无强制水平相当的RTT；与无强制相比，被接纳的超量突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 早期结果表明，在网关进行自适应、可审计的强制控制能够提升医疗物联网的弹性，未来可扩展至按需策略部署的边缘智能场景。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [241] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
*Jin-Seong Kim,Han-Ju Lee,Seok-Won Hong,Takeshi Takahashi,Chansu Han,Tomohiro Morikawa,Seok-Hwan Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GradLock是一种新型训练时注入攻击方法，通过无状态确定性索引建立隔离数据保险库，并利用动态梯度锁定防止载荷退化，使攻击者能够从最终模型中提取像素级完美的私有训练数据，且无需保留对训练环境的访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演（MI）攻击主要依赖训练后优化从模型输出中恢复私有数据，但这些方法受目标模型泛化瓶颈的限制，通常只能恢复通用特征而非特定身份，尤其是在高维数据集上效果不佳。作者旨在解决这一根本性局限。

Method: GradLock在模型训练阶段（而非训练后）进行注入攻击：1）在受损供应链环境中操作；2）利用无状态确定性索引建立隔离的数据保险库；3）采用动态梯度锁定机制防止载荷在优化过程中退化。攻击者可以在不保留训练环境访问权限的情况下，从最终模型中提取像素级完美的数据。

Result: 在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA数据集上的实验表明：GradLock实现了近乎无损的重建（SSIM约1.0）和即时提取（<1.0秒）；相比现有训练时注入方法，在量化、剪枝和微调等标准部署优化下具有更强的鲁棒性；用户部署研究显示93.3%的参与者未能检测到恶意逻辑。

Conclusion: GradLock揭示了现代AI供应链中严重的盲点：训练时注入攻击可以在不引起注意的情况下窃取敏感训练数据，且难以被标准防御措施和人类检测所发现。这凸显了AI供应链安全亟需更有效的防护机制。

Abstract: Existing model inversion (MI) attacks predominantly rely on post-training optimization to recover private data from model outputs. However, these methods are fundamentally constrained by the target model's generalization bottleneck, often yielding generic features rather than specific identities, particularly on high-dimensional datasets. In this paper, we introduce GradLock, a novel training-time injection attack that stealthily injects sensitive training data directly into the model parameters. Operating within a compromised supply chain context, GradLock leverages stateless deterministic indexing to establish isolated data vaults and employs dynamic gradient locking to prevent payload degradation during the optimization process. This mechanism allows the adversary to extract pixel-perfect data from the final model without retaining access to the training environment. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Imagenette, and CelebA demonstrate that GradLock achieves near-lossless reconstruction (SSIM ~ 1.0) and instant extraction (< 1.0s). Compared to existing training-time injection methods, our approach exhibits superior robustness against standard deployment optimizations, including quantization, pruning, and fine-tuning. Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [242] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双生成器学习的干净标签"遗忘激活型后门"框架，通过双层优化联合学习建立潜在触发器-目标关联的样本特定触发器与提供可移除抑制的标签一致伪装样本。在训练后保持休眠（低攻击成功率），仅当用户请求遗忘小部分伪装样本后才被激活（高攻击成功率），在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上优于现有后门基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击在植入后立即生效，容易被发现和防御。利用机器遗忘激活的休眠后门可以避免行为暴露，但现有方法难以在干净标签约束和现实遗忘请求下，同时实现低植入前攻击成功率和强植入后激活效果。关键在于需要同时建立持久的潜在关联和可移除的抑制影响。

Method: 提出基于双生成器学习的干净标签遗忘激活后门框架，将问题建模为双层优化问题：通过模拟潜在后门建立和机器遗忘过程，交替学习(a)样本特定触发器以建立潜在触发器到目标的关联，以及(b)标签一致的伪装样本以提供可移除的抑制。当小部分伪装样本被遗忘时，抑制被解除，休眠后门被激活。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上的实验表明，该方法在多种遗忘算法下，相比代表性后门基线，能在保持更低的前遗忘攻击成功率的同时实现更强的后遗忘激活效果。

Conclusion: 通过协调持久的潜在关联与可移除的抑制，可以在干净标签和现实删除约束下实现可靠的"休眠到激活"转换，验证了该方法在机器遗忘场景下的后门攻击有效性。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [243] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出大型语言模型（LLM）的安全防护机制在回答之前无法获知答案的下游用途，导致双重用途任务（dual-use tasks）中同一答案既可能帮助合法专业人员也可能帮助攻击者。作者将模型释放的能力与下游使用的证据分离，推导出可复制证据条件下的"攻击者协助下限"（worst-case floor），提出安全三难困境：有用能力、可靠安全与开放访问三者不可兼得。并提出可信凭证（trusted credential）机制作为补充，通过引入难以复制的信息来预测实际下游用途，从而消除该下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全防护机制在生成答案前无法预见其用途，这是双重用途任务的根本问题：恶意攻击者可以模仿良性请求和交互历史来绕过安全机制。需要在理论上精确刻画这一局限，并探索可行的缓解方案。

Method: 将模型释放的能力（capability）与下游使用的证据（evidence）分离建模。当证据可复制时，推导出攻击者协助的最坏情况下限的精确表达式。在此基础上提出通过可信凭证引入难以复制的信息来预测实际下游用途，并识别消除该下限所需的更强条件。

Result: 推导出可复制证据下的攻击者协助下限，得出安全三难困境（有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问无法共存）；论证了可信凭证机制如何补充现有安全防护以降低该下限，并明确了彻底消除下限所需的更强条件。通过双重用途评估、自适应攻击和已部署的可信访问项目验证了这些条件的实际相关性。

Conclusion: LLM安全防护存在根本性的信息不对称问题，仅靠"先验证后回答"的现有范式无法同时保证有用性、安全性与开放性。可信凭证等额外机制可以改善但无法完全解决这一问题，提示需要在安全设计中投入对实际用途的可靠预测能力。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [244] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次将海绵攻击（sponge attack）引入原生事件驱动的脉冲神经网络（SNN），证明了这类攻击可通过增加推理时的脉冲活动和突触运算量（SynOps）来膨胀能耗，且难以通过正确性监控检测。作者提出了两种攻击模型：逐样本攻击（通过梯度优化为每个输入生成自定义对抗脉冲序列，可在NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture三个数据集上将每次推理的SynOps提高1.5-2.6倍，同时保持至少98%样本的预测类别不变）和通用海绵攻击（离线计算固定二进制扰动，通过XOR应用于所有后续输入，SynOps膨胀1.09-1.24倍，无需逐输入优化，更贴近实际部署威胁）。映射到Loihi-1能耗后，每次推理的额外能耗从14μJ到13.24mJ不等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN通过稀疏二值脉冲事件通信，在神经形态硬件上实现高能效推理，因此被广泛用于常开、电池供电的边缘系统。然而，这种能效优势也带来了新的安全风险：海绵攻击可以通过增加推理时的脉冲活动和突触工作量来膨胀能耗，而传统的基于正确性的监控无法轻易检测到这种攻击。此前对SNN的输入空间效率攻击主要集中在率编码（rate-coded）设置下的逐样本优化，缺乏对原生事件驱动二进制输入的攻击研究，更缺乏通用性攻击方法的探索。

Method: 作者提出了两种攻击模型：（1）逐样本海绵攻击：对每个输入通过基于梯度的优化生成自定义对抗脉冲序列，以最大化脉冲活动和SynOps；（2）通用海绵攻击：首次针对原生事件驱动SNN输入提出，离线计算一个固定的二进制扰动，在部署时通过XOR操作应用于所有后续输入。两种攻击都在三个SNN模型（对应NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture数据集）上进行了评估，并将SynOps膨胀量映射到Loihi-1芯片的能耗估算。

Result: 逐样本攻击在三个数据集上使每次推理的SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍，同时保持至少98%样本的预测类别。通用攻击虽然效果较弱，但仍在三个数据集上实现了1.09-1.24倍的SynOps膨胀。映射到Loihi-1能耗后，每次推理的额外能耗为14μJ至13.24mJ，表明即使是通用扰动也能在持续部署的边缘系统中累积成可观的电池消耗。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动的SNN在输入空间中确实容易受到实用的效率攻击（海绵攻击）。通用可复用扰动虽强度较弱，但无需逐输入优化，代表了一种更现实且可累积的部署威胁，能够在持续运行的边缘系统中造成有意义的电池损耗。这一发现凸显了SNN能效优势背后的安全代价，需要进一步关注SNN系统的安全性设计。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [245] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并评估了针对脉冲神经网络（SNN）的干净标签时间后门攻击。该方法仅对目标类训练流施加固定时间戳变换，保持标签不变且不改变逐像素逐极性的事件总数，使从时间聚合角度看干净样本与触发样本完全相同，但SNN处理的事件序列已被改变。在三个神经形态数据集和两种架构（卷积与Transformer）上最强配置达到100%攻击成功率，并提出了基于逐时间步事件质量的免模型检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN后门攻击主要假设脏标签投毒（需重标触发样本到攻击者选定类别），这与真实场景中攻击者无法任意改标签的现实不符。干净标签攻击是更实际的威胁模型，但此前在SNN和神经形态事件数据上尚未被研究过。

Method: 攻击方法：对目标类别训练样本施加固定的时间戳变换（时间扰动），保持逐像素逐极性事件计数不变，仅改变事件序列的时间顺序，标签不变。评估：在三个神经形态数据集上对卷积和Transformer两类受害者模型进行攻击；通过投毒预算和触发器形状消融分析攻击特性；将既有后门防御方法适配到脉冲模型进行评估；提出基于逐时间步事件质量的免模型检测器。

Result: 最强配置下攻击成功率（ASR）达到1.00（100%）。时间轴折叠类防御因构造原因对攻击盲视；特征空间类防御仅在特定设置下能检测到投毒；所提出的基于逐时间步事件质量的免模型检测器能够检测所评估的时间变换。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个针对SNN和神经形态事件数据的干净标签后门攻击研究，揭示了时间维度的脆弱性，同时展示了既有速率折叠防御的局限性和攻击隐身性的边界。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [246] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析物联网云平台（AWS IoT Core）访问控制策略的方法，通过形式化建模和信息流图来识别设备间不必要的信息流动所导致的安全漏洞，并实现了工具IOT:POKER进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务商提供的IoT访问控制机制虽然关键，但仅单独验证权限配置不足以保证安全。在设备具有不同信任级别或被划分到不同子系统的场景下，需要分析设备间潜在的不合意信息流来发现安全漏洞。

Method: 形式化建模AWS IoT Core的组件，定义信息流图来刻画访问控制策略所允许的设备间通信。利用SMT求解器构建图的有限表示，从而实现对设备间信息流的验证，并实现为工具IOT:POKER。

Result: 在现实场景和多个真实世界策略上对IOT:POKER进行了评估，验证了该方法能够有效识别访问控制策略中的信息流安全漏洞。

Conclusion: 通过形式化建模和SMT求解，该工作的信息流图方法能够有效验证IoT多云环境中访问控制策略的安全性，为检测设备间非预期信息流动提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [247] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，研究自主多智能体系统（AMAS）中推理时harness提取的安全风险。AHD通过黑盒交互两阶段提取目标智能体的推理时harness能力，实验证明存在严重IP泄露风险，并提出基于欺骗的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的自主多智能体系统（如Hermes）依赖推理时harness来协调推理与行动，这些harness在组合搜索空间中迭代优化并与底层LLM共同演化，构成宝贵知识产权。现有研究仅关注静态多智能体系统的IP泄露，而AMAS中harness行为在推理期间动态涌现，其安全风险尚未被研究。

Method: AHD框架包含两个阶段：(1) 预蒸馏阶段（pre-distillation）：通过黑盒交互从目标智能体响应中推断推理时harness行为，构建初始harness；(2) 后蒸馏阶段（post-distillation）：迭代优化初始harness使其与目标智能体的行为模式对齐。此外提出基于欺骗的防御策略。

Result: 在多个主干LLM上的真实AMAS实验中，AHD展示了有效性，揭示了显著的IP泄露风险；欺骗式防御在减少harness提取效果的同时保持受保护智能体的实用性。

Conclusion: 揭示了AMAS中此前未被充分探索的安全威胁——推理时harness提取导致的IP泄露，并为该问题提供了评估框架与防御方案。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [248] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击，提出了指令增强和场景隐蔽技术使恶意音频指令可隐蔽搭载于用户语音之上，构建了首个音频指令注入攻击基准AudioAgentSecurity（涵盖8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式），并提出了基于源分离与一致性分析的级联音频解耦验证防御机制（CADV），在11个先进智能体上验证了攻击效果（对Gemini 3 Pro平均攻击成功率69.10%）和防御性能（检测成功率超90%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多模态智能体越来越多地通过连续音频交互执行自主任务，但音频输入不可避免地包含用户无法控制的环境噪声，这一特性引入了尚未被充分探索的攻击面。现有音频攻击研究主要针对传统语音设备，缺少针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 提出指令增强（instruction augmentation）和场景隐蔽（scenario concealment）两种新技术，使恶意音频指令可检测地"搭载"在用户语音上，实现攻击隐蔽性；2) 构建AudioAgentSecurity基准，覆盖8个真实任务场景和10种攻击模式；3) 提出CADV（Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification）防御机制，基于声源分离和跨模态一致性分析检测音频指令注入。

Result: 在11个先进多模态智能体（含Gemini 3 Pro和GPT-4o-audio）上评估，攻击方法对Gemini 3 Pro的平均攻击成功率达69.10%；CADV防御在多种攻击向量下检测成功率超过90%；在豆包AI智能手机上的真实场景实验中确认了攻击的高隐蔽性和有效性，同时验证了防御的可靠性。

Conclusion: 并发音频提示注入攻击对多模态智能体构成严重且隐蔽的安全威胁，现有提示级防御不足以应对；基于声学源分离和跨模态一致性分析的CADV防御机制能够更稳健地抵御此类攻击，为多模态智能体的音频安全提供了可行的防护方向。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [249] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向临床EEG数据的隐私保护联邦学习框架，以基于掩码的安全聚合为核心，结合图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗丢失聚合、局部更新裁剪、可选布隆滤波器隐私保护记录链接和辅助公证人可验证性，支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置，并在模拟跨机构医疗场景中验证了其与联邦训练兼容但引入额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多家机构在不交换原始临床EEG数据的情况下训练共享模型，但个体模型更新仍可能泄露隐私信息，因此需要额外的隐私保护机制来防止隐私泄露。

Method: 采用基于掩码的安全聚合作为核心保护机制，集成图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗丢失聚合、局部更新裁剪、可选的布隆滤波器隐私保护记录链接初始化模块，以及辅助公证人可验证性机制；支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置，基于Flower联邦学习框架实现。

Result: 在基于TUH EEG数据模拟的跨机构医疗场景中，多种客户端配置下安全变体能够隐藏个体更新不被聚合服务器看到，且与联邦模型训练兼容；但恶意设置的保护机制和轻量级一致性检查会引入额外的计算、通信和回合时长开销，其中半诚实变体开销最低，恶意和辅助公证人变体提供更强的一致性、完整性和轻量验证支持但成本更高。

Conclusion: 在既定假设下，所提出的安全聚合变体在保护临床EEG数据隐私的同时保持与联邦训练的兼容性，半诚实变体在安全配置中开销最低，恶意和公证人变体以更高成本换取更强的安全属性，为跨机构EEG数据协作提供了一套可选的隐私保护联邦学习方案。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [250] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了具身AI世界模型的安全威胁，从数据构建、表征学习、状态接地、想象模拟、轨迹评估、执行及长期记忆/工具适应等全生命周期角度审视攻击面，提出安全生命周期分类法，并强调世界模型作为安全盾牌与其被攻陷后产生"预测性安全幻觉"的双重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供了预测核心（压缩观测、模拟行动条件化未来、实现超越反应式控制的规划），但这一预测层开辟了新的安全边界——妥协可从数据、传感器、提示或反馈传播到物理动作。现有安全研究多将世界模型视为孤立组件，缺乏对其全生命周期威胁的系统性审视。

Method: 作者采用全生命周期方法论，追踪威胁从数据构建和表征学习，经状态接地和想象，到轨迹评估、执行及通过记忆和工具进行长期适应的完整链条。通过将熟悉攻击家族（投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击）映射到世界状态、学习动力学、可布性估计或安全成本等世界模型关键组件的损毁场景，构建了生命周期安全分类法，并设计了评估协议和分层防御框架。

Result: 论文建立了世界模型安全生命周期分类法，将现有攻击映射到世界模型安全属性，概述了安全失败的评估协议，并结构化了跨六个维度的防御体系：来源追溯、稳健接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保证。同时揭示了世界模型的"预测性安全幻觉"风险——当模型被攻陷或被过度信任时会生成虚假的安全感。

Conclusion: 世界模型既是具身AI安全的增强器（可作为运行时安全盾牌），也是新的攻击面（被攻陷后产生预测性安全幻觉）。安全分析必须跨越世界模型的整个生命周期而非孤立看待单个组件，未来的安全保障需要兼顾来自数据到执行的全链条威胁防护，以及对模型预测本身可靠性的审慎评估。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [251] [Implementing Homomorphic Encryption-Based Logic Locking in System-on-Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28542)
*Ye Ziyang,Makoto Ikeda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二元环学习误差（Binary Ring-LWE）算法的逻辑锁定方案，并应用于RISC-V片上系统设计中。该方法在特权切换过程中保护关键逻辑路径，且无需暴露原始锁定参数。锁定模块消耗3519个查找表和2645个寄存器，整体开销为6.0%（LUT）和6.9%（寄存器），解锁耗时约2.6微秒，主要影响系统级操作而保持用户级计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑锁定方法需要向用户提供原始锁定参数，存在敏感参数暴露的安全风险。作者旨在研究一种既能保障芯片知识产权安全、又能避免暴露关键参数的逻辑锁定方案。

Method: 采用基于二元Ring Learning With Errors（Binary RLWE）密码算法的逻辑锁定方法，将其实现为RISC-V SoC中的锁定模块，重点保护特权切换过程中的关键逻辑路径。该方案无需向用户提供原始锁定参数，通过密码学机制实现安全解锁。

Result: 锁定模块占用3519个查找表（LUT）和2645个寄存器，相对基线系统的总开销分别为6.0%（LUT）和6.9%（寄存器）；解锁过程耗时约2.6微秒。

Conclusion: 该方案在实现安全逻辑锁定的同时，产生了适度的硬件开销和性能影响，解锁过程主要影响系统级操作，用户级计算效率得以保持，体现了安全性、代价与性能之间的良好权衡。

Abstract: This study presents a logic locking scheme based on the binary Ring Learning With Errors algorithm, implemented in a RISC-V System-on-Chip design. Unlike traditional logic locking methods that require providing users with raw locking parameters, the proposed approach secures critical logic paths in the privilege switching process without exposing these sensitive parameters. The implemented locking module itself consumes 3519 Look-Up Tables and 2645 Registers, leading to an overall overhead of 6.0% in Look-Up Tables and 6.9% in Registers compared to the baseline system. The unlock process requires about 2.6 us, introducing moderate performance impact, primarily affecting system-level operations while preserving user-level computational efficiency.

</details>


### [252] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文章探讨了证明助手在系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译和密码学领域的应用，主要动机是通过形式化验证确保设计与实现满足预期的安全属性，并支持认证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手常用于验证设计与实现是否满足预期的安全属性，同时支持系统认证，因此在系统安全等多个关键领域中具有重要价值。

Method: 本章聚焦于证明助手在四个具体方向的应用：系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译和密码学。

Result: 本章系统性地阐述了证明助手在上述四个安全相关领域中的应用现状与实践。

Conclusion: 证明助手不仅用于安全属性的验证，还在认证（certification）过程中发挥支持作用，覆盖系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学等多个关键领域。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究固定表示神经网络在合并数据池化重拟合时，是否能保留各数据源共同支持的偏好排序（即CBDT组合公理），提出了可量化的几何失配度量、几何导向正则化方法及三阶段审计框架，并在多个决策代理任务上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的池化训练虽能改善泛化，但可能破坏源数据支持的偏好顺序。现有方法缺乏对此类「组合可靠性」的反转风险进行定量度量和系统审计的工具。

Method: 1）推导了OLS输出头的池化重拟合如何重算逆Gram几何从而可能反转共享偏好的精确/近似保持条件；2) 提出尺度不变的Gram失配度量用于候选池筛选，以及几何导向正则化用于训练中塑造源几何；3) 开发三阶段审计，将严格成对反转追踪至任务定义的效用损失。

Result: 实验（含载量投标代理以及医疗、金融决策代理）显示存在稳定与易反转的池化体制；载量审计在代理效用下识别出可测的非零类源共识相对的有害决策；跨域审计表明可比的失配可对应截然不同的保持率；几何导向目标占据不同的描述精度-一致性-几何-危害工作点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛选、解析认证、几何导向训练与决策后果审计，使组合可靠性变得可量化且可操作。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [254] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对固定部署解在等向高斯环境动态下的预期生存时间进行了严格的理论分析。通过将生存建模为离散首出问题，推导出下界和可计算的多步上界，证明了预期生存时间在慢变环境中按Θ(σ⁻²)增长，在高维环境中趋近最小值1。蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测，并展示了界如何支持优化后的部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化随时间(ROOT)作为进化动态优化的分支，强调解的持续性而非传统TMO范式的每次环境变化后重新优化。尽管领域发展迅速，但大多数贡献停留在算法和实证层面，核心理论性质如生存时间(解在未来环境中继续满足质量阈值的预期环境数)与动态特性、部署质量和问题特征的关系尚不明确。本文旨在从理论上填补这一空白。

Method: 将解在环境序列中的生存建模为离散首出问题，在等向高斯环境动态假设下推导预期生存时间的严格下界和可计算的多步上界；通过分析揭示预期生存时间随环境变化方差σ²的标度关系(Θ(σ⁻²))及高维极限行为；并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论预测、检验模型假设和参数不确定性敏感性。

Result: 推导出预期生存时间的下界与多步上界；证明预期生存时间在慢变环境下按Θ(σ⁻²)缩放，在高维空间中趋近最小值1(即仅存活到下一次环境变化)；蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测，并展示了上下界在部署决策中的应用价值。

Conclusion: 本文提供了部署生命周期的解析刻画框架，能够明确判定所需部署时间范围何时可被保证、何时可被排除、以及何时在分析上仍无法确定。这一理论框架为ROOT领域奠定了更坚实的分析基础。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [255] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上保序回归（Isotonic Regression）的最坏情况自由度给出了有限样本的完全尖锐刻画，确定了使拟合值数量最大化的二元序列，并利用解析数论得到了领先项为 (3/(4π²)^{1/3})·n^{2/3} 的锐界。此外，将该结果应用于校准问题，首次推导出保序回归期望校准误差（ECE）的分布无关保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保序回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但现有自由度界不够尖锐。同时，校准是概率预测的核心需求，而保序回归作为广泛使用的后处理校准方法，此前缺乏非平凡的分布无关期望校准误差（ECE）保证。

Method: 利用解析数论工具对二元样本上保序回归拟合值的数量进行精确分析，刻画最大化自由度（即不同拟合值数量）的二元序列，从而得到尖锐的有限样本界；基于该确定性自由度界，进一步推导出期望校准误差的分布无关上界。

Result: 得到了保序回归在二元样本上最坏情况自由度的完全尖锐有限样本刻画，其界领先项为 (3/(4π²)^{1/3})·n^{2/3}，优于先前结果；并基于此首次获得保序回归 ECE 的、完全模型无关和分布无关的非平凡保证，仅假设 Y∈{0,1}。

Conclusion: 本文在保序回归自由度的有限样本理论上取得了突破性进展，将精确最优界与校准风险的理论保证联系起来，为概率预测校准的后处理分析提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [256] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（Huber-of-Means），一种通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值的新鲁棒估计器，在重尾数据下保持稳定性，同时在高斯干净数据下接近经验均值的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾数据下，经验均值的置信控制变弱；几何中位数均值（MOM）缺乏向均值效率逼近的阈值机制。现有方法无法在鲁棒性和均值效率之间灵活插值。

Method: HOMER通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值。包含canonical和pseudo-Huber两种形式，每种形式都对每个块得分进行有界化处理，在类似中位数的鲁棒性与经验均值之间进行插值。建立了Hilbert空间多数定理和有限二阶矩下的MOM阶偏差界。Canonical HOMER在其二次区域内恢复样本均值，Pseudo-HOMER随阈值增长趋近样本均值。

Result: 在有限三阶矩下，固定有限维投影以通常参数速率支持均值推断，需块大小和块数同时增长且块大小增长更快。重尾模拟显示HOMER在少数块摘要被位移时保持稳定；高斯干净数据上两种版本均接近经验均值效率。有限块sandwich置信区间在偏斜函数型数据上欠覆盖；当大多数块受污染或块内均值被破坏时方法失效。

Conclusion: HOMER提供了介于中位数鲁棒性与均值效率之间的灵活插值方案，理论上支持参数速率推断，但实际应用中受到块内均值和污染比例的严格限制，sandwich区间在有限样本下存在欠覆盖问题。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [257] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯优化的波长区域选择方法，将近红外光谱糖含量估计中的波长选择建模为二值黑箱优化问题，通过稀疏二次代理模型和Thompson采样结合模拟/量子退火求解，相比遗传算法和模拟退火方法获得了更高的预测精度和更稳定的波长区域选取结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析中波长选择是重要的预处理步骤，能够提升预测精度和光谱数据的可解释性。然而波长区域选择本质上是组合优化问题，传统方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）容易陷入过拟合的孤立解，且产生不一致的波长区域，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 将波长区域选择建模为二值黑箱优化问题；构建稀疏二次代理模型作为目标函数的近似；通过Thompson采样依次提取感兴趣的波长区域；将最小化采集函数表述为二次无约束二值优化（QUBO）问题，并用模拟退火或量子退火求解。

Result: 实验表明：该方法相比遗传算法和模拟退火方法提高了偏最小二乘回归（PLSR）的预测精度，且选出的波长区域更一致；在一位局部扰动下，验证集上的均方根误差波动极小，说明收敛到更平滑的误差曲面，避免了孤立过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化（combinatorial Bayesian optimization）是光谱预测任务中稳健特征选择的有效框架，兼具精度提升与解的局部稳定性优势。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [258] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于深度神经网络的Engression方法进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三个部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression方法通过能量分数拟合生成模型来学习条件分布，但此前缺乏系统的理论误差分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，为Engression提供严格的理论基础，包括收敛速率的推导。

Method: 将Engression的经验风险（超额风险）分解为三个可分离的误差分量：近似误差（反映模型容量限制）、随机误差（反映有限样本带来的统计波动）和蒙特卡洛误差（反映能量分数中期望的数值近似）。基于这一分解，在目标条件生成器满足组合光滑性结构的假设下，推导各分量及总误差的收敛速率。

Result: 成功建立了Engression在深度神经网络实现下的收敛速率，表明该方法的超额风险可以系统性地归因于三个独立的误差来源，并在组合光滑性假设下获得了理论的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 该研究为Engression提供了完整的理论误差分析框架，揭示了不同误差来源对整体性能的影响，为后续改进和实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [259] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RPC（Randomized Projection with Clipping）方法，用于解决本地差分隐私（LDP）下多物品用户的鲁棒稀疏向量均值估计问题。该方法通过随机投影和裁剪来限制攻击者的能力，并基于精确偏差分析提出校正方法，无需偏差-方差权衡，从而进一步缩小输出空间并增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP协议易受投毒攻击，已有防御策略主要针对单物品用户。然而实际场景中用户可能拥有多个物品，由于输出空间更大，攻击者可以实施更强且更难以检测的攻击，使得多物品场景下的防御极具挑战性。

Method: 提出Randomized Projection with Clipping（RPC）方法：服务器向每个用户发送随机二值向量，用户将本地数据（每个用户具有m个非零坐标的稀疏向量）投影到该向量上并进行裁剪以限制攻击者能力。针对裁剪偏差，通过精细分析得到偏差的精确表达式，并提出相应的校正方法，从而消除偏差-方差权衡的需求，进一步降低裁剪阈值以缩小输出空间并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在可信环境下，RPC方法与现有方法相比达到相当或更好的性能，表明其本身就是一种高效的估计器；在不可信环境下，RPC方法对投毒攻击显著更具鲁棒性。同时提供了在所有可能的攻击下的估计误差的严格理论保证。

Conclusion: RPC通过精确偏差校正解决了多物品LDP场景下的鲁棒均值估计问题，在无需偏差-方差权衡的情况下同时实现了高效估计和强鲁棒性，为该领域提供了理论上严谨且实践上有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [260] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）框架下研究对抗训练的泛化性质，通过核积分算子推导了源均匀的泛化误差界，揭示了对抗训练在多项式谱模型下的统计精度损失（即对噪声的敏感性），并提出一种两阶段噪声去偏方法来恢复极小极大最优泛化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是抵御对抗攻击的主流方法，但其在非参数框架下的泛化行为尚未被充分理解。作者希望从RKHS和核积分算子角度解释对抗训练中鲁棒性与泛化之间的权衡，并指出精度损失的根源，进而提出解决方案。

Method: （1）利用核积分算子理论，推导RKHS对抗训练估计量的源均匀泛化误差上界，其依赖于鲁棒性水平、样本量、源光滑度和核谱；（2）在固定多项式谱模型上建立匹配的下界，证明最优均衡泛化率慢于极小极大预测基准；（3）提出两阶段噪声去偏过程，估计并去除混合鲁棒项中的噪声贡献，使泛化率提升至极小极大多项式率（至多差对数因子）。

Result: 理论分析表明对抗训练的精度损失源于对抗鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互作用：混合鲁棒项中的噪声贡献拖慢了逼近率，但同一项却降低了估计复杂度。两阶段噪声去偏估计器在鲁棒性水平按样本依赖阶选择时达到极小极大多项式率（至多对数因子误差），数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本文首次在非参数RKHS框架下全面刻画了对抗训练的泛化行为，揭示了对抗鲁棒性与泛化权衡的新解释（噪声干扰），并提供了去偏对抗训练这一原则性解决方案，在理论上达到了极小极大最优。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [261] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出熵最优流形回归（EOMR），将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展以联合识别非平稳非线性回归问题中的特征子集与子空间，在线性复杂度下实现鲁棒学习，并在混沌动力学和托卡马克等离子体湍流等基准问题上显著优于现有AI/ML方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习与人工智能工具（如梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络、基于Transformer的TabPFN）在非平稳、非线性回归任务中常面临预测误差大和模型复杂度高的问题，亟需一种能够同时实现特征子集与子空间联合识别的新型回归方法。

Method: 将熵最优流形聚类（EOMC）扩展为熵最优流形回归（EOMR），实现特征子集和相关子空间的联合同步识别，并保证在线性缩放的时间和内存复杂度下进行鲁棒学习。

Result: 在Lorenz-96系统（F=8和F=12的强混沌与极强混沌状态）以及Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克边缘等离子体模型数据上，EOMR相较最先进的AI/ML工具取得数量级更低的均方根预测误差和更小的模型复杂度。对Hasegawa-Wakatani例子，EOMR仅用8个参数就提炼出由线性因果弱平稳自回归过程描述的EOF主导动力学。

Conclusion: EOMR在混沌与湍流动力学预测问题上展现出显著的优越性，以极低的模型复杂度实现高精度预测，为复杂非线性系统建模提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [262] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于深度学习中不确定性量化（UQ）的结构化综述，重点覆盖基于集成和近似贝叶斯的方法及其输出汇总度量，将产生预测分布的方法与汇总不确定性的度量分开统一处理，并系统评述了五类方法族及相关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统架构缺乏规范化的不确定性量化手段，现有UQ综述对高效集成近似和单次前传方法的深度覆盖不足。

Method: 将方法组织为五个家族：贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似以及最后层/单次前传方法；同时纳入证据网络、先验网络、保形预测和后验校准等相关工作，以及分布外检测和选择性预测等决策任务；从理论动机、实现、实证表现和局限性四个方面逐一审视。此外评述了集成多样性理论和不确定性度量及其分解，对比熵分解与成对散度度量，并统一评估方法论。

Result: 提出了一个统一框架，将产生预测分布的方法与汇总其不确定性的度量分离；对五类UQ方法进行了深度评述，覆盖理论、实现、实证和局限；整合了集成多样性理论和不确定性度量分解，建立了统一的定性比较基础；并简要探讨了大规模语言模型中的不确定性及开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文提出了高效不确定性方法的具体研究方向，包括分类任务的高效认知度量、最后层多样性、分布偏移下的多样性与校准，以及混合架构等开放挑战。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [263] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-FRL（双稳健功能表示学习），一种交叉拟合工作流，将不规则纵向历史数据（如实验室值、生理信号、传感器流）转化为面向估计目标的隐状态，用于观察历史制度下的因果推断。该方法通过功能/时序编码器、扰动头（outcome/treatment/censoring）和EIF目标化验证与诊断，实现渐近线性均值估计，并在VitalDB真实数据审计中给出有价值的阴性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究常记录为不规则的功能片段（不等间隔、信息性测量时间），但标准双稳健估计量通常要求标量摘要，而序列学习器优化的预测损失未必能稳定有效影响函数（EIF），导致两者之间存在方法上的鸿沟。现有方法未能将不规则历史直接转化为支持EIF估计方程的状态表示。

Method: DR-FRL采用交叉拟合工作流：(1) 功能编码器和时序编码器分别将点云数据和先前历史映射为隐状态；(2) 扰动头（nuisance heads）估计结局、治疗和删失函数；(3) EIF目标化的验证、校准、重叠（overlap）、尾部（tail）和消融（ablation）诊断用于评估状态是否支持估计方程；(4) 若所选状态保留EIF所需的扰动信息，表示误差进入与普通扰动误差相同的二阶乘积余项，均值估计量在显式速率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下渐近线性；(5) 将Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计单独处理，而非Wald推断的替代。

Result: 模拟实验表明，当功能混杂维度高、测量具信息性、支持（support）弱或伪结局重尾时，DR-FRL相比基线方法有显著增益。VitalDB审计显示，DR-FRL能够利用不规则实验室点云，并得到一个有用的阴性发现：对于该ICU处置端点（ICU-disposition endpoint），标量实验室摘要已携带了大部分与端点相关的信息。

Conclusion: DR-FRL弥合了表示学习与双稳健因果推断之间的差距，提供了将不规则功能历史转化为EIF支持状态的理论保证工作流。其价值不仅在于性能提升（高维功能混杂、信息性测量等场景），还在于通过诊断工具揭示数据本身的特性（如VitalDB中标量摘要已足够），体现了方法论在实证评估中"证否"的严谨价值。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [264] [Discrete to continuum limits in Bayesian inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27408)
*Alexander Katsevich*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向贝叶斯反问题的后验层面离散到连续统理论，针对有限差分正则化的有限维先验和一般凸GLM型似然函数，证明离散高斯先验和后验测度在L²上的弱收敛性，并建立了耦合网格细化与小噪声极限下的MAP估计收敛、后验集中和Gaussian涨落定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯反问题研究通常采用"连续统优先"方法，即先假设连续统先验和后验存在，再将其离散化。这种方法忽略了数值计算中实际使用的有限维测度与理想连续统对象之间的一致性保证，缺乏后验层面的严格收敛理论，亟需构建从离散到连续统的严谨框架。

Method: 首先在固定总信息参数τ和正则化强度κ的条件下，证明离散高斯先验和后验测度在L²上的弱收敛到定义良好的连续统测度。随后考虑耦合的网格细化和噪声极限N→∞且τ_N,κ_N→∞（κ_N/τ_N固定），在明确的增长条件约束下证明：离散MAP估计收敛到连续统代价泛函的唯一极小元u_*；后验测度集中于u_*；中心化且重标度的涨落收敛到以零为均值、协方差为Q^{-1}的高斯分布（Q为u_*处连续统代价泛函的Hessian）。最后证明了先构造连续统后验再取小噪声高正则化极限可获得相同的确定性极限和高斯涨落律。

Result: 构建了后验层面的离散到连续统收敛理论框架；建立了固定参数下的弱收敛结果；证明了对角极限下MAP估计的确定性收敛、后验的Bernstein-von Mises型集中现象以及高斯涨落定理；验证了"先连续统后极限"与"先离散后极限"两种路径得到一致的极限结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为贝叶斯反问题数值计算中有限维离散测度提供了严格的连续统理论基础，确认了两种极限路径的等价性，从而为实际计算中的正则化参数选择和网格细化策略提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We develop a posterior-level discrete-to-continuum theory for Bayesian inverse problems whose finite-dimensional priors arise from local finite-difference regularization and whose likelihoods are of a general convex GLM-type form. In contrast to continuum-first approaches, the continuum prior and posterior are not assumed at the outset but are constructed as limits of the finite-dimensional probability measures used in numerical computation.
  First, with the total information parameter $τ$ and the regularization strength $κ$ fixed, we prove weak convergence on $L^2$ of the reconstructed discrete Gaussian priors and posterior measures to well-defined continuum laws. We then consider a coupled grid-refinement and small-noise limit in which $N\to\infty$ and $τ_N,κ_N\to\infty$, while $κ_N/τ_N$ remains fixed. Under explicit growth conditions relating $N$ and $τ_N$, we prove that the reconstructed discrete MAP estimates converge to the unique minimizer $u_*$ of the limiting continuum cost functional, that the reconstructed posterior measures concentrate at $u_*$, and that their centered and rescaled fluctuations converge to $\mathcal N(\vec 0,Q^{-1})$, where $Q$ is the Hessian of the continuum cost functional at $u_*$. Finally, we show that the same deterministic limit and Gaussian fluctuation law are obtained by first constructing the continuum posterior and then taking its small-noise, high-regularization limit.

</details>


### [265] [Sample Complexity for the 2-Gromov-Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27514)
*Pui Kuen Leung,Riku Okada,Samuel Lok-Hei Wong*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了经验plug-in估计器用于2-Gromov-Wasserstein距离D₂的样本复杂度。证明了当μ和ν分别支持在R^{d_x}和R^{d_y}的紧致子集上时，经验估计的均方误差以n^{-2/((d_x∧d_y)∨4)}的速率收敛（临界维度时含对数因子），该速率在临界维度下是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov-Wasserstein距离是衡量不同度量空间中概率分布之间结构相似性的重要工具，但其样本复杂度（即经验估计的收敛速率）尚未被充分刻画。理解经验plug-in估计器的收敛速率对于实际应用中的统计推断和算法设计至关重要。

Method: 1. 利用欧氏距离的几何表示：将欧氏距离表示为半空间特征映射的平方L²距离；2. 由此获得Gromov-Wasserstein泛函的变分对偶公式，转化为一族由无限维辅助参数索引的经典最优输运问题；3. 引入边际重定位（marginal recentering）技术恢复代价函数的凹性结构，从而获得尖锐的度量熵界；4. 结合经验过程估计得到收敛速率。

Result: 证明了\mathbb{E}|D₂²(\hatμ_n,\hatν_n)-D₂²(μ,ν)| \lesssim n^{-2/((d_x∧d_y)∨4)}(\log n)^{1_{d_x∧d_y=4}}，该速率在临界维度(d_x∧d_y=4)下除对数因子外是尖锐的，且由两个环境维度中较小者决定。

Conclusion: 通过几何表示和对偶变分公式，结合边际重定位技术与经验过程估计，成功地为2-Gromov-Wasserstein距离的经验估计建立了最优（除对数因子外）的样本复杂度收敛速率，其收敛速度由两个环境维度的较小者决定。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the sample complexity of the empirical plug-in estimator for the $2$-Gromov-Wasserstein distance $D_2$ between compactly supported probability measures on Euclidean spaces. Let $μ$ and $ν$ be supported on compact subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ and $\mathbb{R}^{d_y}$, respectively, and let $\widehatμ_n$ and $\widehatν_n$ be their empirical measures based on independent samples of size $n$. We prove that \[ \mathbb{E}\left|D_2^2(\widehatμ_n,\widehatν_n)-D_2^2(μ,ν)\right| \lesssim n^{-2/((d_x\wedge d_y)\vee 4)} (\log n)^{\mathbf 1_{\{d_x\wedge d_y=4\}}}. \] This rate is sharp up to the logarithmic factor in the critical dimension. The proof is based on a geometric representation of the Euclidean distance as a squared $L^2$-distance between half-space feature maps. This yields a variational dual formulation of the Gromov-Wasserstein functional in terms of a family of classical optimal transport problems indexed by an infinite-dimensional auxiliary parameter. Although the resulting cost functions need not be semiconcave in either argument, we introduce a marginal recentering of the costs that restores the concavity structure needed for sharp metric-entropy bounds. Combining this representation with empirical-process estimates gives a rate governed by the smaller of the two ambient dimensions.

</details>


### [266] [Selfnormalization for relevant inference with supremum-type statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27818)
*Patrick Bastian*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自归一化方法，用于功能性时间序列中由上确界范数度量的相关变化推断。为解决上确界范数不可Hadamard可微的问题，作者采用log-sum-exp平滑近似替代上确界范数，并从其导数构造投影自归一化器，使统计量具有渐近枢轴分布。通过多平滑水平组合消除前导偏差项，避免直接估计极值集的几何量，在温和正则条件下得到渐近精确的相关变化检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性时间序列中检测相关变化通常使用上确界范数度量，但上确界范数不可Hadamard可微，导致标准基于投影的自归一化方法无法适用，且极限分布依赖于极值集的几何结构和长期协方差结构，给推断带来实质困难。

Method: 用log-sum-exp平滑函数近似上确界范数，并利用其导数构造投影自归一化器；推导孤立非退化极值点和正测度极值集两种情形下的平滑偏差展开；通过组合多个平滑水平消除前导偏差项，避免直接估计极值数量、曲率或测度等几何量。

Result: 所构造的统计量具有渐近枢轴分布，不依赖长期协方差冗余参数，仅依赖断点位置；验证了平滑偏差展开，多平滑水平组合可有效消除前导偏差，在温和正则条件下检验具有渐近精确性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的平滑与偏差校正原理为将自归一化与上确界型统计量相结合提供了通用框架，适用于涉及相关假设的推断问题，扩展了功能性时间序列相关变化检验的方法论。

Abstract: We develop a selfnormalized approach to inference for relevant changes in functional time series measured by the supremum norm. The main difficulty is that the supremum norm is not Hadamard differentiable, so standard projection-based selfnormalization does not apply and the limiting distribution may depend on the geometry of the extremal set and the long-run covariance structure. We address this problem by replacing the supremum norm with a smooth log-sum-exp approximation and constructing a projected selfnormalizer from its derivative. The resulting statistic has an asymptotically pivotal distribution that is free of long-run covariance nuisance parameters and depends only on the break location. We derive explicit smoothing-bias expansions for both isolated nondegenerate extrema and extremal sets of positive measure. To avoid direct estimation of geometric quantities such as the number, curvature, or measure of the extrema, we combine several smoothing levels to cancel the leading bias terms. This yields an asymptotically exact test for relevant changes under mild regularity conditions. More generally, the proposed smoothing and bias-correction principles provide a framework for combining selfnormalization with supremum-type statistics in problems involving relevant hypotheses.

</details>


### [267] [OT-FairBoost: Optimal Transport-Guided Gradient Boosting for Fairness Regularization on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28014)
*Veronika Shilova,Abdoulaye Sakho,Younes Boumoussou,Laurent Risser,Jean-Michel Loubes,Emmanuel Malherbe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出OT-FairBoost，一种将Wasserstein-2距离惩罚直接嵌入梯度提升树目标函数的in-processing公平性缓解框架，在二分类、回归和多组敏感属性场景下均取得了最佳的准确率-公平性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络的机器学习模型近年来备受关注，基于树的模型（如梯度提升）在表格数据上仍具竞争力并被广泛应用。然而这些模型可能因算法偏见产生跨人口群体的歧视性预测。现有的公平性缓解策略中，针对梯度提升模型的pre-processing和post-processing方法较多，但in-processing方法很少，存在研究空白。

Method: 将Wasserstein-2距离惩罚直接纳入梯度提升树的目标函数中，将群体预测间的Wasserstein-2距离的样本级梯度估计扩展到离散分布和海森对角线上，并将该方法集成到LightGBM训练流程中。

Result: 在二分类、回归和多组敏感属性设置下的实验结果表明，OT-FairBoost在准确率-公平性权衡方面优于现有替代方法。

Conclusion: OT-FairBoost填补了梯度提升模型in-processing公平性方法不足的空白，证明基于最优传输（OT）的公平性缓解策略可有效应用于树模型，并为AI公平性法规遵从提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Although neural-based machine learning models have received a lot of attention recently, tree-based models such as gradient boosting are competitive for tabular data and therefore remain widely used in various applications of AI. As when using other machine learning predictive models, they can however yield discriminative predictions across demographic groups, due to so-called algorithmic biases. These undesirable phenomena have motivated the emergence of new regulatory frameworks and various AI fairness strategies. While several pre-and post-processing methodologies exist to mitigate such bias on gradient boosting models, only a few in-processing methods have been proposed. To bridge this gap, we introduce OT-FairBoost, a novel in-processing framework that incorporates a Wasserstein-2 distance penalty directly into the objective function of gradient-boosted trees. This OT-based mitigation strategy has been shown to efficiently optimize group fairness criteria such as Demographic Parity and Equalized Odds on neural-based predictions. To adapt this approach for gradient boosting, we extend the sample-wise gradient estimation of the Wasserstein-2 distance between group predictions to discrete distributions and hessian diagonals. We then integrate our approach into the LightGBM training procedure and evaluate it across binary classification, regression, and multi-group sensitive attribute settings. Experimental results in each of these settings demonstrate that OT-FairBoost achieves best accuracy-fairness trade-offs against alternatives.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BridgeMIL，一种两阶段框架，将EEG疾病诊断中的实例表示学习与主体级监督解耦。第一阶段通过时间窗口对齐和子袋对比预训练编码器，第二阶段用注意力MIL聚合器进行主体级监督分类，在15个数据集-骨干网络组合中14个达到最高准确率，平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG疾病诊断需要在主体（subject）级别进行预测，但现有pipeline将记录分割成短实例并继承主体标签进行实例级分类，错误假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据。虽然MIL可以避免继承标签，但EEG数据集中主体数量远少于实例数量，限制了端到端MIL学习到的表示质量。因此需要一种方法既能利用丰富实例学习表示，又能严格保持主体级监督的一致性与诊断目标对齐。

Method: BridgeMIL采用两阶段框架：(1) 阶段一：在不使用继承实例标签的情况下预训练编码器，通过对齐时间上邻近的窗口和独立采样的主体内子袋来学习表示，并采用方差和协方差正则化防止表示坍缩和降低冗余（无需负样本对）；(2) 阶段二：将预训练编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器中，仅对主体预测施加监督，并通过特征保留机制限制表示漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五种代表性骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个数据集-骨干网络设置中的14个取得最高平均准确率，总体平均准确率为76.57%，比最强基线高出4.28个百分点。进一步分析显示：不同实例的继承标签可靠性存在显著差异；模型性能对主体稀缺性比对实例稀缺性更敏感；BridgeMIL学到更结构化的表示空间，具有明显的主体级聚类和改进的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: BridgeMIL的研究结果表明，将监督信号与主体级预测目标对齐至关重要，同时应利用丰富的EEG实例进行表示学习而不将疾病标签分配给单个实例。该框架成功解决了EEG疾病诊断中实例数量远多于主体数量所导致的学习质量问题，验证了监督对齐和表示学习的解耦对提升诊断性能的有效性。
